| [0201](ADR-0201-dsa-is-hand-built-with-rfc-6979-nonces-and-openssl-s-parameter-limits.md) | DSA is the hand-built `DsaSignature` on every platform: RFC 6979 deterministic nonces over SHA-1 to SHA-512 (SHA-224 hand-built), OpenSSL's verification limits (q of 160, 224 or 256 bits, odd p up to 10,000 bits, 1 < g < p), r \|\| s at q's length, constant-time signing by Fermat inversion | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0202](ADR-0202-trace-ids-mark-each-transfers-lines-through-a-per-transfer-view.md) | `--trace-ids` (and `-vv`) write `[<xfer>-<conn>] ` after the stamp, `[<xfer>-x] ` before a connection; each transfer reports through a `TraceIdsTransferEvents` view over the shared writer, and takes its `%{conn_id}` at its first event so the marker and `-w` agree | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0203](ADR-0203-a-sasl-security-context-that-cannot-make-its-first-token-fails-with-exit-94.md) | A GSSAPI or NTLM SASL context that cannot make its first token throws `SaslAuthenticationFailedException` for exit 94, and the SMTP, IMAP and POP3 handlers ask for the initial response before the command only under `--sasl-ir` (or IMAP's `SASL-IR`), otherwise at the first continuation, as curl's `Curl_sasl_start` does | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0204](ADR-0204-the-hand-built-kerberos-reaches-https-kdcs-through-an-optional-ms-kkdcp-proxy-seam.md) | `KerberosKdcClient` reaches `https://` KDCs through an optional `IKerberosKdcProxyTransport`, wrapping each request in a `KDC-PROXY-MESSAGE` as MIT's `sendto_kdc.c` does (TCP-framed `kerb-message`, realm as `target-domain`, no `dclocator-hint`); a malformed proxy reply tries the next KDC | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0205](ADR-0205-the-hand-built-tls-client-offers-tls-1-3-and-tls-1-2-in-one-clienthello.md) | `TlsClientConnection` sends one ClientHello offering TLS 1.3 and the TLS 1.2 half of `TlsClientSettings`, reads the first server message through a replaying stream, and continues as TLS 1.3 or hands the sent hello to the TLS 1.2 client, which refuses either downgrade sentinel; `HandBuiltTlsProvider` runs it for a range spanning both, with TLS 1.2 as the default minimum | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0206](ADR-0206-ssh-key-exchange-fails-with-libssh2-s-minus-8-and-asks-for-2048-to-4096-bit-groups.md) | Every SSH key-exchange failure after the algorithms are agreed is exit 2 with `-8, Unable to exchange encryption keys` (measured); group exchange asks for (2048, 4096, 4096) and refuses primes outside it (the OpenSSL preset's sizes amended by ADR-0268); NIST points are checked by hand; a server's later `KEXINIT` is answered and keeps the session identifier; ephemeral keys come from an injected `ISshEphemeralKeySource`; a packet length of zero ends with -12 and one over 40000 bytes MAC included with -41 (measured, BL-1081) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0207](ADR-0207-kerberos-default-cache-and-keytab-names-expand-mit-s-unix-path-tokens.md) | `CredentialCacheStore` and `KeytabStore` fall from `KRB5CCNAME`/`KRB5_KTNAME` to `krb5.conf`'s `default_ccache_name`/`default_keytab_name`, expanded by `KerberosPathExpansion` (MIT's Unix `%{token}` set; an unclosed or unknown token is `PathTokenInvalid`), then to MIT's built-in names | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0208](ADR-0208-the-hand-built-kerberos-stores-tgs-tickets-back-in-a-file-credential-cache.md) | The hand-built Kerberos appends a ticket got by a TGS exchange to its `FILE:` or `DIR:` credential cache through an injected `IKerberosFileWriter`, as MIT's `cc_file.c` does, so the next request reuses it; `KCM:` is `UnsupportedType`, no writer is `NotWritable`, and a failed store is ignored as MIT ignores it | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0209](ADR-0209-the-hand-built-kerberos-offers-the-encryption-types-krb5-conf-names-resolved-as-mit-does.md) | `KerberosKdcClient` offers `default_tkt_enctypes` in AS-REQs and `default_tgs_enctypes` in TGS-REQs (each defaulting to `permitted_enctypes`, then `DEFAULT`), resolved as MIT 1.22's `krb5int_parse_enctype_list` (measured) and kept to the types the library has; an empty list is `EncryptionTypeNotSupported` before sending | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0210](ADR-0210-the-hand-built-kerberos-forwards-a-ticket-granting-ticket-as-mit-s-krb5-fwd-tgt-creds-does.md) | `KerberosKdcClient.GetForwardedTicketGrantingTicketAsync` gets a forwarded TGT for `--delegation` by a TGS-REQ for `krbtgt/REALM@REALM` with `forwarded`, `forwardable` and the ticket's `proxiable`/`renewable`, addressless, as MIT's `krb5_fwd_tgt_creds`; a ticket without `forwardable` is `TicketNotForwardable` before sending | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0211](ADR-0211-the-tls-1-2-client-checks-dhe-dss-with-the-hand-built-dsa-and-offers-it-only-when-asked.md) | `Tls12ClientHandshake` runs the 13 `DHE_DSS` suites (`Tls12Authentication.Dss`), checking the ServerKeyExchange's `Dss-Sig-Value` under `dsa_*` or TLS 1.0/1.1's SHA-1 with `Curl.Cryptography`'s `DsaSignature` (`HashData` adds SHA-224); an unreadable DSA key is `bad_certificate`, a bad signature `decrypt_error`; the defaults offer neither DSS nor `dsa_*` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0212](ADR-0212-ssh-packets-are-sealed-behind-one-protection-seam-and-a-failed-mac-ends-with-libssh2-s-minus-4.md) | SSH packets after `NEWKEYS` go through one `ISshPacketProtection` per direction (plain, cipher-and-MAC in either order, AES-GCM); a failed MAC ends the session with libssh2's `-4` (measured) and a failed AES-GCM tag with `-12` (from libssh2's source, BL-897 measures it) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0213](ADR-0213-ssh-host-keys-are-checked-as-curl-s-libssh2-build-checks-them-and-a-refusal-is-exit-60.md) | SSH host keys are checked as curl 8.21.0's libssh2 build checks them: `--hostpubsha256`, then `--hostpubmd5`, else the known-hosts file (hashed, `[host]:port`, markers read as libssh2 reads them) unless `-k`; every refusal is exit 60 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0214](ADR-0214-alt-svc-gives-each-http-transfer-its-own-cache-and-uses-only-h1-alternatives.md) | `--alt-svc` gives each `http`/`https` transfer its own cache, read before and written after it; only `h1` alternatives are used, never for plain `http`, a `--connect-to` origin or an entry naming the origin, and a redirect to another origin drops the route | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0215](ADR-0215-ssh-users-authenticate-with-none-password-then-keyboard-interactive-and-a-refusal-is-exit-67.md) | SSH users authenticate as curl 8.21.0's libssh2 build does: the `ssh-userauth` service request ends session startup (exit 2 with libssh2's -43, -13 or -14), then `none`, `password` and `keyboard-interactive` from the first method list; exit 67 `Login denied` when keyboard-interactive failed, `Authentication failure` when it was not tried, exit 79 when the list cannot be read | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0216](ADR-0216-ftp-produces-exit-11-only-for-a-refused-acct-and-never-exit-15.md) | FTP ends with exit 11 only when `ACCT` is refused (BL-635), never for a `PASS` reply, and never with exit 15: curl 8.21.0 resolves the data host through the ordinary connect, so its failures are the connector's exit 6, 7 or 28 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0217](ADR-0217-brainpool-ecdh-and-ecdsa-use-complete-addition-formulas-uncompressed-points-and-p1363-signatures.md) | Brainpool ECDH and ECDSA (P256r1, P384r1, P512r1) run on `MontgomeryModulus` with Renes-Costello-Batina complete addition and a fixed-window ladder; uncompressed points only, r \|\| s signatures with RFC 6979 nonces, keys by reduction of 8 extra random bytes; pinned by RFC 7027 and Wycheproof's P1363 files | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0218](ADR-0218-hsts-is-one-cache-per-run-read-before-and-written-after-each-hsts-transfer.md) | HSTS is one cache per run, shared by every transfer with or without `--hsts`; a `--hsts` transfer reads the file into the cache before the switch and writes the whole cache back after the `-c` jar and `--alt-svc` file | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0219](ADR-0219-tls-1-2-ecdhe-agrees-x448-and-brainpool-only-when-offered-and-verifies-brainpool-ecdsa-by-hand.md) | TLS 1.2 ECDHE agrees x448 and brainpoolP256r1/384r1/512r1 when `SupportedGroups` offers them (the default list is unchanged); a brainpool certificate key verifies with `Curl.Cryptography`'s `BrainpoolEcdsa` on every platform; a bad point or all-zero x448 secret, and a TLS 1.3-only `0x081a`-`0x081c` scheme, are `illegal_parameter` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0220](ADR-0220-sftp-downloads-follow-libssh2s-requests-and-map-each-status-to-curls-exit-code.md) | SFTP downloads follow libssh2 1.11.1's requests as measured: a `session` channel with a 2 MiB window, `INIT` 3, `REALPATH .`, `OPEN`, `STAT`, reads kept 4 × min(remaining, 102400) bytes ahead in reads of up to 30000, `CLOSE`; each `OPEN` status maps to curl's exit code and text, a failed read is exit 79, a short file exit 18 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0221](ADR-0221-a-tls-read-that-ends-without-close-notify-fails-with-curls-exit-56-text-on-both-tls-paths.md) | A secure connection throws `MissingCloseNotifyException` (an `IOException` carrying the Schannel or OpenSSL build's measured text) when a read finds the TLS connection ended without `close_notify`, and returns 0 after it; the hand-built path asks `CloseNotifyReceived`, the `SslStream` path whether the transport under it ended; the HTTP handler reports it as exit 56 with that text | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0222](ADR-0222-curl-s-own-diagnostic-log-is-log-level-and-log-file-off-by-default-with-zero-extra-bytes.md) | Curl's own diagnostic log, apart from curl's `-v`/`--trace`: `--log-level none\|error\|warning\|info\|verbose` (default `none`, zero extra bytes) and `--log-file <path>`, lines `[<UTC>] [<level>] [<component>] <message>`, a bad level refused with exit 2, no credentials ever logged, a hand-rolled `IDiagnosticLog` passed by constructor; an additive departure from real curl, which rejects both options | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0223](ADR-0223-http-3-through-a-proxy-is-refused-as-curl-se-s-ngtcp2-build-refuses-it.md) | `--http3` and `--http3-only` with an `https://` URL through a proxy report `HTTP/3 is not supported over a SOCKS proxy` (or `… an HTTP proxy`); `--http3-only` fails with exit 3 before connecting, `--http3` runs over TCP and reports a failure with the refusal's text and its own exit code, as measured on curl.se's 8.18.0 build; 8.21.0's CONNECT-UDP for HTTP proxies is BL-942 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0224](ADR-0224-ai-help-prints-markdown-for-ai-agents-a-short-index-by-default.md) | `--ai-help [category\|all]` prints Markdown for AI agents generated from the tables `--help` and `--manual` use: a short index by default, one category's option sections by name, every category plus exit codes and `--write-out` variables for `all`; `\n` line ends on every platform; an unknown category prints `--help`'s unknown-category page; an additive departure from real curl, which rejects the option with exit 2 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0225](ADR-0225-scp-downloads-run-scp-pf-with-exec-and-parse-its-header-as-libssh2-does.md) | SCP downloads run `scp -pf '<path>'` with `exec` (libssh2's shell quoting, `/~/` dropped), acknowledge with three zero bytes, and read the `T` and `C` lines with libssh2 1.11.1's byte checks and messages (exit 78); the header's size is copied exactly, -1 reads to the end, a short file is exit 18, a broken connection exit 79 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0226](ADR-0226-alt-svc-uses-h2-and-h3-alternatives-as-curl-se-s-build-looks-them-up.md) | `--alt-svc` uses `h2` and `h3` alternatives on every platform as curl.se's build looks them up by the version option: an `h3` entry for another host or port connects over HTTP/3 alone, one naming the origin makes the transfer race HTTP/3 against TCP, `h1`/`h2` ones let ALPN choose, and each redirect hop looks up again | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0227](ADR-0227-a-negotiate-context-that-needs-another-leg-is-kept-by-the-value-it-sent.md) | A Negotiate context that needs another leg is kept, keyed by the `Authorization` value its token made, and stepped with the token of a 401's `Negotiate` challenge on the same connection; anything else takes the 401. `ws://` sends Negotiate pre-emptively through `CreateAuthorizationAsync` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0228](ADR-0228-a-websocket-upgrade-answers-no-401-as-curl-8-21-0-sends-it-once.md) | A `ws://` or `wss://` upgrade is sent once: a `401`, whatever its challenges, fails with exit 22 `Refused WebSocket upgrade: 401`, as curl 8.21.0 measured does; no continuation is asked for | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0229](ADR-0229-tls-srp-runs-rfc-5054-in-the-hand-built-tls-1-2-client-as-openssl-does.md) | TLS-SRP runs RFC 5054 in the hand-built TLS 1.2 client as OpenSSL does: the nine SRP suites offered only with credentials, `srp` after `server_name`, UTF-8 without SASLprep, Appendix A groups only (`insufficient_security` otherwise), B of 0 or not below N `illegal_parameter`, a 48-byte a, S without leading zeros | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0230](ADR-0230-ssh-publickey-asks-then-signs-and-a-key-that-fails-falls-through-to-password.md) | SSH `publickey` runs before `password` when listed: the question without a signature, then on `PK_OK` the signed request; an RSA key signs with the first of `rsa-sha2-512`, `rsa-sha2-256`, `ssh-rsa` that `server-sig-algs` names (`ssh-rsa` without `EXT_INFO`); `--key` or `$HOME/.ssh/id_rsa`, `id_dsa`, then `id_rsa`, `id_dsa` in the working directory; every ADR-0122 key format but Ed25519 and bcrypt on every platform; any key failure falls through to `password`, ending in ADR-0215's exit 67 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0231](ADR-0231-negotiate-failure-lines-are-worded-by-the-authenticator-and-placed-by-the-http-handler.md) | A Negotiate context's `-v` failure line is worded by `NegotiateHttpAuthenticator` in the platform curl's words (SSPI on Windows, GSS-API elsewhere) and reported to `HttpAuthRequest.Events`; the HTTP handler writes the first request's lines after `using HTTP/1.x` with `Server auth using Negotiate with user '...'`, and a 401's just before its Negotiate challenge header | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0232](ADR-0232-an-empty-authorization-value-sends-the-request-again-without-one.md) | An empty answer from `IHttpAuthenticator.CreateAuthorizationAsync` sends the request again without `Authorization`: `--anyauth` picking Negotiate after a 401 with no ticket sends curl's second request and ends on its 401 (exit 0); a retry's `-v` lines are written before it when the drawing request had not picked Negotiate | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0233](ADR-0233-the-hand-built-tls-1-3-client-offers-ech-to-rfc-9849-with-shared-key-shares-and-boringssl-style-grease.md) | The hand-built TLS 1.3 client offers ECH to RFC 9849: the first supported config of a decoded `ECHConfigList`, inner and outer hellos sharing key shares, section 6.1.3 padding, confirmation checked in the HelloRetryRequest and ServerHello, a rejection authenticated under the public name and ended with `ech_required` and its retry configs, BoringSSL-style GREASE, and resumption inside an offer with the ticket in the inner hello and a GREASE `pre_shared_key` in the outer one (BL-960) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0234](ADR-0234-negotiate-carries-the-https-server-certificate-to-the-hand-built-kerberos-for-tls-server-end-point-bindings.md) | `HttpAuthRequest` and `SecurityContextRequest` carry the TLS server certificate's DER; the hand-built Kerberos turns it into RFC 5929 `tls-server-end-point` channel bindings (SHA-256 for MD5 and SHA-1, else the signature's hash); none over plain HTTP, and provisionally none for a signature with no known hash (BL-965) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0235](ADR-0235-the-hand-built-tcp-client-sends-the-platform-profile-hello-cut-to-what-it-can-honour.md) | `HandBuiltTlsProvider` sends the platform curl's measured ClientHello profile (`ClientHelloProfile.Schannel` or `.OpenSsl`) through `ClientHelloProfileMapping`: its extension order, fixed extensions, suites, groups and a 32-byte legacy session ID, options changing only the lists; signature schemes the client cannot check are left out, `--cert-status` puts `status_request` after `supported_groups`, and below a TLS 1.3 ceiling the profile's lists apply but `Tls12ClientHelloBuilder`'s order stays | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0236](ADR-0236-the-runner-opens-the-diagnostic-log-once-the-command-line-is-accepted-and-writes-it-holding-the-write-gate.md) | `CurlCommandRunner` opens the run's one diagnostic log after an accepted parse and the `--stderr` redirects, from what `CurlComposition` gives it, puts it on every transfer's context, writes each line holding the run's `WriteGate` before the log's own lock, warns about an unopenable `--log-file` even under `-s`, and logs the command line and each transfer's start, failure and end under `cli` and `runner` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0237](ADR-0237-the-hand-built-kerberos-has-des3-cbc-sha1-as-mit-1-22-does.md) | `Des3CbcSha1KerberosEncryption` is encryption type 16 as MIT 1.22 has it: 24-byte keys, `hmac-sha1-des3-kd`, random-to-key fixing parity only, decryption giving the padded plaintext (`KerberosAsn1.WithoutPadding` drops it before decoding), a ciphertext not whole blocks failing as `CiphertextNotWholeBlocks`; `KerberosKdcClient` offers 16 wherever `krb5.conf` names it | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0238](ADR-0238-a-kept-digest-answer-is-counted-on-from-the-value-as-sent.md) | `IHttpAuthenticator.RepeatAuthorization(request, sentAuthorization)` (default: the value as sent) lets `DigestAuthenticator` read a kept Digest answer back and build it again with `nc` one higher for the retry that keeps it (the `Authorization` value answering a 407, the `Proxy-Authorization` value answering a 401); every other value is sent as it was | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0239](ADR-0239-a-forward-proxy-s-407-is-answered-once-like-a-401.md) | A forward proxy's `407` is answered once, like a `401`: `HttpProtocolHandler` takes the `--proxy-*` scheme pick (`proxyAuthSchemes`, Basic by default) and retries once when the request sent no `Proxy-Authorization`, keeping the other header as it was, so a `407` and a `401` are each answered in either order (BL-603; numbered 0187 until BL-886) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0240](ADR-0240-a-redirect-hop-sends-its-own-url-s-user-information-unless-command-line-credentials-win.md) | Under `-L` each hop sends the command-line credentials (`-u`, netrc) when same-origin or `--location-trusted`, else its own URL's percent-decoded user information (BL-814; numbered 0193 until BL-886) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0241](ADR-0241-sftp-directory-listings-print-each-long-name-and-follow-symbolic-links-as-libssh2-does.md) | An `sftp://` path ending with a slash is listed as libssh2 1.11.1 lets curl list it: `OPENDIR`, `READDIR` until `EOF` or no names, each long name and a line feed (the file name alone with `-l`), a symbolic link followed with `READLINK` and printed as ` -> <target>`, then `CLOSE`; a failed `OPENDIR` is `Could not open directory for reading`, a failed `READDIR` `... :: -31`, a failed `READLINK` exit 27 `Out of memory`; `-I` stops before `OPENDIR` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0242](ADR-0242-the-console-finds-the-ssh-known-hosts-file-as-curls-tool-does.md) | The console finds `scp` and `sftp`'s known-hosts file as curl's `findfile(".ssh/known_hosts")` does (`SshKnownHostsFileSearch`: `CURL_HOME`, `HOME`, then `USERPROFILE` and `APPDATA` on Windows or the home directory elsewhere); `-k` means none and `--knownhosts` wins over the search; none found is `Could not find a known_hosts file` and exit 2, ending the run, or a warning with `--hostpubmd5` or `--hostpubsha256` (measured, BL-576) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0243](ADR-0243-aws-sigv4-signs-in-an-authenticator-that-puts-its-headers-in-the-authorization-slot.md) | `--aws-sigv4` signs in `Curl.Console`'s `AwsSigV4HttpAuthenticator`, fed `AwsSigV4Inputs` by the HTTP handler, re-signing every redirect hop; its lines ride the `Authorization` value after CRLFs, and a refusal fails the exchange once connected with nothing sent | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0244](ADR-0244-sftp-uploads-open-write-and-create-directories-as-libssh2-does.md) | A `-T` upload over `sftp://` opens `WRITE,CREATE,TRUNCATE` (`WRITE,APPEND,CREATE` under `-a`, `WRITE` alone to resume) with `--create-file-mode`, resumes `-C -` from `STAT`'s size, makes each directory with `MKDIR` 0755 under `--ftp-create-dirs` when the open fails with 2, 4 or 10, and sends 64 KiB blocks as `WRITE`s of 30000 bytes; a failed open is `Upload failed: <text> (<status>/-31)`, a failed reopen `Creating the dir/file failed`, a failed write exit 79 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0245](ADR-0245-an-http-3-request-stream-that-cannot-be-opened-is-exit-55.md) | An HTTP/3 request stream that cannot be opened is exit 55 `cannot open bidi streams`; `MultiplexedConnectionFailedException` keeps its own exit code and message | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0246](ADR-0246-styled-output-bolds-header-names-and-links-location-on-a-terminal-as-curl-does.md) | `--styled-output` bolds header names and wraps a `Location` value in an OSC 8 hyperlink as curl does (`StyledHeaderLines`, `StyledHeaderStream`), only for a transfer writing to standard output on a terminal that renders styles, for curl's four schemes; on Windows `StandardOutputVirtualTerminal` turns on virtual-terminal processing and restores the mode | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0247](ADR-0247-sftp-quote-commands-run-around-the-transfer-as-libssh2-does.md) | `-Q` on `sftp://` runs `chgrp`, `chmod`, `chown`, `atime`, `mtime`, `ln`, `symlink`, `mkdir`, `rename`, `rmdir`, `rm`, `statvfs` and `pwd` as curl's `sftp_quote` does: plain commands after `REALPATH .` and before the transfer, `-` ones after a successful transfer's `CLOSE`, `+` ones never; `*` passes over a server's failure; exit 21 with curl's messages; `pwd` and `statvfs` write to `-D`; numbers and dates follow the platform's C `long` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0248](ADR-0248-a-negotiate-token-in-a-2xx-is-not-checked-and-the-kept-context-is-ended.md) | A `WWW-Authenticate: Negotiate` token in a 2xx is not stepped, as curl 8.21.0 reads it only on a 401: the 2xx is the result, exit 0, whatever the token; `IHttpAuthenticator.EndAuthorization` lets the HTTP handler end the handshake on a non-401 (non-407 for a proxy), disposing of the kept context unstepped | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0249](ADR-0249-tls-1-2-and-below-run-over-a-byte-stream-in-their-own-connection-and-stream.md) | TLS 1.2, 1.1 and 1.0 run over a byte stream in their own `Tls12ClientConnection` and `Tls12ClientStream`, shaped as their TLS 1.3 counterparts; the ClientHello record carries 0x0301 and later records the negotiated version, or `protocol_version`; HelloRequest is ignored; closing follows ADR-0157; the BEAST empty fragment is a setting | Accepted | 2026-09-28 |
| [0250](ADR-0250-http-3-through-an-http-proxy-runs-quic-in-a-connect-udp-tunnel.md) | `--http3` and `--http3-only` through an HTTP or HTTPS proxy run QUIC in a CONNECT-UDP tunnel (RFC 9298) in `DATAGRAM` capsules, as curl 8.22.0 does (measured, BL-942): `CONNECT-UDP tunnel failed, response <n>` is exit 7, and `--http3` races it against a TCP `CONNECT`; supersedes ADR-0223's interim refusal | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0253](ADR-0253-sftp-downloads-read-the-part-r-and-c-ask-for-as-libssh2-does.md) | `-r` and `-C` on an `sftp://` download read only the part asked for, from its first byte with libssh2's read-ahead, when `STAT` gives a size; `-C N` is the range `N-`; a part beyond the end is exit 33 with `Offset (N) was beyond file size (S)` or `Bad range: start offset larger than end offset`, and `-C` with no size exit 36; the handle is still closed | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0254](ADR-0254-tcpconnector-races-address-families-after-the-happy-eyeballs-timeout.md) | `TcpConnector` races address families through `AddressFamilyRace`: the first address's family is dialled alone until `--happy-eyeballs-timeout-ms` (curl's 200 ms when not given) passes or it has failed, then the other beside it; the first connection wins and the rest are cancelled without a line; proxies are raced the same way (measured, BL-644) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0255](ADR-0255-http-3-framing-sends-nghttp3s-default-settings-and-refuses-pushes-as-a-client-that-never-sends-max-push-id.md) | HTTP/3 framing sends nghttp3's default `SETTINGS` (max field section 2^62 - 1, QPACK table capacity 0, blocked streams 0) and no grease; no server push, so a push stream or `CANCEL_PUSH` is `H3_ID_ERROR`; frames are read whole up to a caller's limit; a closed control or QPACK stream is `H3_CLOSED_CRITICAL_STREAM` | Accepted | 2026-09-28 |
| [0256](ADR-0256-the-hand-built-kerberos-follows-cross-realm-referrals-in-the-tgs-exchange.md) | `KerberosKdcClient` asks the TGS with `canonicalize` and follows `krbtgt/OTHER@REALM` referrals to OTHER's KDCs, up to MIT's 10 hops; the eleventh is `ReferralLimitExceeded` | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0258](ADR-0258-scp-uploads-send-the-c-line-and-bytes-as-libssh2-does.md) | `-T` on `scp://` runs `scp -t`, sends `C0<mode> <size> <name>` (no `T` line; `--create-file-mode 0` sends 0644) after the first zero byte, then the bytes with no trailing zero; a source of unknown size, a refused channel and every refusal before the bytes are exit 25 with libssh2's message; a break during the bytes exit 79 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0259](ADR-0259-chacha20-poly1305-packets-pad-to-eight-without-the-length-and-a-failed-tag-is-minus-12.md) | `chacha20-poly1305@openssh.com` is OpenSSH's construction over the hand-built `ChaCha20` and `Poly1305`: a 64-byte key split into `K_2` and `K_1`, the sequence number as nonce, the tag checked before the payload is decrypted; packets pad to 8 without the length field; a failed tag is libssh2's -12, as for AES-GCM | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0262](ADR-0262-scp-and-sftp-verbose-lines-follow-libssh2-c-and-the-measured-order.md) | `-v` on `scp://` and `sftp://` writes libssh2's session lines as measured and as `lib/vssh/libssh2.c` words them: backend (`WinCNG` or `OpenSSL`), user, known-hosts reading, narrowing and check, fingerprints, the method list and each method's outcome (the agent always fails), `authentication complete`; received data by write; a failure before the transfer closes the connection, a transfer's outcome leaves it intact; the narrowing moves after the connect | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0263](ADR-0263-ssh-keys-read-ed25519-and-bcrypt-encrypted-openssh-key-v1-with-libssh2s-ciphers.md) | `--key` reads Ed25519 keys (`openssh-key-v1` and PKCS #8) and signs `ssh-ed25519` with the hand-built `Ed25519`, and opens bcrypt-encrypted `openssh-key-v1` keys with the ciphers OpenSSH writes and libssh2 1.11.1 reads (AES CTR, CBC and GCM, `3des-cbc`; not `chacha20-poly1305@openssh.com`), on every platform; a wrong passphrase is an unreadable key, exit 67 `Authentication failure` as measured | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0264](ADR-0264-ssh-compression-streams-last-the-session-and-inflate-to-libssh2s-payload-limit.md) | `--compressed-ssh` compresses with the BCL's `ZLibStream` (sync flush out; OpenSSH's partial flushes inflate packet by packet), one stream per direction started once from the first key exchange's choice - `zlib` at `NEWKEYS`, `zlib@openssh.com` after `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS` - and kept across re-exchanges as OpenSSH keeps it; one packet inflates to at most libssh2's 40000 bytes | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0265](ADR-0265-hybrid-post-quantum-ssh-key-exchanges-hash-k-as-a-string.md) | `mlkem768x25519-sha256`, `mlkem768nistp256-sha256`, `mlkem1024nistp384-sha384` and `sntrup761x25519-sha512` (both names) run in one `HybridKemSshKeyExchange`: KEM share then classical share each way, K = HASH(KEM secret || raw classical secret) hashed into H and the keys as a `string`, not an `mpint`; a server share of the wrong length fails with -8 | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0266](ADR-0266-ssh-certificate-host-keys-verify-with-the-certified-key-and-libssh2-agrees-only-the-ed25519-one.md) | Every certificate and `sk-` host-key name has a verifier; a certificate verifies H with its certified key and nothing else in it is checked, as libssh2 1.11.1 does; the reference presets offer the RSA (and on OpenSSL the ECDSA) certificate names but never agree them, failing with -5 as measured | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0267](ADR-0267-an-independent-audit-office-audits-the-dark-factory-from-outside-its-reach.md) | An in-repository audit office of six read-only specialist auditors (quality, security, performance, conformance, truthfulness, process) audits the dark factory from outside its reach: the audit paths `Audit/` and `.claude/agents/audit-*` are guarded by the board, a `CURL_DARK_FACTORY_LANE` hook, CI and the `audit` branch merged only by Stewart; planted defects measure each auditor, and a finding closes only on a confirming re-audit | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0268](ADR-0268-ssh-group-exchange-asks-for-the-platform-builds-sizes-up-to-8192-bits-on-openssl.md) | SSH group exchange sends, hashes and enforces the preset's sizes: (2048, 4096, 4096) on `WindowsReference`, (2048, 4096, 8192) on `OpenSslReference` and `Full`, measured from each build; amends ADR-0206's single size | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0269](ADR-0269-interface-and-local-port-bind-each-dial-and-the-last-address-decides-the-exit.md) | `--interface` and `--local-port` bind each TCP dial through `ITcpDialer.DialFromAsync` and `LocalBindingTcpDialer`: interface (never found on Windows), else host, first address of the dialled family; a failed bind moves to the next address and the last one decides exit 45, 43 or 7; a setopt-refused value is exit 43 before connecting and ends the run | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0270](ADR-0270-proxy-ntlm-and-negotiate-go-on-over-the-same-proxy-connection-as-the-origins-do.md) | `--proxy-ntlm` and `--proxy-negotiate` are answered on the origin's terms for a CONNECT tunnel and a forward proxy: Type 1 or the first token up front, Type 3 or the next token for the proxy's `407` on the same connection, for `HTTP` on the proxy's host; the tunnel goes on through `ContinueAuthorizationAsync` and its contexts come from ADR-0142's router, bound late | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0271](ADR-0271-ssh-users-authenticate-with-an-ssh-agents-identities-after-password-as-libssh2-does.md) | SSH users are authenticated with an ssh-agent's identities after `password` and before `keyboard-interactive` when the list names `publickey`, each tried as libssh2 1.11.1 tries it (question, agent sign request with the RSA SHA-2 flag, one retry from the key's own type on a method mismatch, the leftover `ssh-rsa` method after a failed upgrade), with the measured `-v` lines; the agent is the pipe `SSH_AUTH_SOCK` names or `\\.\pipe\openssh-ssh-agent` on Windows and `SSH_AUTH_SOCK`'s Unix socket elsewhere; Pageant is BL-1035 | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0272](ADR-0272-digest-ntlm-and-negotiate-authorization-go-beside-an-h-authorization-header.md) | A Digest, NTLM or Negotiate `Authorization` value is sent straight after `Host` even when an `-H` value names `Authorization`, which then follows in the custom headers' place; Basic, Bearer and `--aws-sigv4` values are dropped for it, as libcurl's `output_auth_headers` checks the custom headers for those alone | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0273](ADR-0273-preproxy-is-the-connectors-socks-hop-to-an-http-proxy.md) | `--preproxy` is `TcpConnector`'s SOCKS hop to an HTTP or HTTPS proxy (a constructor argument per option group, so the pool key is unchanged); a lone pre-proxy is the transfer's SOCKS proxy; an HTTP pre-proxy and a SOCKS `-x` beside one are exit 5, as curl 8.21.0 measured | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0274](ADR-0274-a-failed-sftp-open-realpath-or-quote-command-closes-the-channel-before-disconnect.md) | A failed SFTP `REALPATH`, `OPEN`, `OPENDIR`, upload `OPEN` or `-Q` command closes the session channel with `EOF` and `CLOSE` before the handler's `DISCONNECT`, as measured from curl 8.21.0; a broken connection is left as it is | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0275](ADR-0275-ntlm-handshake-lines-go-before-the-challenge-header-and-a-type-3-failure-fails-the-retry.md) | NTLM's `-v` handshake lines (`NTLM handshake rejected`, `... failure (internal error)`, `... (bad type-2 message)`, `NTLM authentication problem, ignoring.`) go just before the challenge header, as curl 8.21.0 and 8.18.0 measured; a Type 3 the context cannot make fails the retry, after the connection is taken again, with SSPI's `type-3 message` line | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0276](ADR-0276-socks5-authentication-follows-socks5-basic-and-socks5-gssapi-and-runs-rfc-1961-on-the-security-context-seam.md) | The SOCKS5 greeting offers the methods `--socks5-basic` and `--socks5-gssapi` allow (both when neither is given) and a method not allowed is refused with curl's message; GSS-API runs RFC 1961 on the security-context router with Kerberos, service `rcmd` or the one given, offering no protection wrapped (bare under `--socks5-gssapi-nec`), in the platform build's failure texts | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0277](ADR-0277-an-sftp-url-path-of-tilde-is-the-home-directory-and-a-zero-byte-is-exit-3.md) | An `sftp://` URL path of `/~` lists the home directory and a slash, `/~/rest` gains no second slash when the home directory ends with one, and a `%00` in the path is exit 3 after `REALPATH`, with the channel closed before `DISCONNECT`, as curl 8.21.0's `Curl_getworkingpath` does | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0278](ADR-0278-a-websocket-upgrade-writes-curls-server-auth-and-basic-or-bearer-problem-lines.md) | A WebSocket upgrade writes `Server auth using <scheme> with user '<user>'` before the request when one scheme is picked, and `Basic` or `Bearer authentication problem, ignoring.` before each 401 challenge offering the picked scheme, as curl 8.21.0 does | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0279](ADR-0279-a-refused-http-3-stream-rewinds-a-seekable-upload-and-fails-an-unseekable-one-with-exit-65.md) | A `-T` upload whose HTTP/3 stream is refused is rewound to where the transfer began reading it and sent again on a new connection; one that cannot seek (stdin) fails the retry before it connects with exit 65 `seek callback returned error 2`, as curl-8_21_0's `cr_in_rewind` does | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0280](ADR-0280-ftp-ccc-clears-tls-through-iconnection-and-fails-with-exit-81-as-the-schannel-build-does.md) | FTP's `CCC` (`--ftp-ssl-ccc`) follows `PROT` on a TLS control connection; a 5xx keeps TLS, any other reply calls the new default `IConnection.ClearTlsAsync`, which `SslStreamConnection` answers as the matched build does - OpenSSL clears TLS (sending `close_notify` first only in active mode) and hands back the plaintext connection, Schannel sends `close_notify` and fails, exit 81 with no `QUIT` | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0281](ADR-0281-an-unreadable-ssh-key-names-the-openssl-builds-reason-off-windows.md) | With no `--pubkey`, a private key libssh2 cannot derive a public key from is refused with the OpenSSL backend's reason off Windows (`Unable to open private key file` when it does not open, `Wrong passphrase or invalid/unrecognized private key file format` when it does) and WinCNG's `Reason unknown (-1)` on Windows | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0282](ADR-0282-ssl-verify-result-prints-the-openssl-verify-code-off-windows.md) | `%{ssl_verify_result}` and `%{proxy_ssl_verify_result}` print the OpenSSL verify code off Windows, reported through `ITransferEvents.ReportCertificateVerifyResult` whether or not the handshake went on; a trust error beats a date error, a refused host name is `1`, and Windows keeps `0` (amends ADR-0043) | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0283](ADR-0283-an-ssh-connection-reset-during-the-handshake-fails-as-libssh2-reports-it.md) | An SSH connection reset during the handshake fails with exit 2 as libssh2 reports it: `-43, Failed getting banner` before the server's identification, then the measured close's `-1` or `-8, Unable to exchange encryption keys` | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0284](ADR-0284-curves-and-sigalgs-read-openssl-3-5-lists-and-fail-as-the-applying-build.md) | `--curves` and `--sigalgs` read OpenSSL 3.5's list syntax on every platform and replace only the profile's `supported_groups`, `key_share` and `signature_algorithms`; groups and schemes the client cannot run are dropped; refusals are exit 59 and an empty list exit 35 with OpenSSL's text; a failed handshake prints the applying build's text on Windows (curl.se's for a `--curves` handshake_failure, OpenSSL's under `--sigalgs`) | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0285](ADR-0285-next-option-groups-share-one-connection-cache-and-reuse-on-matching-settings.md) | Measured: the `--next` option groups share one run-wide `ConnectionCache`; each group's `PoolingConnector` keys its connections on the group's `OptionGroupConnectionSettings` (TLS and proxy TLS options, `--connect-to`, Unix socket, local binding, `-4`/`-6`, PROXY line, pre-proxy, ALPN), so a later group reuses an earlier one's connection only when they are equal; the runner closes the cache when the run ends | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0286](ADR-0286-an-ftp-passive-data-connect-is-not-held-to-connect-timeout.md) | Measured: FTP's passive data connections go through `PoolingConnector.Over(TcpConnector.WithoutConnectTimeout())`, so only `-m` or the system ends one, as curl 8.21.0 does; a dial the system timed out is exit 28 with the connect message on every connect | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0287](ADR-0287-compressed-advertises-and-decodes-zstd.md) | Measured: `--compressed` sends `Accept-Encoding: deflate, gzip, br, zstd` on the Schannel and OpenSSL builds and decodes `Content-Encoding: zstd` with `Curl.Zstandard`'s `ZstandardDecoder`, frame after frame; corrupt data and bytes after the last frame are exit 61 | Accepted | 2026-09-30 |
| [0288](ADR-0288-dark-factory-runs-stop-at-a-six-dollar-cost-cap.md) | `RunDarkFactory.ps1 -TaskBudgetUsd` (default $6, 0 for none) caps every headless run with `--max-budget-usd`; a task run that stops at the cap is stashed and blocked for splitting like a timed-out one (AF-0004) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0289](ADR-0289-an-ssh-connection-reset-after-the-key-exchange-fails-as-libssh2-reports-each-step.md) | An SSH connection reset after the key exchange fails as libssh2 reports each step, measured: exit 2 `-43` at the service request and the step's message during SFTP start-up, exit 79 for SFTP requests and mid-download, exit 0 with nothing written at the SFTP `OPEN`, and `Unexpected error` or `Failed waiting for channel success` at an SCP download's start; a local output `IOException` stays apart (`SshConnectionLostException`) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0290](ADR-0290-quic-handshakes-read-ciphers-and-cert-as-openssl-api-builds-and-send-the-openssl-profile-s-tls-1-3-hello.md) | Both QUIC builds (curl.se's LibreSSL build on Windows, OpenSSL elsewhere) read `--ciphers`/`--tls13-ciphers` through `OpenSslCipherSuites` and `--cert` through `ClientCertificateLoader.LoadAsOpenSslBuild`; offered suites are cut to those `QuicPacketProtection.CanProtect` accepts, none left is exit 59; the OpenSSL QUIC hello is `ClientHelloProfile.OpenSsl`'s TLS 1.3 parts with `quic_transport_parameters` last, until BL-957 measures one | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0291](ADR-0291-the-openssl-build-s-quic-hello-is-measured-transport-parameters-first-and-the-profile-s-tls-1-2-extensions-kept.md) | The OpenSSL build's QUIC hello, measured from Fedora's curl 8.18.0 (OpenSSL 3.5.7, ngtcp2 1.22.1): `ClientHelloProfile.OpenSsl`'s hello with `quic_transport_parameters` first in place of `renegotiation_info`, its TLS 1.2 extensions kept, TLS 1.3 suites, and signature schemes without SHA-1, SHA-224 and DSA | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0292](ADR-0292-quic-s-udp-socket-binds-as-interface-and-local-port-ask-and-a-failed-bind-ends-as-the-tcp-path-s.md) | QUIC's UDP socket binds as `--interface` and `--local-port` ask, through `LocalBindingAddressChooser` (shared with `LocalBindingTcpDialer`) and `IUdpChannelOpener.OpenFrom`, before the trust anchors are reported; a failed bind moves on to the next address and ends with the TCP path's exit 45, 43 or 7 in QUIC's `Failed to connect to <host> port <port>` line (measured on curl.se's 8.18.0 ngtcp2 build) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0293](ADR-0293-an-interface-name-binds-its-device-first-on-linux-and-a-plain-or-if-name-bound-so-binds-nothing-else.md) | On Linux a plain `--interface` name, `if!` and `ifhost!`'s interface part bind their device with `SO_BINDTODEVICE` first (`ITcpDialer.DialFromDeviceAsync`); a plain or `if!` name bound so binds no address and no `--local-port` port, `ifhost!` binds its host after it, and a failure carries on as ADR-0269 (measured on curl 8.18.0, unprivileged and root alike) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0294](ADR-0294-scp-and-sftp-uploads-report-libssh2s-writes-and-a-failure-during-the-bytes-closes-the-connection.md) | `scp` and `sftp` uploads report each libssh2 write as sent data (`WRITE`s of 30000 bytes, channel writes of 32700) and write `upload completely sent off: N bytes` (or `Request completely sent off`) when the copy ends; a short file or a connection lost during the bytes closes the connection, as curl's transfer phase does; an `scp` channel's end is reported as 0 bytes received; measured, with a right password and `keyboard-interactive` | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0295](ADR-0295-the-interface-and-local-port-bind-writes-curl-s-v-lines-through-the-dial-s-events.md) | `--interface` and `--local-port` write curl's `-v` bind lines (`Name ... resolved to`, `Local port:`, `Bind to local port ... trying next`, `bind failed with errno`, `Could not resolve host`/`Could not bind to`, `socket successfully bound to interface`) through the target's events, handed to `LocalBindingTcpDialer`, its chooser and a new `ITcpDialer.DialFromAsync` overload; platform errno and `AF_INET6` numbers as measured on Windows 8.21.0 and Linux 8.18.0; QUIC and the `Local Interface` line left for follow-ups | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0296](ADR-0296-the-hand-built-tls-client-shares-keys-on-pure-ml-kem-the-nist-curve-ml-kem-hybrids-and-the-brainpool-tls13-groups.md) | The hand-built TLS client shares keys on `MLKEM512/768/1024` (`MlKemKeyShare`), `SecP256r1MLKEM768` and `SecP384r1MLKEM1024` (`EcdhMlKemKeyShare`, curve first) and the brainpool `tls13` groups (`BrainpoolKeyShare`), so `--curves` drops none of OpenSSL's names; amends ADR-0284 decision 3; pinned to OpenSSL 3.5.5 known answers and measured ClientHellos | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0297](ADR-0297-a-quic-connect-on-windows-writes-curl-ses-libressl-tls-lines-naming-the-windows-stores-it-verified-against.md) | A QUIC connect marks its `TlsTrustEvent` and `TlsHandshakeEvent` `IsQuic`; under Schannel wording they get curl.se's LibreSSL lines (`[blank] / UNDEF`, `Public key type ?`, no `OpenSSL verify result`), and without `--cacert` the trust line is `Native: Windows System Stores ROOT+CA` rather than the build's embedded-bundle blob line; measured on curl.se's 8.18.0 | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0298](ADR-0298-a-quic-socket-bound-to-any-address-reports-the-route-s-source-address-as-its-local-end.md) | `UdpDatagramChannel.LocalEndPoint` on a socket bound to the any address reports the source address a throwaway UDP socket connected to the server gets (nothing sent), so a QUIC connect's `Established connection ... from` line and `%{local_ip}` name the interface address as curl's connected socket does; the channel's socket stays unconnected for TFTP | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0299](ADR-0299-an-in-place-tls-upgrade-reports-its-handshake-and-the-connect-s-established-connection-line-again.md) | `ITlsProvider` gains a default `AuthenticateAsClientAsync(plaintext, host, events, token)` the production providers already implement, and `ConnectionOpenedCapturingTransferEvents` keeps the connect's `ConnectionOpenedEvent`; after an accepted SMTP `STARTTLS` the handshake reports to the transfer's events and the connect's `Established connection` line is written again, as curl 8.21.0 does; the `schannel:` lines (BL-1083) and IMAP, POP3 and FTP (BL-1084) filed | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0300](ADR-0300-a-kdc-proxy-is-verified-against-the-realm-s-http-anchors-with-its-own-tls-never-the-transfer-s.md) | An `https://` KDC proxy is verified with the transport's own TLS (`KerberosKdcProxyTlsClient`, plain TCP from the connector), against the realm's `krb5.conf` `http_anchors` (`FILE:`, `DIR:`, `ENV:`, loaded as MIT's `load_anchor` does; realm only) or the system's trust store when there are none, so `-k`, `--cacert` and `--capath` never apply; `IKerberosKdcProxyTransport` gains an anchored overload whose default refuses anchors | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0301](ADR-0301-ed25519-ed448-and-ml-dsa-client-certificate-keys-are-read-by-hand-and-carried-beside-the-certificate.md) | For an Ed25519, Ed448 or ML-DSA `--cert` the OpenSSL build reads the PKCS #8 `--key` (PEM `PRIVATE KEY` or DER; ML-DSA in RFC 9881's `seed`, `expandedKey` or `both` form) by hand with `HandBuiltPrivateKeyReader` on every platform, checks it against the certificate's public key, and carries it as a `HandBuiltKeyCertificate` the hand-built TLS client signs with; a bad key is the existing exit 43; encrypted keys filed as BL-1085 | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0302](ADR-0302-proxy-alt-svc-and-hsts-cache-lines-take-the-run-s-log-per-call-or-per-cache.md) | `ProxySelector.TrySelect` takes the run's diagnostic log per call (through `TransferProxySelection`), `AltSvcCache` and `HstsCache` at construction; `proxy` logs the proxy chosen at `info` by scheme, host and port only and why none at `verbose`, `altsvc` an alternative used at `info` and stored or missed at `verbose`, `hsts` each entry stored or expired at `verbose` (BL-1072) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0303](ADR-0303-curves-or-sigalgs-leaving-nothing-to-offer-sends-openssl-s-internal-error-alert-in-both-builds.md) | When `--curves` or `--sigalgs` leaves no group, signature scheme or key share (exit 35), the hand-built client writes OpenSSL's measured `internal_error` alert `15 03 01 00 02 02 50` before closing, in both builds; a refused list (exit 59) writes nothing (BL-1087) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0304](ADR-0304-ssh-agent-on-windows-asks-pageant-before-the-openssh-pipe-as-libssh2-does.md) | On Windows the ssh-agent step asks PuTTY's Pageant first (`FindWindow` `Pageant`, the request through a `PageantRequest%08x` file mapping and `WM_COPYDATA` `0x804e50ba`, as libssh2 1.11.1's `agent_win.c`) and the OpenSSH pipe only when no Pageant window is found; a zero result is the measured `failure requesting identities to agent`; an answer over 8188 bytes fails rather than overrunning the mapping (BL-1035) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0305](ADR-0305-schannel-build-reports-its-client-certificate-trust-line-before-a-cert-load-failure.md) | When `--cert` does not load, both TLS providers report the Schannel build's trust without the IP-address flag before exit 58, so `-v` writes `schannel: disabled automatic use of client certificate` and no SNI line, as curl 8.21.0 does; the OpenSSL build reports nothing (BL-1088) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0306](ADR-0306-libcurl-source-is-built-from-the-transfers-performed-and-quotes-utf-8-bytes.md) | `--libcurl` source is generated by `LibcurlSourceCode` from the transfers the runner performed (glob-expanded URL, option group); `MAXREDIRS` for `http`/`https`, `FTP_SKIP_PASV_IP` for `ftp`/`ftps`; strings quoted as UTF-8 bytes with curl's escapes, cut at 2000; `-` writes LF after the output, a file text mode (CR LF on Windows) (BL-652) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0307](ADR-0307-file-transfers-report-received-data-their-failure-line-and-their-connection-end.md) | `file://` downloads report each chunk read as received data; every failure but exit 55 reports its message as an info line; a transfer past its open ends with `closing connection #N` (exit 23, 26) or `shutting down connection #N` from the handler's own counter, after the meter ends (BL-936) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0308](ADR-0308-libcurl-source-writes-http-output-and-connection-options-as-curl-orders-them.md) | `--libcurl` writes the HTTP, output and connection options in curl 8.21.0's order, skips default numbers, writes HTTP-only cookie/redirect/compression lines for `http`/`https` only, numbers `slist`/`mime` variables across transfers, and writes a non-printable byte before a hex digit in octal (BL-653) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0309](ADR-0309-schannel-build-writes-renegotiation-lines-for-each-tls-1-3-session-ticket-record.md) | In the Schannel build `SslStreamTlsProvider` follows the TLS records under `SslStream` after a TLS 1.3 handshake and reports each record before the first application data that carried none as a received `NewSessionTicket`, which `-v` writes as curl 8.21.0's three `schannel:` renegotiation lines (BL-1089) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0310](ADR-0310-under-a-tls-1-2-ceiling-curves-never-fails-and-sigalgs-fails-with-no-ciphers-available.md) | Under a TLS 1.2 ceiling a `--curves` list leaving no group sends the ClientHello without `ec_point_formats` and `supported_groups`, and a `--sigalgs` list leaving no scheme TLS 1.2 can check fails with exit 35 and OpenSSL's `no ciphers available` (BL-1094) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0311](ADR-0311-an-rsa-certificate-identity-signs-with-the-rsa-sha2-certificate-method-on-openssl-as-libssh2-does.md) | An `ssh-rsa-cert-v01@openssh.com` identity is upgraded from `server-sig-algs` to `rsa-sha2-512-cert-v01@openssh.com` or `rsa-sha2-256-cert-v01@openssh.com` as libssh2 1.11.1 does on OpenSSL, not on WinCNG and not for an OpenSSH 7.7 or older banner (BL-1036) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0312](ADR-0312-doh-https-records-for-ech-follow-curl-s-doh-c.md) | DoH HTTPS records for ECH follow curl's `lib/doh.c`: QTYPE 65, `_<port>._https.<host>` off port 443, the first of at most four records decoded by `ServiceBindingRecordDecoder` (BL-707) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0313](ADR-0313-a-key-file-certificate-signs-with-its-private-key-under-the-plain-method-as-libssh2-does.md) | A `--pubkey` OpenSSH certificate signs with its `--key` private key under the plain method, the server judging a key it does not certify, and a private key of another type fails with `Callback returned error`, as libssh2 1.11.1 does (BL-1097) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0314](ADR-0314-the-wincng-preset-reads-no-ed25519-or-ecdsa-private-key.md) | The WinCNG preset reads no Ed25519 or ECDSA private key: with `--pubkey` it asks and then fails with `Callback returned error`, without it `Reason unknown (-1)`, as Windows curl's libssh2 1.11.1 does (BL-1098) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0315](ADR-0315-smb-v-lines-follow-the-openssl-build-and-end-by-curls-transfer-phase.md) | SMB's `-v` lines follow the OpenSSL build: a `failf` text, then `closing connection #N` for a failure before the session is set up and `shutting down connection #N` otherwise, and `{ [N bytes data]` for each read (BL-598) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0316](ADR-0316-ip-tos-and-vlan-priority-set-every-socket-option-the-os-has.md) | `--ip-tos` and `--vlan-priority` parse as curl 8.21.0 does and set `IP_TOS`/`IPV6_TCLASS` and `SO_PRIORITY` raw, in each operating system's numbers, wherever it has them (BL-646) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0317](ADR-0317-tcp-fastopen-and-mptcp-open-the-sockets-the-os-has.md) | `--tcp-fastopen` sets TCP Fast Open raw on Windows, Linux and macOS, and `--mptcp` opens `IPPROTO_MPTCP` sockets everywhere, failing with curl's `failed to open socket` lines where the OS refuses them (BL-647) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0318](ADR-0318-trace-config-sets-ids-time-and-components-each-component-task-writes-its-lines.md) | `--trace-config` parses curl's comma list: `ids`, `time` and `all` set the prefixes (not cleared by a first `-v`), other names are kept as components, and each component's lines are written by its own task (BL-649) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0319](ADR-0319-ssl-sessions-loads-and-saves-curls-session-file-through-the-hand-built-client.md) | `--ssl-sessions` routes to the hand-built TLS client, which offers and keeps TLS 1.3 sessions in a run cache loaded from and saved to curl 8.21.0's file format, under curl's peer key naming `IMPL-Curl`; curl's own lines are kept unused and written back; `--tls-earlydata` is left to BL-1105 (BL-710) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0320](ADR-0320-xattr-stores-curls-four-attributes-through-libc-where-a-curl-build-does.md) | `--xattr` stores `user.creator`, `user.xdg.referrer.url`, `user.mime_type` and `user.xdg.origin.url` (credentials stripped) after a successful `-o`/`-O` transfer, through libc `setxattr` on Linux and macOS and `extattr_set_file` on FreeBSD, and nothing on Windows, as curl's builds do (BL-651) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0321](ADR-0321-the-schannel-build-checks-revocation-for-a-cacert-chain.md) | The Schannel build checks revocation for a `--cacert` chain and fails an unknown status with exit 60 `schannel: the revocation status is unknown`; `TlsClientOptions.SkipRevocationCheck` is `--ssl-no-revoke`; the root is not checked and the OpenSSL build never checks (renumbered from 0086 by BL-663) | Accepted | 2026-09-27 |
| [0322](ADR-0322-the-windows-z-file-lookup-calls-createfile-and-getfiletime-as-curl-does.md) | The Windows `-z` file lookup calls kernel32 `CreateFileW` and `GetFileTime` as curl does (`WindowsFileTimeReader`), so `-z con` and `-z nul` print curl's `GetLastError` lines; replaces ADR-0037's Windows lookup (renumbered from 0088 by BL-663) | Accepted | 2026-09-27 |
| [0323](ADR-0323-ftp-downloads-hold-curls-measured-conversation-in-passive-mode-only.md) | FTP downloads hold curl's measured conversation (`USER`, `PASS`, `PWD`, `CWD`, `EPSV`/`PASV`, `TYPE`, `SIZE`, `RETR` or `LIST`, `QUIT`), in passive mode only, with curl's exit code and message for every failure (renumbered from 0093 by BL-663) | Accepted | 2026-09-27 |
| [0324](ADR-0324-an-http-1-0-keep-alive-body-read-to-the-close-is-reported-left-intact.md) | The HTTP handler reports `left intact` for an HTTP/1.0 `Connection: keep-alive` response whose body ran to the close, as curl 8.21.0 does, but does not mark the closed connection reusable (renumbered from 0109 by BL-663) | Accepted; marking not reusable superseded by ADR-0112 | 2026-09-27 |
| [0325](ADR-0325-alt-svc-seams-a-route-on-the-connect-target-and-a-store-on-the-http-options.md) | Alt-svc seams: `AltSvcRoute` on the connect target and `IAltSvcStore` on the HTTP options; the handler sends `Alt-Used`, hands each `https` `Alt-Svc` header to the store and writes the `Added alt-svc` lines (renumbered from 0208 by BL-663) | Accepted | 2026-09-29 |
| [0326](ADR-0326-libcurl-writes-the-schannel-builds-proxy-tls-and-authentication-lines.md) | `--libcurl` writes curl 8.21.0 Schannels proxy, TLS and authentication setopt lines in its order and bitmask format on every platform, and a test keeps every parsed option classified (BL-654) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0327](ADR-0327-ech-modes-through-the-hand-built-client.md) | `--ech` through the hand-built client: libcurl's mode bits, the `ecl:` list or the DoH HTTPS record's `ech`, `pn:` as the outer name, `hard` without a usable list exit 35 and a rejected offer exit 101 | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0328](ADR-0328-tlsuser-runs-tls-srp-through-the-hand-built-client-as-curls-openssl-build.md) | `--tlsuser` runs TLS-SRP through the hand-built client on every platform: OpenSSL's `SRP` cipher list without `--ciphers`, exit 43 without `--tlspassword`, the OpenSSL build's alert lines | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0329](ADR-0329-ftp-reply-lines-go-to-the-d-file.md) | FTP control reply lines go to the `-D` file in arrival order, `QUIT`'s excepted; a refused write is exit 23 | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0330](ADR-0330-no-sessionid-and-ssl-allow-beast-run-on-the-hand-built-client-which-resumes-by-default.md) | `--no-sessionid` and `--ssl-allow-beast` run on the hand-built client, which offers the run's sessions by default; `--ssl-allow-beast` routes only a range reaching TLS 1.0 | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0331](ADR-0331-a-tls-1-0-or-1-1-ceiling-connects-through-the-hand-built-client-on-every-platform.md) | A TLS 1.0 or 1.1 ceiling connects through the hand-built client on every platform, as curl's Schannel build does, though the LibreSSL and OpenSSL builds refuse (measured) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0332](ADR-0332-remove-on-error-tells-a-non-regular-output-file-through-the-runtimes-stat-shim.md) | `--remove-on-error` tells a non-regular output file apart through the runtime's `stat` shim and prints curl's `Skipping removal` warning off Windows (measured) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0333](ADR-0333-a-chunked-407-to-connect-is-discarded-and-the-connection-reused.md) | A chunked `407` to CONNECT is read through and the answer sent on the same connection; a malformed one is exit 56 with curl's chunk parser message (measured) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0334](ADR-0334-a-stale-digest-407-to-connect-is-answered-again-five-reconnects-at-most.md) | A stale Digest `407` to CONNECT is answered again with the new nonce, and the proxy is dialled again five times at most, then exit 7 `Could not connect to server` (measured) | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0335](ADR-0335-cert-status-prints-its-status-line-on-every-platform.md) | `--cert-status` prints `* SSL certificate status: good (0)` (or `revoked (1)`, `unknown (2)`) under `-v` on every platform, reported by the hand-built provider as an info line | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0336](ADR-0336-pinnedpubkey-hash-line-rides-the-handshake-event-mismatch-lines-are-info-lines.md) | `--pinnedpubkey`'s `-v` ` public key hash:` line rides `TlsHandshakeEvent`, placed per build; a refusal's hash and mismatch lines (twice for Schannel, once for OpenSSL) are info lines | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0337](ADR-0337-tls-earlydata-defers-the-handshake-to-the-first-write.md) | `--tls-earlydata` routes to the hand-built client, which defers the handshake on a resumed session allowing early data to the first write and sends that write as 0-RTT early data | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0338](ADR-0338-http3-sessions-are-pooled-and-shared-by-z-transfers.md) | HTTP/3 sessions are pooled under a key of their own and `-Z` transfers share one up to the server's MAX_STREAMS | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0339](ADR-0339-authenticators-log-through-a-late-bound-run-log.md) | The authenticators log through a late-bound run log, by scheme name and status only | Accepted | 2026-10-01 |
| [0340](ADR-0340-the-tls-1-2-only-hello-follows-the-measured-platform-order.md) | The TLS 1.2-only hand-built hello follows the platform curl's measured extension order | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0341](ADR-0341-negotiate-channel-bindings-come-from-the-origin-tls-only.md) | Negotiate's channel bindings come from the origin's TLS only, never an HTTPS proxy's | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0342](ADR-0342-connect-phase-lines-follow-the-platform-build.md) | A proxy tunnel's CONNECT-phase lines follow the platform build, with curl 8.21.0's text | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0343](ADR-0343-an-ignored-http2-body-resets-its-stream.md) | An ignored HTTP/2 body resets its stream with STREAM_CLOSED instead of being read | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0344](ADR-0344-a-failed-negotiate-context-names-curls-error.md) | A failed Negotiate context's first-request line is the error message of an HTTP transfer that then fails | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0345](ADR-0345-http2-and-http3-frame-lines-go-to-the-transfers-log.md) | HTTP/2 and HTTP/3 frame lines go to the log of the transfer they belong to; the connection's SETTINGS and GOAWAY to the transfer that last opened a stream | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0346](ADR-0346-a-tftp-connection-is-numbered-with-the-runs-connections.md) | A TFTP transfer's connection is numbered with the run's other connections | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0347](ADR-0347-a-file-transfer-is-numbered-with-the-runs-connections.md) | A file:// transfer is numbered with the run's connections, through `IConnectionNumbers` | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0348](ADR-0348-a-refused-tunnel-fails-with-the-first-sspi-negotiate-failure.md) | A refused CONNECT tunnel fails with the first SSPI Negotiate failure as its message | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0349](ADR-0349-schannel-warns-that-tls13-cipher-lists-are-ignored.md) | The Schannel build warns that `--tls13-ciphers` and `--proxy-tls13-ciphers` are ignored, once per URL | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0350](ADR-0350-the-openssl-builds-tls13-hello-pads-and-drops-ec-point-formats-as-measured.md) | The OpenSSL build's TLS 1.3 hello pads and drops `ec_point_formats` as measured | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0351](ADR-0351-next-option-groups-share-one-dns-cache.md) | The `--next` option groups share one run-wide `DnsCache`: a later group answers a host an earlier group resolved, or an earlier group's `--resolve` entry, from it, and its own entry replaces the cached answer with `old addresses discarded` | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0352](ADR-0352-quic-s-udp-socket-bind-writes-the-tcp-path-s-v-bind-lines.md) | QUIC's UDP socket bind writes the TCP path's `-v` bind lines, as curl.se's ngtcp2 build does (amends ADR-0295 Decision 4) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0353](ADR-0353-an-interface-found-after-a-refused-device-bind-writes-curl-s-local-interface-line.md) | An interface found after a refused device bind writes curl's `Local Interface ... is ip ...` line, and a plain name then the `Name` line of its address (completes ADR-0295 Decision 4) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0354](ADR-0354-encrypted-pkcs8-keys-for-hand-built-client-certificates-are-decrypted-by-hand.md) | An `ENCRYPTED PRIVATE KEY` for an Ed25519, Ed448 or ML-DSA client certificate is decrypted by hand: PBES2 with PBKDF2 and AES or DES-EDE3 CBC (amends ADR-0301) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0355](ADR-0355-tcp-fastopen-on-macos-connects-through-connectx.md) | `--tcp-fastopen` on macOS connects through `connectx` with `CONNECT_DATA_IDEMPOTENT`, as libcurl does, falling back to a plain connect when it refuses (BL-1101; amends ADR-0317) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0356](ADR-0356-trace-config-dns-writes-the-dns-filter-and-doh-lines-around-the-connect.md) | `--trace-config dns`, `doh` and `all` write the DNS filter's `[DNS]` lines around a direct connect and route the DoH resolver's lines to the resolving transfer through an `AsyncLocal` view (BL-1102) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0357](ADR-0357-vv-and-trace-config-setup-write-the-setup-filter-lines.md) | `-vv` to `-vvvv` turn on curl's trace components in the parser, and `-vv` or `--trace-config setup` write the setup filter's `[SETUP]` lines around a direct connect (BL-1103) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0358](ADR-0358-a-deferred-connectx-socket-is-read-through-its-own-stream.md) | A socket `connectx` left connecting is read and written through `DeferredConnectSocketStream`, as `NetworkStream` refuses it (BL-1158; amends ADR-0355) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0359](ADR-0359-ech-v-lines-and-exit-101-text-as-measured-with-openssl-4.md) | `--ech` writes curl's `ECH:` setup lines and exit 101 OpenSSL's `ech required` text, as measured with curl 8.21.0 on OpenSSL 4.0.0; `pn:` or `ecl:` without a mode is `hard` (BL-1107; amends ADR-0327) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0360](ADR-0360-a-tls-1-0-or-1-1-minimum-runs-on-the-hand-built-client.md) | A `--tlsv1.0` or `--tlsv1.1` minimum runs on the hand-built client whatever the ceiling, so a TLS 1.0 or 1.1 server connects where the operating system's stack refuses those versions (BL-1143; amends ADR-0140) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0361](ADR-0361-a-stale-digest-401-or-407-is-answered-again-without-limit.md) | A Digest `401` or forward proxy `407` challenged with `stale=true` is answered afresh with the new nonce, without limit, as curl 8.21.0 does (BL-1148) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0362](ADR-0362-libcurl-writes-nothing-for-options-the-schannel-build-refuses.md) | `--libcurl` writes no line for the options curl's Schannel build refuses, and no `smb`/`smbs` in `--proto all` (BL-1174). | 2026-10-02 |
| [0363](ADR-0363-a-failed-tls-handshake-reports-the-lines-curl-prints-before-it.md) | A failed TLS handshake is reported as a `TlsHandshakeEvent` marked `Failed`, so `-v` prints the Schannel build's ALPN offer and the OpenSSL build's certificate details before a `--pinnedpubkey` refusal (BL-1149) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0364](ADR-0364-the-tls-1-2-only-hello-carries-each-builds-record-version-and-openssl-refuses-a-legacy-ceiling.md) | Below a TLS 1.3 ceiling the Schannel build's hello is in a record of the ceiling's version, and the OpenSSL build refuses `--tls-max 1.0` and `1.1` with a `protocol_version` alert and exit 35 (BL-1152) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0365](ADR-0365-the-openssl-builds-tls12-hello-pads-and-its-quic-hello-never-does.md) | The OpenSSL build's TLS 1.2-ceiling hello pads a 256-to-511-byte hello to 512, and its QUIC hello never pads (BL-1156) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0366](ADR-0366-a-failed-resolve-writes-curls-could-not-resolve-and-dns-filter-lines.md) | A failed resolve writes curl's `Could not resolve` lines and, under `--trace-config dns`, the filter's exit 6 and async teardown lines (BL-1157) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0367](ADR-0367-a-completed-ech-handshake-writes-curls-ech-result-line.md) | A completed `--ech` handshake writes curl's `ECH: result:` line after `SSL connection using` (BL-1170) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0368](ADR-0368-trace-config-smtp-writes-curls-smtp-state-machine-lines.md) | `--trace-config smtp` (and `protocol`, `all`) writes curl 8.21.0's `[SMTP]` state machine lines from the SMTP handler; under the trace the body and its end-of-data mark are separate data events, as measured (BL-1163) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0369](ADR-0369-curl-does-not-write-the-httpsrr-builds-https-rr-lines.md) | Curl does not write the `--enable-httpsrr` build's `Some HTTPS RR to process` and `HTTPS-RR:` lines, and asks the HTTPS query only under `--ech true` or `hard` (BL-1173) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0370](ADR-0370-trace-config-ws-writes-curls-websocket-frame-lines.md) | `--trace-config ws` (and `protocol`, `all`) writes curl 8.21.0's `[WS]` frame decode, pong, upload and established lines from the WebSocket handler (BL-1164) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0371](ADR-0371-the-openssl-build-reports-a-failed-handshake-before-every-tls-failure.md) | The OpenSSL build reports a failed `TlsHandshakeEvent` before every TLS failure: the certificate lines before an exit 60, the ALPN offer before an exit 35, and the hand-built client's lines before a pin refusal (BL-1178) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0372](ADR-0372-trace-config-ssh-writes-curls-ssh-state-lines.md) | `--trace-config ssh` (and `protocol`, `all`) writes curl 8.21.0's `[SSH]` state changes and phase lines for a `publickey` login and an SFTP or SCP download, without the timing-dependent `block=1` and `pollset` lines (BL-1166) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0373](ADR-0373-trace-config-http2-writes-curls-frame-lines.md) | `--trace-config http/2` (and `protocol`, `all`) writes curl's `[HTTP/2]` session, frame, settings and stream-closed lines from the HTTP/2 framing, without curl's run-to-run buffering lines (BL-1167) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0374](ADR-0374-trace-config-ssh-failures-leave-through-the-freeing-state.md) | A failed SSH transfer's `--trace-config ssh` lines leave the failed state for `SSH_SFTP_CLOSE`, `SSH_SCP_CHANNEL_FREE` or `SSH_SESSION_FREE` with the exit code; uploads, listings, `-Q`, password and `keyboard-interactive` logins traced as measured (BL-1204) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0375](ADR-0375-trace-config-http3-writes-curls-stream-lines.md) | `--trace-config http/3` (and `protocol`, `all`) writes curl's `[HTTP/3]` `end_headers`, `DATA len`, `ACK`, `CLOSED` and `quic close` stream lines in curl's places, without curl's I/O loop lines; the connection lines and header echoes are BL-1208 (BL-1168) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0376](ADR-0376-trace-config-quic-writes-no-lines-of-its-own.md) | `--trace-config quic` writes no lines of its own: curl's ngtcp2 build writes no `[QUIC]` line under `quic`, `network`, `protocol` or `all` (measured); pinned as none (BL-1169) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0377](ADR-0377-trace-config-ssh-agent-lines-follow-the-server-answers.md) | The `--trace-config ssh` agent line `auth user ... for key ...` is written once per identity attempt plus once per server answer; a listed link goes through `SSH_SFTP_READDIR_LINK`; `-I` on an SFTP directory sends `STAT` in `SSH_SFTP_FILETIME`, all as measured (BL-1207) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0378](ADR-0378-an-unknown-ech-mode-fails-the-transfer-with-exit-43-as-the-ech-build-does.md) | An `--ech` mode libcurl's `setopt_ech` refuses (anything but `false`, `grease`, `true`, `hard`, `ecl:<list>`, `pn:<name>`, case-sensitive) fails the transfer with exit 43 and `setopt 0x2855 got bad argument`, as curls ECH build does, not exit 2 as the platform builds without ECH do (BL-1172) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0379](ADR-0379-libcurl-writes-the-transfer-file-lines-and-stops-where-the-known-hosts-setopt-fails.md) | `--libcurl` writes `-T`'s upload URL, `UPLOAD` and `INFILESIZE_LARGE`, `--etag-compare`'s `If-None-Match` header, `-C -`'s offset and `SSH_KNOWNHOSTS` from the console's per-transfer file facts, and stops the source after the SSH lines when curl fails to set the known-hosts file (BL-1177) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0380](ADR-0380-doh-sub-transfers-write-their-own-dns-prefixed-verbose-lines-one-after-another.md) | Under `--trace-config dns` each DoH sub-transfer writes its own `-v` lines with `[DNS] ` in front through `Curl.Output`'s `DohSubTransferEvents`, the A and AAAA queries one after another, numbered from `#1` (BL-1180) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0381](ADR-0381-the-dns-filter-traces-proxies-unix-sockets-negative-entries-and-async-resolves.md) | Under `--trace-config dns` the DNS filter's lines cover a connect through a proxy (its first hop), over a Unix socket (`transport=6`), on a negative DNS cache entry (with plain `-v`'s three `Could not resolve` lines), and a looked-up name's `resolve complete` and async shutdown after a refused dial (BL-1181) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0382](ADR-0382-the-multi-trace-lines-are-tied-to-the-transfers-own-lines.md) | Under `--trace-config multi`, `network`, `all` and `-vvvv` the `[MULTI]` lines of a plain HTTP transfer are written by `MultiStateTraceEvents`, each group tied to the transfer line curl writes it beside; `[PGRS-*]` stamps are microseconds on the runner's clock, the poll descriptor the `[TCP]` one or 3 (BL-1188) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0383](ADR-0383-the-http-handler-writes-the-upload-readers-read-trace-lines.md) | Under `--trace-config read` the HTTP handler writes an HTTP/1.x `-d` or `-T` body's upload reader lines, and `ClientReaderResetTraceEvents` a reset line as each redirect hop starts; rewound, chunked, `100 Continue`, multipart and HTTP/2-3 bodies write none yet (BL-1189) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0384](ADR-0384-an-smtp-send-failure-ends-the-transfer-with-exit-55.md) | An SMTP command or message that cannot be written ends the transfer with exit 55, `Send failure: Connection was reset` or `Failed sending data to the peer`, sending and reading nothing more (BL-1243) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0385](ADR-0385-approved-findings-are-released-by-a-dispatched-workflow.md) | The board page's Release buttons dispatch `release-approved-findings.yml`, which runs `New-TasksFromAcceptedFindings.ps1` (with `-Id` only when the script has it), lands the tasks on `work/dark-factory` with dedupe and the findings on `audit`, and opens but never merges the audit pull request (BL-1249) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0386](ADR-0386-trace-config-http2-traces-the-h2c-upgrade-and-echoes-the-head.md) | Under `-v --trace-config http/2` an h2c upgrade writes curl's `added`, `upgrading`, `created session via Upgrade` and `(via h1 upgrade)` lines and stream 1's frame lines, and every HTTP/2 head line is echoed as `status:` or `header:` right after its `<` line; I/O-loop lines stay out (BL-1205) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0387](ADR-0387-the-hand-built-openssl-build-reports-a-failed-handshake-before-every-handshake-failure.md) | `HandBuiltTlsProvider`'s OpenSSL build reports a failed `TlsHandshakeEvent` before every handshake failure: the certificate lines before an exit 60, the ALPN offer before an exit 35 (BL-1202) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0388](ADR-0388-trace-config-http3-writes-the-connection-lines-and-echoes-the-head.md) | Under `-v --trace-config http/3` a QUIC connect writes curl's `handshake complete`, `max bidi streams`, `peer verified` and `connect` lines, the first stream the peer's idle timeout, each head line its `status:` / `header:` echo, and a finished transfer `easy handle is done`, `no active streams` and `MAX_CONCURRENT` (BL-1208) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0389](ADR-0389-chunked-stdin-continue-and-multipart-bodies-write-their-read-trace-lines.md) | Under `--trace-config read` chunked, stdin (`-T -`), `100 Continue` and multipart (`-F`) HTTP/1.x bodies write curl's `[READ]` lines: `len=-1` and `total=-1` for stdin, `cr_mime_read` for `-F`, `http_chunk` for chunks, and two held-back `client_read` lines around a head that waits (BL-1214) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0390](ADR-0390-connect-timeout-timer-lines-are-written-for-the-connect-phase-from-the-configured-delays.md) | `--trace-config timer` writes a given `--connect-timeout`'s `[TIMER] [CONNECTTIMEOUT]` lines around a direct connect, and `multi` adds each timer's `expires in` line, written from the configured delays; the response wait and `-m`'s `[TIMEOUT]` lines are a follow-up (BL-1210) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0391](ADR-0391-a-refused-connect-and-a-reused-connection-write-their-own-multi-lines.md) | Under `--trace-config multi` a refused connect writes curl's close, `connect failed -> 7`, `multi_done` and `[COMPLETED]` lines, polling once, and a reused connection writes its own lines up to `xfer_setup` (BL-1212) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0392](ADR-0392-a-redirect-after-a-request-body-writes-curls-rewind-lines.md) | A `3xx` under `-L` after a request body writes `Need to rewind upload for next request` from the HTTP handler, and `--trace-config read` adds curl's three rewind `[READ]` lines around the hop (BL-1213) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0393](ADR-0393-camellia-reads-its-s-box-by-masked-scan-and-is-constant-time.md) | `Camellia`'s F-function reads SBOX1 by one masked scan of all 256 entries per round, so no address depends on the key or data, and it is constant-time; supersedes ADR-0145 (BL-1268, AF-0012) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0394](ADR-0394-the-ssh-library-exposes-its-server-byte-readers-for-fuzzing.md) | `Curl.Protocol.Ssh.UnitLibrary` exposes its packet, zlib, `KEXINIT`, SFTP attribute and host-key signature readers over raw bytes through the public `SshWireDecoders`, so the audit fuzzer can reach them (AF-0011, BL-1267) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0395](ADR-0395-aria-reads-its-s-boxes-by-masked-scan-and-is-constant-time.md) | `Aria`'s substitution layers read each of its four S-boxes by one masked scan of all 256 entries, so no address depends on the key or data, and it is constant-time; supersedes ADR-0147's S-box choice (BL-1269, AF-0013) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0396](ADR-0396-des-reads-its-s-boxes-by-masked-scan-and-is-constant-time.md) | `Des`'s round function reads each of its eight S-boxes by one masked scan of all 64 entries, so no address depends on the key or data, and it is constant-time; supersedes ADR-0156's table look-up (BL-1270, AF-0014) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0397](ADR-0397-the-windows-build-refuses-the-options-curls-schannel-build-lacks.md) | On Windows `--http2`, `--http2-prior-knowledge`, `--http3`, `--http3-only`, the six TLS-SRP options and `--ssl-sessions` are refused with exit 2 as curl's Schannel build refuses them; accepted elsewhere (BL-1278, AF-0022) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0398](ADR-0398-cast-128-reads-its-s-boxes-by-masked-scan-and-is-constant-time.md) | `Cast128`'s round function and key schedule read each 256-entry S-box by one masked scan, so no address depends on the key or data, and it is constant-time (BL-1271, AF-0015) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0399](ADR-0399-rc4-swaps-and-reads-its-permutation-by-masked-scan-and-is-constant-time.md) | `Rc4` swaps and reads its permutation at the key-dependent index by one masked scan of all 256 entries, so no address depends on the key, and it is constant-time (BL-1272, AF-0016) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0400](ADR-0400-blowfish-reads-its-s-boxes-by-masked-scan-and-is-constant-time.md) | `Blowfish`'s F function reads all four S-boxes by one vectorised masked scan, so no address depends on the key, passphrase or data, and `Blowfish` and `BcryptPbkdf` are constant-time (BL-1273, AF-0017) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0401](ADR-0401-max-time-timer-lines-and-the-response-wait-timer-lines-are-written.md) | `--trace-config timer` writes `-m`'s `[TIMER] [TIMEOUT]` lines beside the connect timeout's, the nearest named in `gives`, and the response wait's timer lines after `Request completely sent off` (among the `[MULTI]` poll lines under `multi`), from the configured delays (BL-1258) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0402](ADR-0402-native-curl-exe-links-com-through-api-sets-and-skips-the-account-home-on-windows.md) | The native `curl.exe` links the runtime's COM calls through `mincore.lib` (the `api-ms-win-core-com` API sets) instead of `ole32.lib`, and skips the account home lookup on Windows, so a plain GET loads no `ole32`, `user32`, `gdi32` or `imm32` (BL-1290) | Accepted | 2026-10-02 |
| [0403](ADR-0403-a-failed-telnet-window-size-write-reports-curls-send-failure-line.md) | A telnet NAWS window size write that fails reports curl's socket filter line `Send failure: <text>` (the Schannel build's Winsock words on Windows, `strerror` elsewhere) between the suboption's `Sending data failed (N)` lines, from curl's source, since loopback never loses the race to measure it (BL-1312) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0404](ADR-0404-the-conformance-server-skips-its-waits-unless-a-curl-timer-races-them.md) | The upstream conformance runner gives the `sws` emulation a wait-skipping clock unless the command names a curl timer that races it (`-m`, `-y`, `-Y`, `--connect-timeout`, `--expect100-timeout`), so `writedelay` and `<postcmd>` `wait` take no real time and no case's server timing depends on machine load (BL-1355) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0405](ADR-0405-a-crl-is-expired-from-its-nextupdate-moment-on.md) | A `--crlfile` list whose `nextUpdate` equals the moment checked has expired, as OpenSSL's `X509_cmp_time` counts an equal time as past (AF-0029, BL-1371) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0406](ADR-0406-a-fixed-lane-shift-retires-idle-lanes-down-to-the-boards-capacity.md) | A fixed `-Lanes N` shift retires idle lanes down to the board's capacity every 5 minutes and adds them back one a step up to N, and every retire goes to an idle lane first, so lanes stop polling overlapping touches (BL-1374, AF-0032) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0407](ADR-0407-the-run-cost-cap-follows-the-median-run.md) | `RunDarkFactory.ps1` caps each headless run at 2.7 times the median of the newest 40 task runs' costs, between $2 and `-TaskBudgetUsd`, so runs stay under three times the median as it moves (BL-1377, AF-0033) | Accepted; numbers superseded by ADR-0478 | 2026-10-03 |
