| [0408](ADR-0408-proxy-http2-tunnels-over-an-h2-proxy-off-windows-and-proxy-http3-stays-refused.md) | `--proxy-http2` keeps the refusal on Windows and, off Windows, offers `h2` to an HTTPS proxy and tunnels through an HTTP/2 CONNECT stream in `Curl.Networking.UnitLibrary`, falling back to HTTP/1.1; `--proxy-http3` stays refused everywhere, as the reference builds refuse it (BL-1397) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0409](ADR-0409-skipped-alt-svc-and-illegal-sts-headers-are-reported-before-their-header-line.md) | A skipped `Alt-Svc` alternative and an illegal `Strict-Transport-Security` header are reported with curl 8.21.0's `-v` lines just before their header line: `IAltSvcStore` returns ordered outcomes with skip reasons, and a new `IHstsStore` learns HSTS per header during the transfer (BL-1407) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |

## Template

```markdown
# ADR-NNNN — <title>

- **Status:** Proposed / Accepted / Superseded by ADR-NNNN
- **Date:** YYYY-MM-DD

## Context
The forces at play. What made a decision necessary.

## Decision
What was chosen, stated as a decision rather than a description.

## Consequences
What this makes easy, and what it makes hard. Both, honestly.

## Alternatives considered
Each option and the specific reason it lost.
```
| [0410](ADR-0410-remote-file-times-travel-as-unix-seconds-so-r-can-stamp-and-cap-times-past-year-9999.md) | A remote file time travels as Unix seconds (`TransferResult.SourceLastWriteUnixSeconds`, with the `DateTimeOffset` property as an in-range view), so HTTP and `file://` times past year 9999 reach `-R`, which caps above 30827-12-31T23:59:59Z on Windows with curl's `Capping set filetime to max` warning and passes the time on elsewhere (BL-1409) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0411](ADR-0411-a-file-source-time-past-what-curl-holds-reads-as-minus-one-on-windows.md) | A `file://` source time past what curl holds reads as -1 on Windows (from local 3002-01-01: `-z` compares -1, `-R` leaves the output alone) and whole in Unix seconds elsewhere, read from the handle with `GetFileTime` (BL-1423) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0412](ADR-0412-http-last-modified-is-read-with-curls-getdate.md) | HTTP `Last-Modified` is read with `CurlDateParser`, curl's `parsedate`, into Unix seconds, so a year past 9999 reaches `-R` and `-z` compares in Unix seconds (BL-1428) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0413](ADR-0413-credentials-with-control-characters-are-refused-only-when-they-would-be-used.md) | URL and netrc credentials holding a control character are refused only when they would be used: not beside a `-u` user name, under `--netrc-optional` too, and on every redirect hop's own URL (BL-1411) | Accepted | 2026-10-03 |
| [0414](ADR-0414-a-tftp-first-reply-that-switches-direction-hands-the-transfer-over.md) | A TFTP first reply that switches direction hands the transfer over to the other direction's class, and every `failf` on the way is a noted failure whose message the transfer keeps (BL-1444) | Accepted | 2026-10-04 |
| [0415](ADR-0415-file-system-failures-off-windows-are-worded-from-errno.md) | Off Windows a failed `-R` stamp and a failed `--create-dirs` directory are worded from errno with curl's POSIX texts; `utimes` sets every time off Windows so the code is an errno (BL-1433) | Accepted | 2026-10-04 |
| [0416](ADR-0416-a-file-path-decoding-to-nul-is-refused-with-exit-3.md) | A `file://` path that percent-decodes to a NUL is refused with exit 3 and `URL using bad/illegal format or missing URL` before anything opens, with no `-v` line (BL-1451) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0417](ADR-0417-every-unit-test-writes-timing-and-a-slow-line-through-shared-test-diagnostics.md) | Every unit test writes START, END and over 3 seconds SLOW: lines from MSTest global hooks in a linked `TestDiagnostics.cs`, which also gives tests ARRANGE, ACT, ASSERT, BYTES, DIFF and PHASE lines (BL-1457) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0418](ADR-0418-a-drive-letter-file-download-decoding-to-nul-gives-the-file-connect-text.md) | A drive-letter `file://` download decoding to NUL gives `file_connect`'s `URL using bad/illegal format or missing URL`, as the drive-less one does; ADR-0416's `URL rejected` came from a space in the measured path (BL-1526) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0419](ADR-0419-a-read-aborted-after-a-peer-reset-is-reported-as-the-reset-on-windows.md) | On Windows a TCP read failing with WSAECONNABORTED, which Windows gives a receive issued a few milliseconds after a peer's RST, is reported as curl's `Connection was reset` (BL-1450) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0420](ADR-0420-the-conformance-harness-reports-every-upstream-test-feature-curl-has.md) | The conformance harness reports every upstream test feature Curl has, by `runtests.pl`'s names, and leaves off libcurl's build and API features, `ftp`, names absent from `curl -V` and `codeset-utf8` (BL-1440) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0421](ADR-0421-integration-tests-live-only-in-integrationtests-projects.md) | Integration tests (a socket, the disk, the OS, a native API or a system agent) live only in `Curl.<Area>.IntegrationTests` projects, sorted just before their library; CI needs no change; slow pure computation is `LongRunning`, skipped unless `CURL_RUN_LONG_RUNNING_TESTS=1`; test-owned temporary files and unconnected loopback sockets stay unit tests (BL-1598) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0422](ADR-0422-audit-findings-close-on-mechanical-evidence.md) | Audit findings close on mechanical evidence: the run's own targeted rerun on the clean commit, a reliable re-audit or two "no"s running; duplicates and Stewart's close; Re-fix tasks; planted-defect overlaps set aside | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0423](ADR-0423-the-conformance-harness-caps-a-repeat-at-16-mib.md) | The conformance harness caps one `%repeat` at 16 MiB of output; a longer one is left as written and listed as unsupported, so its case is skipped rather than running out of memory (BL-1648) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0424](ADR-0424-des3-weak-keys-refused-as-kerberos-cryptography-weak-key.md) | A `des3-cbc-sha1` key whose triple DES collapses to single DES (equal first and second, or second and third, parts) is refused as `KerberosCryptographyError.WeakKey`, never a `CryptographicException` (BL-1649) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0425](ADR-0425-ldap-filters-nest-at-most-256-sets.md) | An LDAP URL's filter nests at most 256 `&`, `\|` or `!` sets; a deeper one is refused as a bad filter rather than overflowing the stack (BL-1661) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0426](ADR-0426-curl-cryptography-compiles-optimized-in-debug.md) | `Curl.Cryptography.UnitLibrary` compiles optimized in Debug too, so its constant-time table scans run at Release speed under test; one bcrypt hash drops from about 600 ms to 42 ms (BL-1558) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0427](ADR-0427-ntlmv2-timestamp-before-1601-is-a-negative-filetime.md) | An NTLMv2 timestamp before 1601 is written as a negative FILETIME, as curl's signed arithmetic writes it, rather than letting `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` escape `NtlmChallengeAnswerer.Answer` (BL-1654) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0428](ADR-0428-parallel-progress-sizes-and-times-cut-to-their-columns.md) | A `-Z` progress size is cut to five characters and a time to eight, as curl's six- and nine-byte `msnprintf` buffers cut them, so 100 MiB less one byte is `99.10` (BL-1657) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0429](ADR-0429-montgomery-exponentiation-runs-on-64-bit-limbs.md) | `MontgomeryModulus.Exponentiate` runs on 64-bit limbs with its own squaring, carries from unsigned comparisons (a flag set, never a branch), and its hot routines optimized from the first call (BL-1656) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0430](ADR-0430-tftp-receives-block-size-plus-four.md) | A TFTP download receives the block size plus four bytes, as curl's `recvfrom` does, so an oversized DATA packet is cut to the block size on Linux and macOS and noted as `Received too short packet` on Windows (BL-1668) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0431](ADR-0431-native-curl-exe-delay-loads-dlls-a-plain-transfer-never-calls.md) | The native `curl.exe` delay-loads `ncrypt`, `Secur32`, `ADVAPI32`, `CRYPT32` and `IPHLPAPI`, so a plain `http://` GET maps none of them and peaks under 2x real curl's working set (BL-1682, AF-0063) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0432](ADR-0432-connect-reply-heads-and-http2-http3-trailers-count-toward-5000-headers.md) | The CONNECT reply that opens a tunnel and HTTP/2 and HTTP/3 trailers count toward curl's 5000 stored response headers, the 5001st failing with exit 100 (BL-1609) | Accepted | 2026-10-07 |
| [0433](ADR-0433-a-gap-analysis-office-measures-curl-against-upstream-curl-releases.md) | A gap analysis office measures Curl against upstream curl's own release data (the targeted version's tarball, never Curl's own tables), scores seven areas "X of Y match", closes a gap only on re-measurement, and follows each new curl release (BL-1719 to BL-1748) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0434](ADR-0434-native-curl-exe-is-optimized-for-size-without-stack-trace-data.md) | The native `curl.exe` publishes with `OptimizationPreference=Size` and `StackTraceSupport=false`: 19.4% smaller (17.0 to 13.7 MiB) so each launch costs less under on-access scanning, with crypto and TLS within 5% (BL-1749, AF-0048) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0435](ADR-0435-environment-gap-uses-the-gap-format-reason-vocabulary.md) | The environment gap tool scores missing recipes `no-probe` and other platforms' config locations `platform:<os>`, Gap-Format's own reasons, and measures the executable-folder `.curlrc` with copied binaries (BL-1727) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0436](ADR-0436-board-only-factory-pushes-skip-ci.md) | Board-only dark factory pushes (claim, requeue, park, return, CI-watch filing) say `[skip ci]` so they no longer cancel the CI run testing the code; the shift-end merge starts CI by hand on such a head (BL-1767, AF-0089) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0437](ADR-0437-a-task-s-earlier-runs-come-off-its-cost-cap.md) | `RunDarkFactory.ps1` takes what a task's runs of the last 24 hours cost off its next run's cost cap, down to $1, so a requeued task stays near one cap in all (BL-1773, AF-0095) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0438](ADR-0438-a-task-whose-runs-spent-its-cap-is-not-run-again.md) | `RunDarkFactory.ps1` blocks a freshly claimed task, without running it, when its runs of the last 24 hours left less than $1 of its cost cap, so repeated requeues cannot add up past one cap (BL-1774, AF-0096) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0439](ADR-0439-windows-features-line-lists-sspi.md) | On Windows `curl -V` lists `SSPI` after `SSL`, as the Schannel reference build does, because NTLM, Negotiate and Kerberos answer through SSPI there (BL-1796, GF-0003) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0440](ADR-0440-an-auth-retry-rewinds-a-seekable-upload.md) | A 401 or 407 to a request whose body is a seekable stream - a `-T` file or a `-F` form - is answered with the body rewound and sent again whole, as curl 8.21.0 does (BL-1798, GF-0005) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0441](ADR-0441-a-digest-post-probes-with-an-empty-body.md) | With `--digest` or `--proxy-digest` and no challenge answered yet, the first POST or PUT goes with an empty body and `Content-Length: 0`; the body follows with the answer, or after a 2xx (BL-1799, GF-0006) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0442](ADR-0442-max-filesize-holds-the-kept-decoded-body.md) | `--max-filesize` is not applied to a discarded body (a redirect `-L` follows), and with `--compressed` it holds the decoded bytes too, ending with exit 63 once they pass it (BL-1807, GF-0014) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0443](ADR-0443-d-dash-switches-standard-output-to-binary.md) | `-D -` switches standard output to binary mode, so `-w` line feeds stay LF on Windows, even under `-B` or after a failure (BL-1816, GF-0023) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0444](ADR-0444-features-line-lists-threadsafe.md) | `curl -V` lists `threadsafe` on every platform, after `SSL` (and `SSPI`), as both reference builds do; supersedes ADR-0021's `threadsafe` rows (BL-1828, GF-0035) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0445](ADR-0445-b-cookie-strings-stay-with-the-first-origin.md) | `-b name=value` strings are not sent after `-L` follows a redirect to another scheme, host or port unless `--location-trusted`; stored cookies keep their own rules (BL-1846, GF-0010) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0446](ADR-0446-config-file-header-text-keeps-its-utf8-bytes-on-windows.md) | A `-K` file's `-H`, `--proxy-header`, `-A` and `-e` text goes out as the file's UTF-8 bytes on Windows, re-spelled for the ANSI encoder (BL-1848, GF-0020) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0447](ADR-0447-skip-existing-note-only-under-a-trace-option.md) | The `--skip-existing` note stays behind `-v` or a trace option, as in curl 8.21.0; upstream tests 994, 996 and 1491 see it because their harness adds `--trace-ascii` (BL-1806, GF-0013) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0448](ADR-0448-windows-features-line-omits-ech.md) | `curl -V` on Windows omits `ECH` from `Features:`, as the Schannel reference build does; Linux and macOS keep it, and `--ech` still works everywhere (BL-1822, GF-0029) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0449](ADR-0449-windows-features-line-omits-gss-api.md) | `curl -V` on Windows omits `GSS-API` from `Features:`, as the Schannel reference build does, whose Negotiate answers through SSPI; Linux and macOS keep it (BL-1823, GF-0030) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0450](ADR-0450-windows-features-line-omits-http2.md) | `curl -V` on Windows omits `HTTP2` from `Features:`, as the Schannel reference build does; Linux and macOS keep it, and `--http2` still works everywhere (BL-1824, GF-0031) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0451](ADR-0451-windows-features-line-omits-http3.md) | `curl -V` on Windows omits `HTTP3` from `Features:`, as the Schannel reference build does; Linux and macOS keep it, and `--http3` still works everywhere (BL-1825, GF-0032) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0452](ADR-0452-windows-features-line-omits-tls-srp.md) | `curl -V` on Windows omits `TLS-SRP` from `Features:`, as the Schannel reference build does; Linux and macOS keep it, and the TLS-SRP options still work everywhere (BL-1826, GF-0033) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0453](ADR-0453-windows-protocols-line-omits-smb.md) | `curl -V` on Windows omits `smb` and `smbs` from `Protocols:`, as the Schannel reference build does; Linux and macOS keep them, and `smb://` and `smbs://` still work everywhere (BL-1830, GF-0037) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0454](ADR-0454-dns-and-ech-options-stay-supported-and-no-proxy-http3-is-accepted.md) | `--no-proxy-http3` is accepted and does nothing, as the Schannel build accepts it; `--dns-servers`, `--dns-interface`, `--dns-ipv4-addr`, `--dns-ipv6-addr` and `--ech` stay supported on every platform, an intended difference from the Schannel build (BL-1829, GF-0036) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0455](ADR-0455-upstream-harness-answers-ntlm-hand-built-and-screens-internet-cases.md) | The upstream case harness answers NTLM with the hand-built context on every platform, and skips a server-less case that expects success from a host on the internet (BL-1858, GF-0001) | Accepted | 2026-10-08 |
| [0456](ADR-0456-upstream-harness-ssh-server-lives-in-its-own-library-reusing-the-ssh-client-code.md) | The upstream harness's SSH server lives in `Curl.Conformance.SshServer.UnitLibrary`, reuses `Curl.Protocol.Ssh.UnitLibrary`'s wire code through `InternalsVisibleTo`, and uses a fixed Ed25519 host key so fingerprints are stable (BL-1899) | Accepted | 2026-10-09 |
| [0457](ADR-0457-upstream-harness-gives-rtspport-a-value-and-builds-no-rtsp-server.md) | The upstream harness gives `%RTSPPORT` the value 8996 and builds no `rtspd` stand-in, since all 9 cases naming it are `<tool>` libtests that skip for that reason (BL-1902) | Accepted | 2026-10-09 |
| [0458](ADR-0458-upstream-harness-rewrites-pwd-logdir-and-srcdir-libtest-before-expansion.md) | The upstream harness rewrites `%PWD/%LOGDIR` to `%LOGDIR` and `%SRCDIR` before the two emulated libtest scripts to `.` before expansion, gives `%SRCDIR` no other value, and names curl's output `%LOGDIR/curl%TESTNUMBER.out` (BL-1944) | Accepted | 2026-10-09 |
| [0459](ADR-0459-upstream-harness-serves-mail-over-implicit-tls-and-emulates-no-starttls.md) | The upstream harness serves `%SMTPSPORT`, `%IMAPSPORT` and `%POP3SPORT` through an implicit-TLS relay to the plain mail stand-ins and emulates no STARTTLS, since `ftpserver.pl` offers none (BL-1914) | Accepted | 2026-10-09 |
| [0460](ADR-0460-upstream-harness-dials-in-memory-servers-through-tcpconnector.md) | The upstream harness dials its in-memory servers through `TcpConnector` with `InMemoryServerTcpDialer` as the `ITcpDialer`, so curl's own CONNECT tunnel and PROXY-line code runs in every case; no new seam in `Curl.Networking.UnitLibrary` (BL-1915) | Accepted | 2026-10-09 |
| [0461](ADR-0461-an-ech-offer-is-tls-1-3-alone-and-a-tls-1-2-server-hello-is-exit-35.md) | An `--ech` offer of a real configuration is a TLS 1.3-only ClientHello, and a TLS 1.2 ServerHello to it is exit 35 with OpenSSL's `unsupported protocol`, as curl 8.21.0 on OpenSSL 4.0.0 does (BL-1950; amends ADR-0359) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0462](ADR-0462-upstream-harness-serves-ftps-over-implicit-tls-with-plain-data-and-emulates-no-auth-tls.md) | The upstream harness serves `%FTPSPORT` through an implicit-TLS relay to the plain FTP stand-in, with plain data connections, and emulates no `AUTH TLS`, since `ftpserver.pl` offers none (BL-1913) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0463](ADR-0463-ftp-ascii-uploads-convert-line-feeds-to-crlf-off-windows.md) | An FTP ASCII upload (`-B` or `;type=a`) converts each lone line feed to CRLF off Windows, as curl built with `CURL_PREFER_LF_LINEENDS` does; `--crlf` converts everywhere (BL-1957) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0464](ADR-0464-upstream-harness-times-ftp-delay-and-slowdowndata-on-the-server-clock.md) | The upstream harness carries out `ftpserver.pl`'s `DELAY <COMMAND> <seconds>` and `SLOWDOWNDATA` (5 ms a data byte) on the runner's server clock, so test190 and test1086 run (BL-1958) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0465](ADR-0465-ci-watch-confirms-a-one-platform-failure-after-5-minutes-not-30.md) | The CI watch confirms a failure seen on one platform in only the newest run after 5 minutes, not 30, so a Linux- or macOS-only break is filed within a heartbeat or two (BL-1969; AF-0150) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0466](ADR-0466-a-config-file-that-is-not-utf8-is-read-in-the-ansi-code-page-on-windows.md) | On Windows a `-K` file that is not valid UTF-8 is read in the ANSI code page, so its bytes go out raw with no Unicode warning; the unreadable-config line wraps at the parse's `COLUMNS` (BL-1973, GF-0020) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0467](ADR-0467-a-kept-connection-is-asked-whether-its-peer-closed-it-before-it-is-sent-on-again.md) | A kept connection is asked whether its peer closed it (`IConnection.HasPeerClosed`) before a retry is sent on it or the pool reuses it, so a half-closed connection is reported dead as curl reports it (BL-2018); amended: the pool asks only a connection idle a second or more and drops a closed one silently, as curl 8.22.0 does (BL-2203) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0468](ADR-0468-ftp-keeps-the-control-connection-for-the-next-url.md) | FTP keeps the control connection in the run's connection cache for the next URL, skips login, same TYPE and same-path CWDs on reuse, and sends `QUIT` only when the cache closes it at exit (BL-1981, GF-0051) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0469](ADR-0469-imap-keeps-its-connection-for-the-next-url-and-tags-by-connection-number.md) | IMAP keeps a logged-in connection in the run's connection cache for the next URL with the same login, skips a repeated `SELECT`, sends `LOGOUT` only when the cache closes it, and tags each connection's commands with `A` plus its number modulo 26 (BL-1987, GF-0057) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0470](ADR-0470-sasl-plain-keeps-an-empty-authorization-identity-as-curl-8-21-0-sends-it.md) | SASL PLAIN keeps an empty authorization identity unless `--sasl-authzid` is given, as curl 8.21.0 sends it; upstream test938, which expects the user name there, is in curl's own `tests/data/DISABLED` and is measured `excluded` (BL-1992, GF-0062) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0471](ADR-0471-a-decoded-piece-that-would-pass-max-filesize-is-not-written.md) | A `--compressed` body's decoded piece that would pass `--max-filesize` is not written; exit 63 with `Would have exceeded max file size`, as curl 8.21.0 (amends ADR-0442; BL-2007, GF-0014) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0472](ADR-0472-digest-authorization-takes-the-sspi-layout-where-the-schannel-build-is-matched.md) | `DigestAuthenticator` writes SSPI's layout (no blank after a comma, `algorithm` before `response`, `qop` quoted after it, `opaque` last) when `matchesSspiBuild` is set, as curl 8.21.0's Schannel build sends it; curl's own `, ` form otherwise (BL-2022, BL-2033) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0473](ADR-0473-upstream-test2043-is-measured-excluded-because-it-checks-a-live-internet-host.md) | Upstream test2043 (`--ssl-no-revoke` against revoked.badssl.com) stays skipped in the ratchet and is to be measured `excluded` by the gap office (intent; the converter has no such rule yet): it checks a live internet host's revocation status, which no in-process stand-in reproduces; `--ssl-no-revoke` is pinned by unit tests (BL-2020, GF-0001) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0474](ADR-0474-upstream-test1211-is-measured-excluded-because-upstream-disables-it-and-real-curl-exits-10.md) | Upstream test1211 (FTP `-P -`, `NODATACONN425`, no `--max-time`) is not listed and is measured `excluded` by the gap office: upstream's `tests/data/DISABLED` lists it, and real curl 8.21.0 exits 10 after `QUIT` on it, which Curl matches (BL-2021, GF-0048) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0475](ADR-0475-upstream-case-stderr-naming-logdir-is-compared-with-wrapped-notes-joined.md) | An upstream case's `<stderr>` that names the harness's long absolute `%LOGDIR` is compared with curl's 79-column-wrapped `Note:`, `Warning:` and `curl:` lines joined on both sides (`UpstreamWrappedMessageLines`), so test994, test996 and test1491 pass; `COLUMNS` is not widened (BL-2038) | Accepted | 2026-10-10 |
| [0476](ADR-0476-digest-cnonce-follows-the-matched-build.md) | A Digest `cnonce` follows the matched build: 32 lower-case hex digits where the Schannel build's SSPI layout is written, 16 base64 characters from curl's own code otherwise (`DigestClientNonce.ForBuild`; amends ADR-0025, ADR-0472; BL-2048, AF-0155) | Accepted | 2026-10-11 |
| [0477](ADR-0477-an-http-body-read-is-written-to-the-output-in-one-write.md) | An HTTP/1.x Content-Length or read-to-close body that is not decoded is written one read per write, then reported as 16 KiB data events and progress counts; a failed write is placed in its 16 KiB piece by the accepted count, so `passed 16384` stays (BL-2043, AF-0144) | Accepted | 2026-10-11 |
| [0478](ADR-0478-the-run-cost-cap-is-4-times-the-median-run-between-3-and-10-dollars.md) | `RunDarkFactory.ps1` caps each headless run at 4 times the median of the newest 40 task runs' costs, never below $3 and never above `-TaskBudgetUsd` (default 10), so a task that needs a few more turns finishes; supersedes ADR-0407's numbers (BL-2116) | Accepted | 2026-10-12 |
| [0479](ADR-0479-curl-retargets-from-curl-8-21-0-to-curl-8-22-0.md) | Curl and the gap office retarget from curl 8.21.0 to curl 8.22.0: every Git for Windows since 2.56.0 (2026-09-28) ships 8.22.0 as the matched Schannel reference, six of seven areas are at 100% against 8.21.0, and 8.22.0 adds 4 `--httpsig-*` options, drops TLS-SRP and adds, changes or removes 154 upstream cases; measurements recorded on 8.21.0 stay until re-measured (amends ADR-0433; BL-1748) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0480](ADR-0480-the-six-tls-srp-options-warn-with-no-function-as-curl-8-22-0-does.md) | `--tlsuser`, `--tlspassword`, `--tlsauthtype` and their `--proxy-` forms are no-function value options on every platform, warning `is deprecated and has no function anymore` as curl 8.22.0 does, rather than being refused on Windows; the TLS-SRP plumbing behind them goes in BL-2152 (BL-2142, AF-0183) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0481](ADR-0481-srp-raises-its-secret-exponents-through-the-constant-time-finite-field-exponentiation.md) | `SrpClient` raises g to x and a, and B - k * v to a + u * x, through `Curl.Cryptography`'s constant-time `FiniteFieldDiffieHellman` instead of `BigInteger.ModPow`, with a fixed-width exponent and the bases 0, 1 and N - 1 answered without exponentiation (amends ADR-0229; AF-0182, BL-2141) | Superseded by ADR-0480 (BL-2159) | 2026-10-13 |
| [0482](ADR-0482-a-fix-found-through-an-upstream-case-also-gets-a-focused-unit-test.md) | Any product fix found through an upstream case (ratchet, gap finding, audit conformance finding) also adds a focused unit test in the fixed library's own `.UnitTests` project, written in CLAUDE.md and the task-run command (BL-2156) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0483](ADR-0483-a-file-url-naming-a-directory-lists-it-on-windows-as-curl-8-22-0-does.md) | A `file://` URL naming a directory lists its entry names on Windows too, as curl 8.22.0's `win32_file_list` does, rather than failing with exit 37 as 8.21.0's Windows build did (BL-2166, GF-0074) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0484](ADR-0484-an-mqtt-topic-decoded-to-a-control-byte-is-exit-3-before-subscribe.md) | An MQTT topic decoded to a byte 0x00 to 0x1F is exit 3 after CONNACK and before SUBSCRIBE or PUBLISH, following upstream test 2109 over the local 8.22.0 binary (BL-2168) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0485](ADR-0485-curl-implements-the-experimental-httpsig-options-on-every-platform.md) | Curl implements curl 8.22.0's experimental `--httpsig-algo`, `--httpsig-key`, `--httpsig-keyid` and `--httpsig-headers` (RFC 9421) on every platform as an intended difference from the Windows reference, which is built without them; the signing is hand-built in `Curl.HttpMessageSignatures.UnitLibrary` from upstream's `lib/http_httpsig.c`, and an interactive session adds their facets to the gap office's option exclusions (BL-2171, GF-0040) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0486](ADR-0486-relicense-from-mit-to-the-curl-licence.md) | Relicense from MIT to the curl licence: `LICENSE.txt` is curl's own licence text with this project's copyright line (SPDX `curl`) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0487](ADR-0487-httpsig-options-are-parsed-as-strings-and-checked-when-an-http-transfer-is-set-up.md) | `Curl.Cli` parses `--httpsig-algo`, `--httpsig-key`, `--httpsig-keyid` and `--httpsig-headers` verbatim as curl 8.22.0's `tool_getparam.c` does (blank refused, the last winning); their checks (exit 2) and the key file read (exit 26) happen when an HTTP transfer is set up, as `config2setopts.c` does (BL-2182), and the recorded `--help` references gain their four rows (BL-2180) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0488](ADR-0488-a-listed-upstream-case-another-platforms-curl-runs-is-not-a-regression-where-its-features-are-missing.md) | A listed upstream case whose `<features>` another platform's Curl meets and this one's does not (test2087 and test3023 need `Schannel`) is skipped there as `SkippedOnThisPlatform` and judged `Inconclusive`, not a regression; any other skip of a listed case still fails the ratchet (BL-2176) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0489](ADR-0489-a-died-connection-resend-is-drawn-with-one-done-draw-on-the-progress-meter.md) | A request resent on a fresh connection after its reused connection died is reported as `ITransferProgress.ReportRequestResentOnFreshConnection` and drawn with one done draw, a newline and a new zero line, as curl 8.22.0 draws upstream test675 (BL-2200) | Accepted | 2026-10-13 |
| [0490](ADR-0490-the-gap-office-excludes-upstream-source-checks-and-cases-that-run-only-on-another-platform.md) | The gap office measures a `<command type="perl">` case that never names `%CURL` as `excluded` `upstream-source-check`, and a case that runs only on another platform (`!win32` or a `<precheck>` refusing `MSWin32` on Windows, `win32` elsewhere) as `excluded` `platform:<os>`; test321 stays `unmeasured` for the harness to run (BL-2160, GF-0038) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0491](ADR-0491-the-h2c-stand-in-for-nghttpx-relays-each-stream-to-sws-in-memory.md) | The case runner's stand-in for nghttpx (`Curl.Conformance.Http2Server.UnitLibrary`) serves h2c by prior knowledge or `Upgrade: h2c` and relays each stream in memory to the sws emulation as HTTP/1.1 with `X-Forwarded-Proto: http` and `Via: 2 nghttpx`, answering with `server: nghttpx` and `via: 1.1 nghttpx`, within the client's flow-control windows (BL-2244) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0492](ADR-0492-curl-dns-server-is-honoured-only-behind-the-harness-seam.md) | The dialing `CreateRunner` honours `CURL_DNS_SERVER` only when the harness passes `honoursDnsServerVariable`, resolving through a `DnsServerResolver` whose UDP queries go through the run's `IDatagramConnector`; the shipped `curl` ignores it, as curl's release build does (BL-2248) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0493](ADR-0493-curl-time-fixes-the-hsts-clock-only-behind-the-harness-seam.md) | The dialing `CreateRunner` fixes the HSTS clock at `CURL_TIME` only when the harness passes `honoursHstsTimeVariable`; the shipped `curl` ignores it, as curl's release build does (BL-2234) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0494](ADR-0494-curl-hsts-http-learns-hsts-over-http-only-behind-the-harness-seam.md) | The HTTP handler hands every `Strict-Transport-Security` header to the HSTS store, which learns an `http` origin's only when the harness passes `honoursHstsHttpVariable` and `CURL_HSTS_HTTP` is set; the shipped `curl` ignores it, as curl's release build does (BL-2250) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0495](ADR-0495-http2-cases-run-off-windows-only.md) | The harness reports `http/2` and `h2c` off Windows only, as Curl's Schannel-matching Windows build has no `HTTP2`; the h2c stand-in reaches `%HTTP2PORT` through `UpstreamHttp2Server` and `%H2CVER` is `h2c` (BL-2230) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0496](ADR-0496-the-http2-tls-stand-in-for-nghttpx-offers-alpn-h2-on-port-9018.md) | The HTTP/2-over-TLS stand-in for nghttpx stands on `%HTTP2TLSPORT` 9018 with a certificate directory, offers ALPN `h2` alone and relays each stream to sws with `X-Forwarded-Proto: https` (BL-2245) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0497](ADR-0497-curl-altsvc-http-uses-alt-svc-over-http-only-behind-the-harness-seam.md) | The HTTP handler hands every `Alt-Svc` header to the alt-svc store, which learns and looks up an `http` origin only when the harness passes `honoursAltSvcHttpVariable` and `CURL_ALTSVC_HTTP` is set; the shipped `curl` ignores it, as curl's release build does (BL-2263) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0498](ADR-0498-the-http3-stand-in-takes-datagrams-on-port-9019-and-http3-cases-run-off-windows-only.md) | The nghttpx-h3 stand-in reaches `%HTTP3PORT` (9019) through `UpstreamHttp3Server`, TCP to that port is refused so `--http3` settles on QUIC, and the harness reports `http/3` off Windows only, as Curl's Schannel-matching Windows build refuses `--http3` (BL-2261) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0499](ADR-0499-a-failed-resolve-through-the-dns-server-resolver-is-cached-as-a-negative-entry.md) | `TcpConnector` keeps a look-up that answered no address as a negative DNS cache entry when its resolver is `DnsServerResolver` (the c-ares path), exit 43 excepted, so later transfers fail with `Negative DNS entry` and exit 6 without asking again; the system resolver and DoH keep nothing, as measured on the Schannel build (BL-2262) | Accepted | 2026-10-14 |
| [0500](ADR-0500-the-conformance-harness-reports-headers-api-on-every-platform.md) | The conformance harness reports `headers-api` on every platform, since Curl writes `-w %header{name}` and `%{header_json}`, which curl writes only with libcurl's headers API; 1670, 1671 and (off Windows) 2403 pass (BL-2264) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0501](ADR-0501-write-out-header-all-joins-every-hop-of-a-redirect-chain.md) | `-w %header{name:all:separator}` joins every value of the header from every response of a `-L` chain (`TransferReport.EarlierResponseHeaders`, then the last response), and `\}` in the braces is a literal `}`; upstream cases 764 and 765 pass (BL-2267) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0502](ADR-0502-the-http3-stand-in-sends-no-server-field.md) | The nghttpx-h3 stand-in relays sws's reply with no `server` field, as upstream's HTTP/3 cases 2501 and 2503 expect (its h2 stand-in keeps `server: nghttpx`); 2503 passes off Windows (BL-2268) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0503](ADR-0503-a-short-whole-solution-coverage-run-is-rechecked-per-project.md) | When a whole-solution `Measure-CodeQuality.ps1` run leaves a member short of line or branch coverage, the test projects reaching its library run again one at a time and their reports are merged in, so a report the merged run lost (CI run 38700283564, Cryptography) no longer fails the gate (BL-2269) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0504](ADR-0504-the-schannel-build-judges-the-pin-before-a-cacert-chain.md) | In the Schannel build with `--cacert` (manual chain validation), `ServerCertificateVerification.Judge` checks `--pinnedpubkey` before the chain, so a wrong pin with an unknown-revocation private CA is exit 90, not 60; the OpenSSL build and system-store validation keep verification first (measured curl 8.21.0, BL-2270) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0505](ADR-0505-the-gap-office-measures-linux-and-macos-in-github-actions-against-curl-built-with-openssl.md) | Every gap run measures Windows locally and Linux and macOS in `gap-platforms.yml` against curl built from the pinned release tarball with OpenSSL, through one measurement script; findings, tasks and scores stay per platform, a finding closes only on its own platform, and the headline is the lowest platform's score | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0506](ADR-0506-libcurl-and-curl-api-over-one-transfer-engine.md) | Two public .NET APIs, `Libcurl` (libcurl-faithful) and `Curl.Api` (.NET-normal), over one seam (`Curl.Api.UnitLibrary`: engine contracts and shared vocabulary) implemented by a dedicated `Curl.Engine.UnitLibrary` moved out of `Curl.Console`, which ends up consuming `Libcurl`; project split approved by Stewart, details decided by Claude (plan: `Documentation/Planning/Curl-Api-And-libcurl.md`) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0507](ADR-0507-libcurl-callbacks-are-typed-delegates.md) | libcurl's 22 `CURLOPT_*FUNCTION` callbacks are typed delegates in `Curl.Api.Callbacks` held by `EasyCallbackOptions` (all `null` by default): spans for buffers, `long` byte counts with upstream's magic constants, enums with upstream's numbers for closed result sets, an opaque `CurlSocket`, closures for `*DATA` (BL-2301) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0508](ADR-0508-easyhttpoptions-shapes-for-libcurl-http-options.md) | `EasyHttpOptions`: `FOLLOWLOCATION` is a `FollowLocation` enum, `POSTFIELDS`/`COPYPOSTFIELDS` share `PostFields` bytes, `COOKIEFILE` and `COOKIELIST` are ordered lists, `MIMEPOST`/`HTTPPOST` hold an `EasyMimeBody` data model, `STREAM_DEPENDS(_E)` is one `EasyStreamDependency` with an `object` parent (BL-2299) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0509](ADR-0509-engine-contracts-icurlengine-ieasytransfer-easyoptions.md) | The engine contracts in `Curl.Api.EngineContracts`: `EasyOptions` is one record of the nine option groups (`new EasyOptions()` is `curl_easy_reset`), `IEasyTransfer.Options` is settable and changed with `with`, `CurlPause` carries upstream's `CURLPAUSE_*` numbers, `CurlVersionInfo` lists features by name and defaults to libcurl 8.22.0 with nothing built in (BL-2312) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0510](ADR-0510-multi-contract-imultitransfer-and-multioptions.md) | The multi contract in `Curl.Api.EngineContracts.Multi`: `IMultiTransfer` with the socket-action API, `MultiOptions` (not `EasyMultiOptions`) with libcurl 8.22.0's `CURLMOPT_*` defaults, code-and-count tuples, `Memory<CurlWaitSocket>` extra waits, no `curl_multi_fdset` member, and `ICurlEngine.CreateMulti()` (BL-2313) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0511](ADR-0511-share-url-and-mime-contracts.md) | The share, URL and MIME contracts: `IShareHandle` with a `CurlShareCode Cleanup()` rather than a dispose, `ICurlUrlHandle`, `IMimeBuilder` and `IMimePartBuilder` building `EasyMimeBody`, `ICurlFormBuilder` with `CurlFormArgument` pairs; `CURLOPT_SHARE` becomes `IShareHandle?`; `ICurlEngine.CreateShare/Url/Mime/Form()` (BL-2314) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0512](ADR-0512-websocket-header-and-connect-only-contracts.md) | The WebSocket, header and connect-only contracts: `IEasyTransfer` gains `WebSocket`, `Headers` and `Connection`; code-and-count result record structs, `CurlWebSocketFrame` defaulting to zeros, `curl_ws_meta` as nullable `CurrentFrame`, `[Flags]` enums with `None`, `TryGet` returning `CurlHeaderCode` (BL-2315) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0513](ADR-0513-network-group-mapping-from-the-command-line.md) | Mapping the command line's network group onto `EasyNetworkOptions`: `ConnectTimeout` defaults to zero (libcurl's stored default), `--ip-tos`, `--vlan-priority` and `--mptcp` become members, `--ftp-ssl-control` outranks `--ssl-reqd`, `--proto` keeps `smb` (BL-2303) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0514](ADR-0514-tls-group-mapping-from-the-command-line.md) | Mapping the command line's TLS group onto `EasyTlsOptions`: `--ech`'s public name and config list become members, `-E` is split in the mapping, no minimum leaves `SslVersion.Default`, `--ssl-sessions` stays the tool's (BL-2304) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0515](ADR-0515-proxy-group-mapping-from-the-command-line.md) | Mapping the command line's proxy group onto `EasyProxyOptions`: the type is the last proxy option's kind, `--proxy-http2` makes an `-x` proxy `CURLPROXY_HTTPS2`, no `--socks5-basic`/`--socks5-gssapi` keeps libcurl's SOCKS5 methods, the proxy environment stays the engine's (BL-2305) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0516](ADR-0516-authentication-group-mapping-from-the-command-line.md) | Mapping the command line's authentication group onto `EasyAuthenticationOptions`: the methods are the sets the engine uses (every known scheme is `CURLAUTH_ANY`), credentials are `user:password`, no-function options keep libcurl's defaults, and the `--httpsig-*` options are Curl-specific members (BL-2306) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0517](ADR-0517-http2-interim-status-line-over-http1-off-windows.md) | An `HTTP/2 1xx` or `HTTP/3 1xx` status line over HTTP/1.x is that version's interim response off Windows, and a later head naming another major version fails with exit 8, `Version mismatch` (upstream test1704, BL-2326) | Superseded by ADR-0679 | 2026-10-15 |
| [0518](ADR-0518-sslkeylogfile-on-the-hand-built-tls-client.md) | `SSLKEYLOGFILE` runs on the hand-built TLS client, off Windows only: the engine reads it into `TlsClientOptions.KeyLogFile`, routing sends the connection to `HandBuiltTlsProvider`, TLS 1.2 logs `CLIENT_RANDOM` through `ITlsKeyLog` (BL-2327) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0519](ADR-0519-ssls-export-feature-off-windows.md) | `-V` lists `SSLS-EXPORT` on Linux and macOS, as the curl 8.22.0 OpenSSL reference does, and not on Windows, where `--ssl-sessions` is refused (amends ADR-0021, BL-2331) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0520](ADR-0520-features-line-omits-ech-on-every-platform.md) | `-V` lists `ECH` on no platform: the curl 8.22.0 OpenSSL reference, a default build, does not list it either; `--ech` still works (supersedes ADR-0448 for Linux and macOS, BL-2329, GF-0086) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0521](ADR-0521-features-line-omits-http3-on-every-platform.md) | `-V` lists `HTTP3` on no platform: the curl 8.22.0 OpenSSL reference has no QUIC library and does not list it; `--http3` still works (supersedes ADR-0451's Linux and macOS premise, BL-2330, GF-0087) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0522](ADR-0522-tls13-stand-in-where-sslstream-cannot-serve-it.md) | The HTTPS stand-in answers a TLS 1.3-only ClientHello with Curl's own TLS 1.3 server (`Tls13ServerStream`) where `SslStream` cannot serve TLS 1.3 (macOS), so test4001's `--ech` client sees ECH ignored and exits 101 (BL-2335) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0523](ADR-0523-protocols-line-omits-smb-on-every-platform.md) | `-V` lists `smb` and `smbs` on no platform: the curl 8.22.0 OpenSSL reference omits them as the Schannel one does; `smb://` still works (supersedes ADR-0453's Linux and macOS half, BL-2334, GF-0091) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0524](ADR-0524-unrewindable-stdin-resend-to-http10-fails-with-exit-65.md) | A chunked resend of `-T -` read through a pipe to an HTTP/1.0 server fails its rewind with exit 65 off Windows, as curl's `lseek` of stdin does; Windows and a seekable stdin keep exit 25 (`IRewindableUpload`, upstream test1072 and test1073, BL-2337) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0525](ADR-0525-chunked-stdin-upload-sends-last-chunk-with-last-data.md) | A chunked upload of unknown length (`-T -`) holds each chunk until the next read, so its last data chunk and `0\r\n\r\n` go out in one write as curl fills its upload buffer before sending; a server that answers on the data and closes still gets the closing chunk (upstream test60, BL-2341) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0526](ADR-0526-engine-network-factories-read-easynetworkoptions.md) | The engine's connection, DNS and timeout factories read `EasyNetworkOptions` beside `CommandLineOptions`; engine tests link `EasyOptionsFromCommandLine`; `--happy-eyeballs-timeout-ms 0` keeps libcurl's 200 ms as curl 8.22.0 does (BL-2323) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0527](ADR-0527-http-group-maps-onto-easyhttpoptions.md) | `EasyOptionsFromCommandLine.HttpOf` maps the HTTP command-line group onto `EasyHttpOptions`, which gains `SslEnableAlpn`; `-F` and the engine retarget are split to BL-2344 to BL-2346 (BL-2307) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0528](ADR-0528-behaviour-and-transfer-groups-map-onto-easy-records.md) | `EasyOptionsFromCommandLine.BehaviourOf` and `TransferOf` map the behaviour and transfer command-line groups onto `EasyBehaviourOptions` and `EasyTransferOptions`, `UploadTransferOf` adds a transfer's `-T` upload; the engine retarget is split to BL-2347 (BL-2308) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0529](ADR-0529-protocol-group-maps-onto-easyprotocoloptions.md) | `EasyOptionsFromCommandLine.ProtocolOf` maps the FTP, SSH, mail, TFTP and TELNET command-line options onto `EasyProtocolOptions`; RTSP and WebSocket keep libcurl's defaults and the default known-hosts file stays per transfer; the engine retarget is split to BL-2348 (BL-2309) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0530](ADR-0530-engine-tls-classes-read-easytlsoptions.md) | The engine's TLS classes (`TlsClientOptionsMapping`, `TlsBuildInformation`, the transport factories) read `EasyTlsOptions`; `-E` is split once, the certificate and key types are unset by default, and the `pkcs11:` `ENG` guess waits for BL-2349 (BL-2324) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0531](ADR-0531-form-parts-map-onto-easymimebody.md) | `HttpOf` maps the `-F` and `--form-string` parts onto `EasyHttpOptions.MimePost` (an `@file` part keeps its base name as `FileName`, `<file` clears it, `@-`/`<-` are the new `EasyMimeStandardInput`, text is UTF-8 bytes) and `MultipartFormPartMapping` builds the form from it (BL-2344) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0532](ADR-0532-engine-http-mappings-read-easyhttpoptions.md) | `HttpRequestOptionsMapping`, `HttpVersionMapping` (now over `Curl.Api`'s `HttpVersion`), `RedirectPolicyMapping` and `TransferContextFactory`'s HTTP fields read `EasyHttpOptions`; `Compressed` is a set `AcceptEncoding`, `Raw` both decodings off; the `EngineComposition` and `OptionGroupConnectionSettings` reads split off as BL-2350 (BL-2345) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0533](ADR-0533-engine-cookie-alt-svc-and-hsts-read-easyhttpoptions.md) | `CookieEngine`, `AltSvcTransferCache`, `HstsCacheFile` and `TransferPerformer`'s cookie, HSTS and alt-svc steps read `EasyHttpOptions`; `HttpOf` joins the `-b` strings as `cookie_setopts` does, no space before a string starting with a blank (BL-2346) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0534](ADR-0534-engine-transfer-settings-read-easybehaviouroptions-and-easytransferoptions.md) | `TransferContextFactory`, the `-m` and `-Y`/`-y` watchdogs, `TransferTimers`, `HttpRequestOptionsMapping` and `MailRequestOptionsMapping` read `EasyBehaviourOptions` and `EasyTransferOptions`; `TimeValue` is whole seconds, `-C -` with `-T` resumes from -1, zero limits are none (BL-2347) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0535](ADR-0535-engine-protocol-settings-read-easyprotocoloptions.md) | `TransferContextFactory`'s FTP, TFTP and TELNET fields, `MailRequestOptionsMapping`'s sender and recipients, `SshOptionsMapping` and `TransferPerformer`'s SSH step read `EasyProtocolOptions`; `-Q` is rejoined by prefix, a 512 block size is not given (BL-2348) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0536](ADR-0536-engine-proxy-settings-read-easyproxyoptions.md) | `TransferProxySelection`, `PreProxyOf`, `HaproxyProtocolOf`, `CreateProxyTunnelOptions`, `Socks5AuthenticationMapping`, `TransferPerformer`'s proxy selection and `TransferContextFactory`'s `-p` and `--suppress-connect-headers` read `EasyProxyOptions`; `Https2` stays HTTP and asks the tunnel for HTTP/2 (BL-2325) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0537](ADR-0537-pkcs11-certificate-and-key-type-eng.md) | A `pkcs11:` `-E`/`--key` (and proxy twin) without a type is `ENG`, as curl's tool sets it; the OpenSSL build then fails loading the pkcs11 provider, the Schannel build reports the missing file (BL-2349) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0538](ADR-0538-engine-transport-factories-read-engine-tool-options.md) | `EngineComposition`'s transport factories and `OptionGroupConnectionSettings.Of` read `EngineToolOptions` (`-Z`, `--parallel-immediate`, `--ssl-sessions`, `-m`) and the easy groups; the engine parses `--interface` with its own `EngineInterfaceBinding` (BL-2356) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0539](ADR-0539-engine-transfer-setup-reads-engine-transfer-settings.md) | `TransferPerformer`'s setup steps, `TransferContextFactory`, `RedirectPolicyMapping`, `HttpRequestOptionsMapping.Of` and `MailRequestOptionsMapping.FromCommandLine` read an engine-owned `EngineTransferSettings` and the easy groups, not `CommandLineOptions` (BL-2357) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0540](ADR-0540-http3-options-refused-on-every-reference-build.md) | `--http3` and `--http3-only` are refused with exit 2 on every platform, as every reference build (Schannel, OpenSSL without a QUIC library) refuses them, unless the parse reads as a QUIC build, which only tests and the conformance harness ask for; supersedes ADR-0521 decision 2 (BL-2332) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0541](ADR-0541-managed-engine-easy-transfer.md) | `ManagedCurlEngine` and `ManagedEasyTransfer` run one transfer from `EasyOptions` over engine-owned caches, body and headers to the callbacks, the tool-only settings at libcurl defaults, phase 3 members throwing `NotSupportedException` (BL-2316) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0542](ADR-0542-tool-settings-and-engine-wide-state-reach-the-easy-handle.md) | The curl tool's settings reach `ManagedEasyTransfer` through the easy options where libcurl has a `CURLOPT_*` and through the internal `ToolSettings` hook where it has none; uploads through the read callback; cookies, HSTS, pooled connections and `%{xfer_id}` shared across an engine's handles (BL-2359) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0543](ADR-0543-engine-trace-and-progress-reach-the-debug-and-xferinfo-callbacks.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` hands every transfer event to the debug callback under `CURLOPT_VERBOSE` with libcurl's info types and `-v`'s wording, and its progress to the xferinfo or progress callback unless `CURLOPT_NOPROGRESS`, whose non-zero return aborts with `CurlCode.AbortedByCallback`; `DebugCallback` keeps libcurl's signature (BL-2360) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0544](ADR-0544-easy-handle-host-for-cookie-alt-svc-and-hsts-files.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` loads the `-b` files, reads and writes the `--alt-svc` and `--hsts` files and writes the `-c` jar through an internal `EasyTransferHost` (`PhysicalFileSystem` by default), and numbers `%{conn_id}` with `EngineConnectionIdTransferEvents` (BL-2362) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0545](ADR-0545-easy-handle-trace-components-wrap-the-debug-events.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` adds the `--trace-config read`, `write` and `timer` lines to its debug events through an internal `TraceComponents` hook, wrapping them in the runner's decorators in the runner's order; the `[MULTI]` lines stay the tool's (BL-2363) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0546](ADR-0546-chunked-stdin-upload-after-continue-wait-sends-last-chunk-with-last-data.md) | A chunked upload of unknown length (`-T -`) sent after the wait for `100 Continue` also holds each chunk until the next read and sends its last data chunk and `0\r\n\r\n` in one `SendUnlessStoppedAsync`, keeping the 300-or-above stop; extends ADR-0525 to upstream test60 on macOS (BL-2371) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0547](ADR-0547-easy-handle-info-holds-live-transfer-and-connection-ids.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer.Info` holds the running transfer's `TransferId` from its start and its `ConnectionId` (-1 before) from the moment it opens or reuses a connection, so the debug callback reads the `--trace-ids` IDs as curl's tool reads `CURLINFO_XFER_ID` and `CURLINFO_CONN_ID` (BL-2364) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0548](ADR-0548-curl-mcp-an-mcp-server-over-the-api-seam-on-microsofts-sdk.md) | `Curl.Mcp` is an MCP server over the `Curl.Api.UnitLibrary` seam, built on Microsoft's C# MCP SDK (`ModelContextProtocol`, `Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting`, approved by Stewart) and packed as a NuGet MCP server: a thin host, its tool logic in `Curl.Mcp.UnitLibrary`, the tools `curl`, `transfer`, `parse_url`, `build_url` and `version`, and every file path confined to `CURL_MCP_ROOT` | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0549](ADR-0549-easy-handle-info-build-constants-per-platform-and-no-sockets.md) | `TransferInfo`'s `TlsSslBackend`, `CaInfo` and `CaPath` default to the platform's curl build (Schannel 8 with no CA paths on Windows; OpenSSL 1 with `/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt` and `/etc/ssl/certs` on Linux, `/etc/ssl/cert.pem` on macOS); `SslEngines` stays empty, `LastSocket` and `ActiveSocket` answer -1 and `EarlyDataSent` 0 (BL-2369) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0550](ADR-0550-curlinfo-os-errno-from-the-last-failed-connect-attempt.md) | `CURLINFO_OS_ERRNO` is the last failed connect attempt's `SocketError` carried on `ConnectResult.LastSocketError` and turned into the platform's number by `SocketException.NativeErrorCode` (10061 on Windows, 111 on Linux, 61 on macOS for a refused connect) (BL-2374) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0551](ADR-0551-curlinfo-proxy-error-from-socks-handshake-failures-and-no-proxy-error-variable.md) | `CURLINFO_PROXY_ERROR` is the `CURLPX_*` code upstream's `socks.c` gives a failed SOCKS handshake, carried on `ConnectResult.ProxyError` and `TransferReport.ProxyError` as `CurlProxyCode`; `-w` gets no `%{proxy_error}`, which curl 8.22.0 does not have (BL-2375) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0552](ADR-0552-curl-console-serial-attempts-perform-through-an-easy-handle-with-a-tool-attempt.md) | `Curl.Console`'s serial transfers take one `ICurlEngine` easy handle each and perform every attempt, `--retry` included, through `PerformAsync`, carrying the runner's prepared attempt as the internal `ToolAttempt`; `-Z` stays direct until phase 3, and building the attempt from the options and callbacks is BL-2394 (BL-2318) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0553](ADR-0553-write-out-reads-libcurl-variables-from-transferinfo.md) | `-w` reads every libcurl variable from a `TransferInfo`, built once by `Curl.Output`'s `WriteOutTransferInfo.FromReport`, which `Curl.Engine`'s `TransferInfoFromReport` now starts from; the tool's own variables stay with the result and report, and the bytes are unchanged (BL-2319) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0554](ADR-0554-easy-handle-keeps-last-result-and-takes-the-runners-chosen-transfer-pieces.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` keeps its last perform's whole `TransferResult` as the internal `LastResult` for the runner's `--retry`, failure lines and `-w`, and takes the runner's already-chosen proxy, credentials, SSH options, `If-None-Match` lines, range, offset, form body, upload and alt-svc cache as the internal `ToolInputs`, which also leaves the cookie, alt-svc and HSTS files to the runner (BL-2395) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0555](ADR-0555-mcp-tool-errors-text-or-base64-and-the-response-cap.md) | MCP tool errors carry curl's trimmed stderr message and `curl exit code <n> (<CurlCode name>)`; output is text when valid UTF-8, else base64, capped in raw bytes (`CURL_MCP_MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES`, unset or empty means 1 MiB, anything but plain positive digits fails the start) at a character boundary with the cut stated; a cancelled request is its own error with no output (BL-2380) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0556](ADR-0556-libcurl-global-init-returns-a-curllibrary-over-an-injected-engine.md) | `CurlGlobal.Init(ICurlEngine)` returns a `CurlLibrary` that counts inits, holds the engine and disposes it on cleanup (a second cleanup does nothing); its internal `Create*` methods delegate to the engine and each handle area makes its own public creator in a `partial` part; `InitMemory` checks and ignores its callbacks; `SslSet` and `Trace` answer as the Schannel build (OpenSSL off Windows) (BL-2456) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0557](ADR-0557-mcp-path-confinement-resolves-twice-and-measures-the-case-rule.md) | `McpConfinementRoot` resolves a path both as written and step by step through every symbolic link and allows it only when both lie inside `CURL_MCP_ROOT`; the case rule is measured at the root, a loop or invalid path is `Unresolvable`, and an unset root refuses every path (BL-2377) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0558](ADR-0558-easy-handle-takes-the-runners-body-and-header-outputs.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` takes the runner's body stream chain, `-D` stream, `-J` header watcher and `--styled-output` styles as the internal `ToolOutputs`, passed to `TransferContextFactory.Create` in place of the write and header callbacks; a short write callback fails with `OutputWriteFailedException`, `passed N returned M` (BL-2396) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0559](ADR-0559-mcp-classifies-every-option-by-how-it-uses-a-local-path.md) | `McpOptionPathTable` maps every `CommandLineOptionTable` long name to an `McpOptionPathUse` (`None`, `ReadsFile`, `WritesFile`, `WritesDirectory`, `ReadsOrWritesFile`, `ReadsFileOrLiteral`, `ValueEmbedded`, `UnixSocketPath`) from the curl 8.22.0 manpage; an unknown option is refused and a drift test names any unclassified row (BL-2378) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0560](ADR-0560-libcurl-options-table-and-setoption-dispatch.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlOption` and `CurlOptions` (`by_name` ASCII case-insensitive, `by_id` skipping aliases, `next`) are generated from curl 8.22.0's 327-row `easyoptions.c`; the internal `EasyOptionDispatch.SetOption` answers `UnknownOption`, `BadFunctionArgument` for a wrong .NET value type, `NotBuiltIn`, or the setter's answer, and each option group adds its setters in an `EasyOptionSetters` partial part in its own folder (BL-2399) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0561](ADR-0561-easy-handle-takes-the-runners-events-through-an-internal-hook.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` takes the runner's `-v` and `--trace` events and the running transfer they keep as the internal `ToolEvents`, reported to in place of the debug callback and the trace-component decorators, with `%{conn_id}` numbered once by the runner's recorder; the debug callback cannot carry the structured events (BL-2397) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0562](ADR-0562-libcurl-error-text-tables-and-the-header-api-text.md) | `CurlErrorText.Multi`, `Share`, `Url` and `Header` answer upstream's strerror strings (test1538) from tables with upstream's fallbacks; the header API, which has no upstream strerror, answers its `CURLHE_*` comments and `CURLHcode unknown` (BL-2459) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0563](ADR-0563-libcurl-curleasy-perform-and-cleanup.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlEasy` (from `CurlLibrary.EasyInit`) sets options through `EasyOptionDispatch` onto the engine transfer's `Options`, writing back only on `Ok`; `Perform` blocks on `PerformAsync`, the one sanctioned bridge; `Dispose` and `DisposeAsync` dispose the transfer once, and a member used afterwards throws `ObjectDisposedException` (BL-2408) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0564](ADR-0564-mcp-checks-a-whole-argument-array-before-curl-runs.md) | `McpArgumentChecker` reads a whole curl argument array as `Curl.Cli`'s parser does from `CommandLineOptionTable.Rows` (the library now references `Curl.Cli.UnitLibrary`), finds paths embedded in values and `file:` URLs, reads `-K` files inside the root at most 8 deep, and refuses the first outside or uncheckable path naming the option (BL-2379) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0565](ADR-0565-libcurl-http-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s HTTP option setters (`EasyHttpOptionSetters`, one named static method per row) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does; `POSTFIELDS` keeps the caller's `byte[]` by reference and `COPYPOSTFIELDS` copies it, cut to `POSTFIELDSIZE` (BL-2406) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0566](ADR-0566-libcurl-getinfo-dispatch-and-string-list-infos.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlEasy.GetInfo` (`curl_easy_getinfo`) answers one `out object?` through `EasyInfoDispatch`: `UnknownOption` for an id `curl.h` lacks, `NotBuiltIn` for one no group reads; groups add named readers in `EasyInfoReaders` parts; strings, lists, `PRIVATE` and `TLS_SSL_PTR` (backend, no native pointer) first (BL-2412) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0567](ADR-0567-easy-handle-takes-the-runners-progress-and-starts-its-watchdogs.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer` takes the runner's progress meter and `--fail-early` token as the internal `ToolProgress`, reported to in place of the xferinfo and progress callbacks, and while it is set starts fresh `-Y`/`-y` and `-m` watchdogs on the running transfer for every perform; the xferinfo callback's four counts cannot carry the meter's start, retry and done reports (BL-2398) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0568](ADR-0568-libcurl-network-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s network option setters (`EasyNetworkOptionSetters`) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does; `PROTOCOLS_STR` needs one known scheme or `all`, the `PROTOCOLS` bitmasks become the same names, times are cut to `INT_MAX`; `CURLU` waits for BL-2462 (BL-2402) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0569](ADR-0569-mcp-curl-tool-runs-through-an-injected-runner.md) | The MCP `curl` tool (`McpCurlTool`) checks its arguments with `McpArgumentChecker`, then runs them unchanged through the injected `ICurlCommandRunner` with the root as working directory, stdin empty unless given, and stdout and stderr captured in `McpCappedCaptureStream` (cap plus one byte kept, every byte counted); it returns `McpCurlToolResult` with both outputs, the exit code and a tool error for nonzero exits (BL-2381) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0570](ADR-0570-libcurl-curlmulti-core.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlMulti` (partial, namespace `Libcurl.Multi`) adds and removes `CurlEasy` handles over `IMultiTransfer`, maps engine transfers back so `InfoRead` answers a `CurlMessage` naming the added handle, and answers `BadEasyHandle` and `AddedAlready` itself where it knows (BL-2429) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0571](ADR-0571-libcurl-protocol-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s protocol option setters (`EasyProtocolOptionSetters`, one named static method per row) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does: the FTP enum options and a non-32-character `SSH_HOST_PUBLIC_KEY_MD5` are `BadFunctionArgument`; `TFTP_BLKSIZE` is clamped, an unknown `RTSP_REQUEST` is `None`, `WS_OPTIONS` keeps its two bits (BL-2407) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0572](ADR-0572-libcurl-transfer-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s transfer option setters (`EasyTransferOptionSetters`) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does; each `_LARGE` or `_MS` alias sets its option's field, times are cut to `INT_MAX`, buffer sizes are clamped, never refused (BL-2401) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0573](ADR-0573-write-read-and-header-callbacks-pause-abort-and-data-pointers.md) | A write or header callback's `CURL_WRITEFUNC_PAUSE` and a read callback's `CURL_READFUNC_PAUSE` wait on the handle's `EasyPauseGate` until `Pause` clears the direction, then get the same data again (a `file` URL or the synchronous path answers libcurl's pause-not-supported error); `CURL_READFUNC_ABORT` is `AbortedByCallback`; `Libcurl` sets the three functions and their `*DATA` streams, with one partial method per callback area (BL-2415) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0574](ADR-0574-libcurl-authentication-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s authentication option setters (`EasyAuthenticationOptionSetters`) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does: a `CURLAUTH_*` bit beyond the 32 `HttpAuthenticationMethods` carries is `BadFunctionArgument`, `CURLAUTH_ONLY` alone `NotBuiltIn`; `NETRC` outside 0..2 and a `TLSAUTH_TYPE` other than `SRP` are `BadFunctionArgument`; `AWS_SIGV4` turns on SigV4 authentication (BL-2404) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0575](ADR-0575-curl-mcp-runs-curl-in-the-root-as-current-directory.md) | `Curl.Mcp`'s host reads `CURL_MCP_ROOT` and the response cap once and makes the root the process's current directory before serving; `InProcessCurlCommandRunner` runs `InProcessCurl` over the production `TcpConnector` and refuses any other working directory; `CurlCommandTool` returns the result as source-generated JSON text with `IsError` set (BL-2382) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0576](ADR-0576-libcurl-proxy-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s proxy option setters (`EasyProxyOptionSetters`) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does: `PROXYPORT` and `PROXYTYPE` out of range are `BadFunctionArgument`, an unknown `SOCKS5_AUTH` bit `NotBuiltIn`; `HEADEROPT` keeps its separate bit, `HAPROXY_CLIENT_IP` turns the HAProxy protocol on, and `PROXYHEADER` is copied at set time (BL-2405) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0577](ADR-0577-progress-xferinfo-and-debug-callback-setters.md) | `Libcurl` sets `CURLOPT_PROGRESSFUNCTION`, `XFERINFOFUNCTION` and `DEBUGFUNCTION` (own delegate type or `null`, else `BadFunctionArgument`) over the engine's existing calls (ADR-0543); `XFERINFODATA`/`PROGRESSDATA` and `DEBUGDATA` take any value and change nothing (BL-2416) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0578](ADR-0578-libcurl-curl-getdate-and-curlslist.md) | `Libcurl`'s `curl_getdate` is `CurlDate.Parse` over `CurlDateParser`, -1 for text that is not a date; `curl_slist_append` and `_free_all` are `CurlSlist.Append` and `FreeAll` on an `IReadOnlyList<string>`, and every list-valued option copies the list when it is set (BL-2448) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0579](ADR-0579-libcurl-global-init-builds-the-managed-engine.md) | `CurlGlobal.Init(flags)`, the parameterless `curl_global_init`, builds a `ManagedCurlEngine`, so `Libcurl` references `Curl.Engine.UnitLibrary`; the engine-taking overloads stay for tests (BL-2457) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0580](ADR-0580-libcurl-tls-option-setters.md) | `Libcurl`'s TLS option setters (`TlsPeerOptionSetters` twice, for the origin and the `PROXY_*` twins, and `EasyTlsOptionSetters`) check values as curl 8.22.0's `setopt.c` does: `SSLVERSION` refuses SSL 2/3 and unknown versions, `CA_CACHE_TIMEOUT` below -1; backend-gated options are all stored; `SSLENGINE` with a name, `SSLENGINE_DEFAULT` and `SSL_FALSESTART` answer `NotBuiltIn` (BL-2403) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0581](ADR-0581-libcurl-curlshare-and-curlopt-share.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlShare` is `curl_share_init/_setopt/_cleanup` over the engine's `IShareHandle`: unshareable kinds, unknown options and wrong-typed values answer `BadOption` without the engine, a cleaned-up handle `Invalid`; `CURLOPT_SHARE` stores a `CurlShare`'s engine handle, and a cleaned-up one or `null` detaches (BL-2433) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0582](ADR-0582-libcurl-multi-setopt-and-settings-calls.md) | `CurlMulti.SetOption` sets every `CURLMOPT_*` with `multi.c`'s checks (counts below 0 `BadFunctionArgument`, `MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` outside 1 to `INT_MAX` 100, the `*DATA` and the five options ignored since 7.62.0 taken and ignored); `GetHandles`, `WaitFds`, `GetInfo` (`curl_multi_get_offt`, `UnknownOption` and -1 for a count `multi.h` lacks) and `NotifyEnable`/`Disable` go to the engine (BL-2432) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0583](ADR-0583-libcurl-curlmime-and-curlmimepart.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlMime` and `CurlMimePart` wrap the engine's `IMimeBuilder` one call per `curl_mime_*` setter, `curl_mime_free` is `Dispose` (use afterwards throws), and `CURLOPT_MIMEPOST` stores the handle's body as it is when set (BL-2441) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0584](ADR-0584-libcurl-curlmulti-wait-poll-wakeup-fdset-and-timeout.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlMulti` has `WaitAsync`, `PollAsync`, `Wakeup`, `Timeout` and `FdSet` over the engine's `IMultiTransfer`: a negative timeout answers `BadFunctionArgument` without the engine, and `FdSet` fills `ISet<CurlSocket>` read, write and exception sets from the engine's wait sockets (BL-2430) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0585](ADR-0585-libcurl-websocket-api-on-curleasy.md) | `Libcurl`'s WebSocket API is `CurlEasy` extension methods in `Libcurl/WebSocket` over the engine's `IEasyWebSocket`: `WebSocketRecv`, `WebSocketSend`, `WebSocketMeta` and `WebSocketStartFrame`, blocking forms over `Async` ones with libcurl's out parameters; a `null` or cleaned-up handle answers `BadFunctionArgument` (`Meta` `null`) without the engine (BL-2445) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0586](ADR-0586-libcurl-curlform-and-curlopt-httppost.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlForm` (`[Obsolete]`, as upstream deprecates the form API) is `curl_formadd/_formget/_formfree` over the engine's `ICurlFormBuilder`: every `CURLFORM_*` argument passed as given, a `null` form started and set only on `Ok`; `CURLOPT_HTTPPOST` stores the form's body as it is when set (BL-2442) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0587](ADR-0587-libcurl-curlurlhandle.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlUrlHandle` (`CurlLibrary.UrlInit`, `curl_url`) passes `curl_url_get` and `_set` through to the engine's `ICurlUrlHandle`, wraps `curl_url_dup`'s copy in a new handle, throws `ObjectDisposedException` after `curl_url_cleanup`, and its `ErrorText` is `curl_url_strerror` (BL-2438) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0588](ADR-0588-libcurl-curlmulti-socket-action-and-callbacks.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlMulti.SocketActionAsync` and `Assign` pass straight to the engine; its own `CurlMultiSocketCallback` (given the added `CurlEasy`) and `CurlMultiTimerCallback` (given the `CurlMulti`) are wrapped into the seam's delegates by internal setters that BL-2432's `SetOption` calls (BL-2431) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0589](ADR-0589-curl-mcp-serves-ai-help-as-resources.md) | `Curl.Mcp` serves `--ai-help` as resources `curl://ai-help`, `curl://ai-help/all` and one per category, listed from `CurlHelpText.Categories` by `McpAiHelpResourceCatalogue` through plain list and read handlers; URIs match exactly and an unknown one is `ResourceNotFound` (BL-2387) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0590](ADR-0590-libcurl-curlopt-curlu-holds-the-engine-url-handle.md) | `CURLOPT_CURLU` stores a `CurlUrlHandle`'s engine `ICurlUrlHandle` by reference as `EasyNetworkOptions.UrlHandle`, replacing the string `UrlFromUrlHandle`, so the engine reads the URL at perform time; `null` clears it, any other object answers `BadFunctionArgument` (BL-2462) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0591](ADR-0591-libcurl-curl-version-and-curl-version-info.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlVersion.Text` (`curl_version`, `libcurl/<version>` and the TLS backend, the tail of `curl -V`'s first line) and `CurlVersion.Info` (`curl_version_info`, ignoring the age as libcurl does and answering every `CURLVERSION_NOW` field) read the engine's `VersionInfo`; the `CURL_VERSION_*` bits come from `lib/version.c`'s name table, and library versions the seam lacks are `null` or 0 (BL-2458) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0592](ADR-0592-upload-rewinder-seam.md) | An upload that cannot seek rewinds through `Curl.Protocol.Abstractions`' `IUploadRewinder` (`HasBeenRead`, `RewindToStart`) on a followed redirect, an authentication retry, a 417 resend and a refused-stream retry, only once read; a failed rewind ends with exit 65 and the rewinder's message (BL-2463) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0593](ADR-0593-curl-mcp-transfer-tool-logic.md) | `Curl.Mcp`'s `transfer` tool checks its request first (`McpTransferRequestCheck`, naming the bad field; `file:` URLs refused), sets only the options its fields name as `-X`, `-d`, `-I`, `-H`, `-L`, `-k`, `-x` and `-A` would, runs one easy handle and returns the last response's status, headers, capped body and `McpTransferInfo`; a failing `CurlCode` is a tool error with the handle's `ErrorText` (BL-2383) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0594](ADR-0594-engine-managed-multi-event-loop.md) | The managed engine's `ManagedMultiTransfer` runs each added transfer as its own `PerformAsync`, started by the next multi perform; `PerformAsync` never waits and queues one `CURLMSG_DONE` per finished transfer in add order; a transfer is in one of the engine's multi handles at a time; `Remove` cancels a running perform and drops its unread message; cleanup cancels and awaits them all (BL-2424) | Accepted | 2026-10-15 |
| [0595](ADR-0595-libcurl-curlstrings-escape-getenv-strequal.md) | `Libcurl`'s `CurlStrings` is `curl_easy_escape/_unescape` (and the `[Obsolete]` `curl_escape/_unescape`), `curl_getenv`, `curl_strequal` and `curl_strnequal` over UTF-8 bytes: `null`, a negative length or one past the end answers `null`, empty input empty, `%00` a NUL byte, and an unset or empty variable `null` (BL-2447) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0596](ADR-0596-curl-mcp-nuget-mcp-server-packing.md) | `Curl.Mcp` packs as a .NET tool of `PackageType` `McpServer`: a pointer package and one self-contained package per runtime (win, linux, osx; x64 and arm64), version 0.1.0 held equal to `.mcp/server.json`, whose registry entry requires `CURL_MCP_ROOT`; `Curl.Console` is referenced runtime-agnostic (BL-2388) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0597](ADR-0597-engine-multi-timeout-wait-poll-wakeup.md) | The managed engines multi handle answers `curl_multi_timeout` as 0 while a perform has work, else the first running transfers `CURLOPT_TIMEOUT` left on the engines `TimeProvider`, else -1; `WaitAsync` and `PollAsync` end when a perform completes, an extra socket (checked every 10 ms through `IWaitSocketPoller`) has an event, or at the timeout capped by the handles; wait with nothing to wait on returns at once, poll waits the timeout and a sticky `Wakeup` ends it (BL-2425) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0598](ADR-0598-curl-mcp-native-aot-fails-keeps-self-contained.md) | `Curl.Mcp` stays self-contained: `dotnet publish Curl.Mcp -r win-x64 -p:PublishAot=true` builds clean (30.2 MB) but the server stops at start with `NotSupportedException` for `McpTransferHeader[]` from ModelContextProtocol 2.2.0's parameter marshaller, which ignores our source-generated options; retry on an SDK upgrade (BL-2389) | Superseded by 0621 | 2026-10-16 |
| [0599](ADR-0599-engine-multi-socket-action-and-callbacks.md) | The managed engines multi handle gives each running transfer an engine-numbered socket (from `0x40000000`) through `CURLMOPT_SOCKETFUNCTION` (`CURL_POLL_IN`, then `CURL_POLL_REMOVE` with the `curl_multi_assign` value), drives progress through `CURLMOPT_TIMERFUNCTION` at most 10 ms apart while transfers run, moves every transfer on from both `curl_multi_socket_action` and `curl_multi_perform`, and fails every transfer with `CURLE_ABORTED_BY_CALLBACK` once a callback returns -1 (BL-2426) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0600](ADR-0600-mcp-reads-write-out-template-files-for-output.md) | `McpArgumentChecker` reads a `-w @file` template inside `CURL_MCP_ROOT` and checks its `%output{}` files as an inline template's; an unreadable template and `-w @-` are refused; supersedes ADR-0564 decision 7 (BL-2460) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0601](ADR-0601-engine-url-handle-get.md) | The managed engine's `ICurlUrlHandle` (`ManagedCurlUrlHandle`) holds upstream's parts, each nullable, parsed by `CurlUrl`; the written port is recovered from the text without leading zeros and a scheme counts as guessed when the text does not start with it; `curl_url_get` answers every part and every get flag as curl 8.22.0 does, `file` URLs as `file://` and the path, query and fragment; `Set` parses a whole URL only until BL-2437 (BL-2436) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0602](ADR-0602-engine-url-handle-set-dup-and-clear.md) | The managed engine's `ICurlUrlHandle` sets every part as curl 8.22.0's `curl_url_set` does (scheme, port and host checked with upstream's codes, `CURLU_URLENCODE`, `APPENDQUERY`, `NON_SUPPORT_SCHEME`, `NO_AUTHORITY`, `ALLOW_SPACE`), resolves a relative URL against the one held as `redirect_url` does, clears a part given null and duplicates over the same immutable parts (BL-2437) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0603](ADR-0603-header-api-over-the-engines-header-output.md) | The managed engine records every header line an HTTP transfer writes (`HeaderRecordingStream` into `TransferHeaderStore`), numbering requests from the status lines and tagging CONNECT, 1xx, trailer and HTTP/2 `:status` pseudo headers; `ITransferHeaders` answers `curl_easy_header` and `curl_easy_nextheader` with upstream's `CURLHE_*` checks, and `Libcurl`'s `CurlEasyHeaders` passes through (BL-2446) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0604](ADR-0604-engine-multi-handles-waitfds-counts-notifications-network-change.md) | The engine multi lists its transfers in add order, reports one `POLLIN` socket per started transfer numbered as the socket callback is told, counts `CURLMINFO_*` as curl 8.22.0 (pending 0), sends `INFO_READ` and `EASY_DONE` notifications at the end of each perform, and treats `CURLMOPT_NETWORK_CHANGED` as an action that closes idle connections and empties the DNS cache (BL-2427) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0605](ADR-0605-engine-multi-connection-limits-multiplexing-and-push.md) | The engine multi holds a transfer pending while starting it would pass `CURLMOPT_MAX_HOST_CONNECTIONS` or `MAX_TOTAL_CONNECTIONS`, lets `https` and prior-knowledge HTTP/2 transfers to one origin share a connection `MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` at a time under `CURLPIPE_MULTIPLEX`, and answers a server push through `CURLMOPT_PUSHFUNCTION` (`OK` adds, `ERROROUT` fails the parent with `CURLE_HTTP2`); `MAXCONNECTS` waits on BL-2468 and push wiring on BL-2469 (BL-2428) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0606](ADR-0606-engine-share-handle.md) | The engine's share handle owns its own cookie, DNS, TLS session, connection and HSTS caches, which a transfer attached through `CURLOPT_SHARE` uses in place of its engine's for each kind shared; `PSL` is accepted and shares nothing, options answer `CURLSHE_IN_USE` while a transfer is attached, and the lock callbacks run as synchronous pairs around attach, detach, cleanup, each cache taken and each shared cookie access (BL-2434) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0607](ADR-0607-ws-upgrade-only-and-public-frame-codec.md) | `Curl.Protocol.Ws` gives libcurl's WebSocket API an upgrade-only connect (`ConnectAndUpgradeOnlyAsync`, same request, `-v` lines and refusals as the tool, then the open connection and the bytes after the head), a public masked frame encoder with any opcode and FIN bit, and a public incremental decoder reporting each payload run's opcode, FIN, offset and bytes left with the tool's own violation messages (BL-2471) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0608](ADR-0608-connect-only-recv-and-send.md) | A `CURLOPT_CONNECT_ONLY` 1 perform of the managed engine connects to the URL's host and port (TLS for TLS schemes) through its own `TcpConnector`, sends nothing and keeps the connection until the next perform or cleanup; `curl_easy_recv` and `curl_easy_send` never block, answering `CURLE_AGAIN` while a read or write runs and `CURLE_UNSUPPORTED_PROTOCOL` with no connect-only connection; proxies wait on BL-2472 (BL-2423) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0609](ADR-0609-seek-callback-rewinds-a-read-callback-upload.md) | `CURLOPT_SEEKFUNCTION` makes the engine's read-callback upload an `IUploadRewinder` that calls the seek callback with `(0, SEEK_SET)` once bytes were read, any result but `CURL_SEEKFUNC_OK` failing with exit 65 and `seek callback returned error <n>`; without a seek callback the upload is unchanged (BL-2417) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0610](ADR-0610-socket-callbacks-over-opaque-connection-handles.md) | The managed engine calls `CURLOPT_OPENSOCKETFUNCTION`, `SOCKOPTFUNCTION` and `CLOSESOCKETFUNCTION` around each TCP connection it opens, beneath the pool, over opaque `CurlSocket` handles (the open-socket callback's, else the engine's own numbers): `CURL_SOCKET_BAD` fails with exit 7, `CURL_SOCKOPT_ERROR` with exit 42, close-socket runs once when the connection closes; QUIC passes through (BL-2418) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0611](ADR-0611-prerequest-and-resolver-start-callbacks-in-the-engine.md) | The managed engine calls `CURLOPT_PREREQFUNCTION` each time a request is given its connection (a `PrerequestConnector` over the pool, abort exit 42) and `CURLOPT_RESOLVER_START_FUNCTION` before each look-up its DNS cache misses (a `ResolverStartDnsResolver` under the connectors, abort exit 6); the trailer callback is split off to BL-2475 (BL-2419) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0612](ADR-0612-rtsp-interleaved-frames-go-to-an-injected-sink.md) | RTSP hands each interleaved `$` RTP frame whole to an optional `IRtspInterleaveSink` injected into `RtspProtocolHandler`, every channel, before a reply and during `RECEIVE`; a short take fails with 23; with no sink frames before a reply are skipped (measured) and a `RECEIVE` writes them to the body output (BL-2477) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0613](ADR-0613-ftp-list-parser-and-wildcard-matcher-port-curls-own.md) | `FtpFileListParser` ports `lib/ftplistparser.c` (Unix and DOS `LIST` formats) byte for byte into `FtpFileListEntry` values, texts as Latin-1 cut at the first NUL, hard link limit by platform, malformed exit 87 and an entry past 10,000 bytes exit 27; `FtpWildcardPattern` ports curl's own `Curl_fnmatch` (BL-2476), and its BL-2480 addendum matches as glibc's `fnmatch` on Linux and macOS libc's on macOS, pinned on `unit1307.c`'s `LINUX_*` and `MAC_*` rows | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0614](ADR-0614-easy-handle-takes-the-runners-follower-and-context-factory.md) | `ManagedEasyTransfer.ToolDispatch` (`EngineToolTransferDispatch`: the runner's `RedirectFollower` and `TransferContextFactory`) makes a perform from the options run through the curl tool's option-group dispatch and context factory, not copied by `Duplicate`, cleared by `Reset`; `LastContext` hands back the context for the runner's URL reads (BL-2479) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0615](ADR-0615-ftp-wildcard-download-follows-wc-statemach.md) | The FTP wildcard download follows curl 8.22.0's `wc_statemach`: hooks are `IFtpWildcardHooks` on `FtpProtocolHandler.WildcardHooks`; malformed listing 87/27 and failing chunk-begin 88 with no `QUIT`, no match 78 after `QUIT`; a failing chunk-end hook is ignored and a failing match hook leaves the entry out, as upstream does; no `CWD` or `SIZE` per file (BL-2478) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
