| [0616](ADR-0616-engine-websocket-api-over-a-connect-only-upgrade.md) | A `CURLOPT_CONNECT_ONLY` 2 perform of a `ws`/`wss` URL upgrades through `WsProtocolHandler.ConnectAndUpgradeOnlyAsync` over the handle's own connector and keeps the connection in `ManagedEasyWebSocket`: non-blocking `curl_ws_recv` with `lib/ws.c`'s flags, offset and bytes left, auto-pong unless `CURLWS_NOAUTOPONG`, `curl_ws_send` choosing the opcode as `ws_frame_flags2firstbyte`, `curl_ws_meta` the last frame received; `CURLWS_OFFSET` and `curl_ws_start_frame` left to BL-2482 (BL-2443) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0617](ADR-0617-share-handle-psl-and-hsts.md) | The share handle's public suffix list is the one embedded snapshot every transfer already uses, so it is shared whatever the handle says; while `CURL_LOCK_DATA_PSL` is shared an attached transfer takes it under the `Psl` lock as it attaches; an HSTS entry one engine's handle learns switches another's `http` URL while HSTS is shared (BL-2435) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0618](ADR-0618-engine-form-builder-follows-formdata-c.md) | The engine's `curl_formadd` reads each call as curl 8.22.0's `FormAdd` (nodes per extra file, its error codes and type guessing), `Build` makes `Curl_getformdata`'s parts (a stream part has no data until BL-2482), and `curl_formget` hands over the top part's `Content-Type` line and body in 8192-byte pieces, blocking on the async build (BL-2440) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0619](ADR-0619-chunk-fnmatch-and-interleave-callbacks-adapt-the-ftp-and-rtsp-hooks.md) | `CallbackWildcardHooks` adapts `CURLOPT_FNMATCH_FUNCTION`, `CHUNK_BGN_FUNCTION` and `CHUNK_END_FUNCTION` to the FTP handler's wildcard hooks under `CURLOPT_WILDCARDMATCH` only, each unset one falling back to libcurl's default, and `CallbackInterleaveSink` hands RTSP's interleaved frames to `CURLOPT_INTERLEAVEFUNCTION`; Libcurl sets the four and takes any `*DATA` value (BL-2420) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0620](ADR-0620-hsts-read-and-write-callbacks-go-through-the-cache-file-text.md) | `CallbackHstsCache` asks `CURLOPT_HSTSREADFUNCTION` for entries before each perform and hands `CURLOPT_HSTSWRITEFUNCTION` every entry at cleanup, while `HSTS_CTRL` enables HSTS or `CURLOPT_HSTS` is set, both through the cache's file text; Libcurl sets the two and takes any `*DATA` value (BL-2421) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0621](ADR-0621-curl-mcp-runs-native-aot-once-its-json-options-are-built-on-first-use.md) | `Curl.Mcp` runs as native AOT: ADR-0598's failure was `CurlMcpJsonContext.ToolOptions` reading the generated `Default` in a static initializer before it was set, so `Combine` dropped it; `ToolOptions` is now built on first use, `release.yml` packs each runtime with `-p:PublishAot=true` on its own OS, and a plain `dotnet pack` stays self-contained for `ci.yml` (BL-2486) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0622](ADR-0622-the-runner-performs-each-serial-attempt-from-the-options-through-its-hooks.md) | `CurlCommandRunner` performs each serial attempt from the options through `IToolEasyTransfer`'s hooks and reads `LastResult`; `EngineToolTransferInputs.Url` carries its checked URL, and the runner's events and abort token reach the context unwrapped; `ToolAttempt` is retired (BL-2394) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0623](ADR-0623-ssh-key-callbacks-decide-the-host-key-inside-the-ssh-handler.md) | `SshOptions` gains `HostKeyAcceptance` and `KnownHostDecision`, which `SshHostKeyChecker` asks where libssh2.c asks `CURLOPT_SSH_HOSTKEYFUNCTION` and `CURLOPT_SSH_KEYFUNCTION`; the engine adapts the callbacks and gives a library handle's `scp`/`sftp` perform its SSH options; `CURLOPT_SSL_CTX_FUNCTION` answers `NotBuiltIn` as Schannel's does (BL-2422) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0624](ADR-0624-serial-transfers-run-through-a-libcurl-easy-handle.md) | A serial transfer runs through a `Libcurl` `CurlEasy` from `ToolCurlEngine`'s library, paired with the engine's `IToolEasyTransfer` for the tool's hooks; `CURLOPT_URL` goes through `SetOption`, the other groups follow in BL-2491 to BL-2493 (BL-2451) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0625](ADR-0625-write-out-variables-read-through-the-easy-handles-getinfo.md) | With `-w`, a serial transfer reads its handle's `curl_easy_getinfo` answers before cleanup (`EasyHandleWriteOutInfo`) and `-w` prints them when they describe its result; `-Z` and handle-less transfers keep `WriteOutTransferInfo.FromReport`, and the runner's own IDs, scheme, `Referer`, verify results and early data still lie over them (BL-2452) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0626](ADR-0626-ssh-key-callback-writes-the-known-hosts-file-as-libssh2-does.md) | `CURLOPT_SSH_KEYFUNCTION`'s `FINE_ADD_TO_FILE` and `FINE_REPLACE` make `SshHostKeyChecker` return the known-hosts file with the plain host name added (the mismatching entry deleted for a replace), which the SSH handler writes through `IFileSystem` in libssh2's OpenSSH format, warning `WARNING: writing <file> failed` on failure (BL-2489) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0627](ADR-0627-mime-callback-and-raw-byte-parts-in-a-mimepost.md) | `MultipartFormPart` gains `Bytes` and `Reader` kinds; `CURLOPT_MIMEPOST` sends `curl_mime_data_cb` parts through `MimeCallbackReadStream` (abort is `CURLE_ABORTED_BY_CALLBACK`, seek rewinds) and non-UTF-8 `curl_mime_data` bytes verbatim, while valid UTF-8 stays text so `-F` keeps its platform encoding (BL-2470) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0628](ADR-0628-parallel-transfers-run-through-a-libcurl-multi-handle.md) | A `-Z` run holds one `Libcurl` `CurlMulti` (`ParallelTransferMulti`, `ParallelRun.Transfers`); each attempt adds its easy handle and performs in its own flow, one loop polls and reads the `CURLMSG_DONE` messages, and the tool's queues still decide when a transfer starts (BL-2453) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0629](ADR-0629-curl-console-reaches-the-engine-through-libcurl.md) | `Curl.Console` drops its direct `Curl.Engine.UnitLibrary` reference and reaches the engine transitively through `Libcurl`, whose `CurlGlobal.Init` gives `ToolCurlEngine` its library; the engine types the tool still names stay (BL-2454) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0630](ADR-0630-curl-api-keeps-its-own-strerror-table.md) | `Curl.Api` keeps its own `curl_easy_strerror` table (`CurlCodeText`, measured from curl 8.22.0's `libcurl-4.dll`); `CurlException.ThrowIfFailed` throws for any code but `Ok`, with the handle's error buffer as the message when it has one (BL-2498) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0631](ADR-0631-serial-easy-handle-sets-behaviour-transfer-and-network-options-through-setopt.md) | A serial transfer's behaviour, transfer and network options go through `CurlEasy.SetOption`, one call per field off libcurl's default (`EasySetoptsFromCommandLine`); `--ip-tos`, `--vlan-priority`, `--mptcp` and values a setter would clamp stay on the hooks, and a refused call ends the run with curl's `setopt 0x<option> got bad argument` (BL-2491) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0632](ADR-0632-curl-api-request-and-response-records.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlRequest` (method, `string` URL, headers in order and casing, in-memory body) and `CurlResponse` (status, reason, version, headers, trailers, `TransferInfo`, body) records; `SendAsync` maps the method as the tool maps `-I`, `-d` and `-X`, and splits header lines into the final response's headers and trailers (BL-2499) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0633](ADR-0633-curl-api-websocket-client.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlClient.ConnectWebSocketAsync` and `CurlWebSocket`: whole messages joined from `curl_ws_recv` pieces, control frames between fragments, `ReadMessagesAsync` through the close frame, and a 1 ms wait before asking again after `CURLE_AGAIN` (BL-2508) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0634](ADR-0634-curl-api-request-bodies-are-curlcontent-bytes-or-a-stream.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlRequest.Body` is `CurlContent`: bytes as `CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS` (implicit from `byte[]`), or a stream uploaded through the read callback from its position at send time, rewound by the seek callback, sized by the given length, the remaining length or -1 (chunked); a throwing read aborts with `CURLE_ABORTED_BY_CALLBACK` (BL-2500) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0635](ADR-0635-curl-api-streams-response-bodies-through-a-bounded-buffer.md) | `Curl.Api` streams `CurlResponse.Body` through a 64 KiB buffer that answers `CURL_WRITEFUNC_PAUSE` when full and unpauses at half; `SendAsync` returns once the body starts, disposing the body early aborts the transfer, a later failure is thrown by `ReadAsync`, and status and version come from the status line (BL-2501) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0636](ADR-0636-curl-api-transport-option-records.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlNetworkOptions`, `CurlTlsOptions` and `CurlProxyOptions` map one to one onto the seam's network, TLS and proxy groups (URL and connect-only mode left to their owners), validate each value as it is set, and hang off the client and the request, a request's record replacing the client's whole (BL-2502) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0637](ADR-0637-curl-api-multipart-bodies-build-through-imimebuilder.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlMultipartContent` of immutable `CurlMimePart`s (text, bytes, file, stream, nested; name, file name, type, encoder, headers) builds a new `IMimeBuilder` per send as `CURLOPT_MIMEPOST`; the first failing `curl_mime_*` setter throws `CurlException`, and every builder is disposed when the transfer ends (BL-2506) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0638](ADR-0638-http2-push-sink-offers-curls-pushed-header-lines.md) | HTTP/2 server push reaches an `IHttp2PushSink` given to `HttpProtocolHandler`: ENABLE_PUSH 1 with a sink and 0 without, curl's `name:value` header lines, a pushed response readable as a `Stream`, and a refused or unowned push reset with `RST_STREAM` `CANCEL` (BL-2516) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0639](ADR-0639-curl-api-cancellation-aborts-through-the-progress-callback.md) | `Curl.Api` cancellation: the token aborts the transfer through `CURLOPT_XFERINFOFUNCTION` and unpauses a paused one; a transfer failing after the caller cancelled throws `OperationCanceledException`, any other failure (a timeout included) `CurlException`, and a token cancelled before the start creates no handle (BL-2504) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0640](ADR-0640-curl-api-http-authentication-and-protocol-option-records.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlHttpOptions`, `CurlAuthenticationOptions` and the FTP, SSH, mail, TFTP, TELNET and RTSP records follow ADR-0636 onto the seam's HTTP, authentication and protocol groups; the request keeps its own headers, method and body, and WebSocket options stay `CurlWebSocketOptions`' (BL-2503) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0641](ADR-0641-resume-from-skips-a-read-callback-upload-in-the-engine.md) | `CURLOPT_RESUME_FROM` on a libcurl read-callback upload skips the offset in the engine as curl 8.22.0's `cr_in_resume_from`: the seek callback with `SEEK_SET`, read-and-discard on `CANTSEEK` or no callback, exit 26 `Could not seek stream` or `Could only read N bytes from the input`, exit 18 when `CURLOPT_INFILESIZE` leaves nothing (BL-2473) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0642](ADR-0642-serial-easy-handle-sets-tls-authentication-and-proxy-options-through-setopt.md) | A serial transfer's TLS, authentication and proxy options go through `CurlEasy.SetOption` too (`AWS_SIGV4` before `HTTPAUTH`, `HAPROXY_CLIENT_IP` before `HAPROXYPROTOCOL`, `--ech` as mode, `pn:`, `ecl:`); `--engine`, an `--ech` value `CURLOPT_ECH` would ignore, the TLS-SRP fields and `--httpsig-*` stay on the hooks (BL-2492) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0643](ADR-0643-curl-api-progress-reports-through-iprogress.md) | `Curl.Api` progress: `CurlRequest.Progress` takes a `CurlTransferProgress` (totals, counts, engine speeds, phase from the byte counts) per `CURLOPT_XFERINFOFUNCTION` call on the engine thread, chained before the cancellation callback; a throwing report aborts the transfer as `CurlException`, and reports stop once it ends (BL-2505) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0644](ADR-0644-curl-api-runs-concurrent-transfers-on-one-multi-handle.md) | `Curl.Api` runs every `SendAsync` transfer of a client on one lazily created multi handle driven by one loop; `MaxConnections` and `MaxConnectionsPerHost` set the handle's limits, each call fails on its own, a cancelled call is removed from the handle, and disposing the client cancels every running transfer (BL-2509) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0645](ADR-0645-curl-api-cookie-hsts-and-alt-svc-stores.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlStores`: cookies (Netscape lines through `CURLOPT_COOKIELIST` `ALL` and `CURLINFO_COOKIELIST`) and HSTS (the read and write callbacks) kept client-side and applied at perform, alt-svc as its cache file, shared between clients and live transfers through one share handle (BL-2507) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0646](ADR-0646-trailer-callback-sends-chunked-upload-trailers.md) | `CURLOPT_TRAILERFUNCTION` sends a chunked HTTP/1.1 read-callback upload's trailers as curl 8.22.0's `add_last_chunk`: `IUploadTrailerSource` in the abstractions, the lines in the closing chunk, malformed ones skipped, abort exit 42 `operation aborted by trailing headers callback` (BL-2475) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0647](ADR-0647-curl-api-http-message-handler-maps-onto-curl-client.md) | `Curl.Api`'s `CurlHttpMessageHandler` sends each `HttpRequestMessage` through a `CurlClient` it does not own: headers joined as `HttpClient` writes them, every content as a stream body, the version as `CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION`; the response's headers split by .NET's own header table, its body streamed with trailers filled at the end, a `CurlException` as `HttpRequestException` (BL-2510) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0648](ADR-0648-chunked-stdin-upload-keeps-closing-chunk-out-of-process.md) | A chunked `-T -` upload read from a pipe keeps sending its last data chunk and `0\r\n\r\n` in one write; upstream test60's out-of-process cross-check difference is the recorder answering between curl 8.22.0's two sends, measured on Windows and Linux (BL-2483) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0649](ADR-0649-curl-api-http-message-handler-version-policy-and-proxy.md) | `CurlHttpMessageHandler` speaks only HTTP 1.0, 1.1, 2.0 and 3.0, checks the response's version against `VersionPolicy` and throws `HttpRequestException` (`VersionNegotiationError`) as `SocketsHttpHandler` does, and takes `HttpClientHandler`'s `Proxy`, `UseProxy`, `DefaultProxyCredentials` and `Credentials`, asked per request (BL-2511) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0650](ADR-0650-curl-api-http-message-handler-decompression-redirects-authentication-cookies.md) | `CurlHttpMessageHandler` offers `SocketsHttpHandler`'s decompression, redirect, credential and cookie properties with its defaults: `CURLOPT_ACCEPT_ENCODING` with the decoded `Content-Encoding` removed, `CURLFOLLOW_OBEYCODE` and `CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS` (a limit exceeded thrown), the strongest scheme's credential with `CURLAUTH_ONLY` unless pre-authenticating, and the container's cookies as `CURLOPT_COOKIE` (BL-2512; supersedes ADR-0649's `Credentials`) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0651](ADR-0651-curlopt-httppost-sent-with-stream-parts-through-read-callback.md) | A perform sends `CURLOPT_HTTPPOST` when no `CURLOPT_MIMEPOST` is set (never to a mail URL), built as the MIME post is; a `CURLFORM_STREAM` part is read through `CURLOPT_READFUNCTION` at its `CURLFORM_CONTENTSLENGTH`, 0 sent chunked, the stream object not passed since a managed read callback has no user pointer (BL-2482) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0652](ADR-0652-serial-transfer-http-and-protocol-options-go-through-setopt.md) | A serial transfer's HTTP and protocol fields go through `CurlEasy.SetOption`, one `CURLOPT_COOKIEFILE` per `-b` file, and its `-F` form as a `curl_mime` tree through `CURLOPT_MIMEPOST`; a form with a standard-input part or a refused encoder, and values a setter would refuse or hold, stay on the hooks (BL-2493) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0653](ADR-0653-libtest-first-h-steps-over-libcurl.md) | libtest's `first.h` over Libcurl: `LibTestErrorCodes` holds 8.22.0's `TEST_ERR_*` values (the `CURLE_OBSOLETE*` codes 20 to 57), and `LibTestSteps` carries `result`, answering per step whether the program goes on; a null engine, a cleaned-up library and a null string are the failing `global_init`, `easy_init` and `curl_slist_append` (BL-2554) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0654](ADR-0654-tool-hooked-easy-handle-answers-the-runners-write-out-values.md) | Under `ToolEvents` the engine transfer's `Info` answers the runner's `xfer_id`, `conn_id` (its numbering, through `EngineToolTransferEvents.TakeConnectionId`), request URL and its scheme, `Referer`, verify results and early data, carried on `EngineTransferState`, so `-w` takes a serial transfer's handle answers with no overlay; `-Z` and handle-less transfers keep `FromReport` with the runner's values (BL-2495) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0655](ADR-0655-libtest-multi-share-timing-and-trace-helpers.md) | libtest's multi steps join `LibTestSteps` with `first.h`'s codes and messages (`MultiWaitAsync` held to `multi_poll`'s rules), `ShareInit`/`UrlInit` fail on a cleaned-up library with `TEST_ERR_MAJOR_BAD`, `LibTestClock` times a program on an injected `TimeProvider`, and `LibTestDebugTrace`/`LibTestCliTrace` port `testtrace.c` with per-instance state (BL-2555) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0656](ADR-0656-lib-tool-cases-run-their-ported-program.md) | A `<tool>` case naming `libNNN` or `lib%TESTNUMBER` runs its ported `ILibTest` (looked up by name in `LibTestPrograms`, an explicit AOT-safe table) in place of curl on the same invocation, with the command's words as arguments and the same judging; an unported one skips as "libtest program not ported", any other tool as before (BL-2556) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0657](ADR-0657-engine-applies-curlopt-cookielist-at-perform.md) | A perform from the options applies `CURLOPT_COOKIELIST` in order before the cookie files load: `ALL` and `SESS` remove cookies (`CookieStore.RemoveAll`, `RemoveSessionCookies`), `FLUSH` writes the jar, `RELOAD` loads the files again, any other item is a Netscape or `Set-Cookie:` line that turns the cookie engine on; with the engine off every item is ignored (BL-2520) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0658](ADR-0658-libtest-engine-dials-the-in-memory-servers.md) | `ManagedCurlEngine` takes an internal `EngineDialing` (TCP dialer, resolver, datagram connector) that `EngineComposition.CreateTransports` builds the engine's transports over in place of sockets, and the ratchet runs ported libtest programs over such an engine on the case's in-memory servers (`UpstreamConformanceTests.CreateEngine`) (BL-2558) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0659](ADR-0659-easy-handle-sends-no-default-user-agent.md) | A perform from the options with no `ToolSettings` (a `Libcurl` or `Curl.Api` handle) turns a `null` `CURLOPT_USERAGENT` into an empty one, so it sends no `User-Agent` to the origin or a proxy's `CONNECT`, as libcurl does; the curl tool keeps its `curl/8.22.0` (BL-2559) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0660](ADR-0660-socket-callbacks-run-for-quic-connections.md) | `SocketCallbackConnector` runs the open-socket, socket-option and close-socket callbacks around each QUIC connection as around a TCP one, the open-socket callback seeing `SOCK_DGRAM` and `IPPROTO_UDP`; the QUIC session is built over the wrapped connection, so the pool above still shares it (BL-2474) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0661](ADR-0661-parallel-meter-holds-while-interface-binds.md) | Under `-Z` a transfer given `--interface` holds the combined meter's draws (`ParallelTransferProgress.HoldsTheLoop`) until it reports itself past connect or ends, as curl's loop blocks while it binds, so a failed bind prints `curl: (45)` before the meter (AF-0237, BL-2574) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0662](ADR-0662-libtest-ports-write-to-the-program-stdout-and-wait-in-place-of-select.md) | Each libtest port sets `CURLOPT_WRITEDATA` to the program's stdout, as libcurl's default writer writes to `stdout`; `select_test` waits with `curl_multi_wait`; a port's body is a public `TestAsync` over the library, and setopt runs stop at the first refusal (BL-2527) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0663](ADR-0663-lib530-shares-one-global-init-and-waits-with-multi-wait.md) | `Lib530` calls `global_init` once around its five runs, as Libcurl's cleanup disposes the case's engine; its `select()` over the program's own socket sets is `curl_multi_wait`, after which every socket in them counts as ready (BL-2595) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0664](ADR-0664-init-mem-callbacks-cover-easy-handle-allocations.md) | A `curl_global_init_mem` library's easy handles allocate through its callbacks: `calloc` at `curl_easy_init` and `duphandle`, `strdup` per accepted string option, `malloc` per `curl_easy_escape` with a handle, `free` of all at cleanup; `realloc` never (BL-2598) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0667](ADR-0667-cookielist-at-setopt-over-a-share.md) | Over a share handle that shares cookies, each `CURLOPT_COOKIELIST` item is applied as it is set under its own cookie lock, and `CURLINFO_COOKIELIST` lists the share's cookies under one more cookie lock when Libcurl's getinfo copies it, as libcurl 8.22.0 (upstream test506, BL-2605) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0669](ADR-0669-websocket-upload-through-the-read-callback.md) | A `ws` or `wss` perform that uploads through the read callback sends each read as one binary frame, as curl 8.22.0's `cr_ws_read`, unless `curl_ws_start_frame` inside the callback started a frame, which the following reads fill; the tool's `-T` keeps one frame (BL-2611) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0670](ADR-0670-websocket-tool-upload-framed-per-read.md) | A `ws` or `wss` `-T` upload goes out in one binary frame per 64 KiB read, each read filled as `fread` fills it (a 200 000-byte file is frames of 65 536, 65 536, 65 536 and 3 392 bytes), and an empty one in no frame with `We are completely uploaded and fine`, as measured with curl 8.22.0 (BL-2683) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0671](ADR-0671-libtest-engine-signs-at-the-epoch.md) | The ratchet's libtest engine (`ManagedCurlEngine`'s internal `signingClock`) and `--aws-sigv4` commands sign at the epoch, and a case naming `aws` sees `Debug`, standing in for the debug build's `CURL_FORCETIME` (BL-2675) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0672](ADR-0672-curlopt-port-rewrites-the-transfer-url.md) | `CURLOPT_PORT` is written into the URL a library perform transfers through a `ManagedCurlUrlHandle`, as curl 8.22.0's `parse_remote_port` sets `CURLUPART_PORT`, so the connect, the request target, the `Host` header and the effective URL carry it (BL-2606) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0673](ADR-0673-curlopt-curlu-replaces-the-url-at-perform.md) | `CURLOPT_CURLU` replaces `CURLOPT_URL` when a library perform starts: the engine reads the URL handle's whole URL as curl 8.22.0's `Curl_pretransfer` does, a handle with no URL answering `No URL set` (BL-2628) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0674](ADR-0674-upstream-harness-emulates-rtspd.md) | The upstream harness emulates `rtspd` on `%RTSPPORT` (`RtspServerConnector`): each request answered with the `<dataN>` its path picks, then the part's `rtp:` interleaved packets, the connection kept open until a reply holds `swsclose`; supersedes ADR-0457's decision 2 (BL-2617) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0675](ADR-0675-mimepost-read-at-perform-and-unbound-by-mime-free.md) | A `CURLOPT_MIMEPOST` structure is read as its parts are at each perform and `curl_easy_duphandle`; once `curl_mime_free` frees it the handle posts a zero-length body, as an empty `HttpPost` form (BL-2626) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0668](ADR-0668-libcurl-mprintf-family.md) | Libcurl's `curl_m*printf` family (`CurlPrintf`) ports `lib/mprintf.c` 8.22.0 and reads each argument as its conversion's C type (`%ld` 32 bits on Windows, 64 elsewhere), formats doubles over their exact decimal value, and throws `ArgumentException` where C is undefined (BL-2616) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0666](ADR-0666-share-lock-order-during-a-perform.md) | A perform over a share handle takes the cookie lock (once more with `CURLOPT_COOKIEFILE` set), then the DNS lock to prune, look up and, the first time, add the host, the cookie lock per `Cookie` and `Set-Cookie`, and one DNS lock when done, as libcurl 8.22.0 (upstream test506, BL-2603) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0665](ADR-0665-websocket-partial-frames-and-start-frame.md) | The engine sends a WebSocket frame in parts as curl 8.22.0's `lib/ws.c`: `CURLWS_OFFSET` announces `fragsize` and later sends continue the frame (a part past what is left `CURLE_BAD_FUNCTION_ARGUMENT`), `curl_ws_start_frame` writes the head (`CURLE_FAILED_INIT` in raw mode, `CURLE_SEND_ERROR` while a frame is unfinished), the same inside a read callback or out (BL-2481) | Accepted | 2026-10-16 |
| [0664](ADR-0664-init-mem-callbacks-cover-easy-handle-allocations.md) | A `curl_global_init_mem` library's easy handles allocate through its callbacks: `calloc` at `curl_easy_init` and `duphandle`, `strdup` per accepted string option, `malloc` per `curl_easy_escape` with a handle, `free` of all at cleanup; `realloc` never (BL-2598) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0676](ADR-0676-infilesize-holds-an-http-read-callback-upload.md) | An `http` or `https` read-callback upload is held to `CURLOPT_INFILESIZE` (asked for no more than is left, ended there, sent with `Content-Length`), and `CURLOPT_HEADEROPT` unified sends `CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER` in the `CONNECT` (upstream tests 1525 to 1527, BL-2645) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0677](ADR-0677-library-handle-proxy-credentials-reach-the-proxy.md) | A library handle's `CURLOPT_PROXYUSERPWD` (with `CURLOPT_PROXYUSERNAME` and `CURLOPT_PROXYPASSWORD` winning over its halves) is the proxy credential, so a proxy's 407 is answered (upstream test1588, BL-2660) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0678](ADR-0678-ratchet-stands-in-for-curl-entropy.md) | The ratchet stands in for the debug build's `CURL_ENTROPY` in the WebSocket libtest cases: `Debug` for a case naming `ws` and setting it, and the libtest engine's WebSocket key and masks from the forced sequence (upstream tests 2301 to 2304, BL-2688) | Accepted | 2026-10-17 |
| [0679](ADR-0679-http2-status-line-over-http1-refused-off-windows.md) | Off Windows, an `HTTP/2 ddd` status line over HTTP/1.x, interim or final, fails at once with exit 8, `Version mismatch (from HTTP/1 to HTTP/2)`, and an `HTTP/3 ddd` one with exit 1 (measured, upstream test1704, BL-2715) | Accepted; amended by ADR-0788 | 2026-10-17 |
| [0680](ADR-0680-upstream-case-558-stays-excluded-as-trackmemory.md) | Upstream case 558 stays excluded: the harness never reports `TrackMemory` or fabricates libcurl's `memdump` allocation log, which managed code has no equivalent of (BL-2615) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0681](ADR-0681-connect-only-runs-ftp-and-imap-connect-phase.md) | A `CURLOPT_CONNECT_ONLY` 1 perform of an `ftp`, `ftps`, `imap` or `imaps` URL runs the protocol's connect phase (FTP login, IMAP `CAPABILITY`) and keeps the open connection for `curl_easy_recv` and `curl_easy_send` (upstream tests 597 and 660, BL-2623) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0682](ADR-0682-ca-certificates-from-the-environment-off-windows.md) | Off Windows, with no `--cacert` or `--capath` and the certificate checked, `CURL_CA_BUNDLE` names the CA file, else `SSL_CERT_DIR` the CA folder and `SSL_CERT_FILE` the CA file, as curl 8.22.0's OpenSSL build reads them; the Schannel build Curl matches on Windows reads none (BL-2723) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0683](ADR-0683-curl-formadd-standard-input-and-file-reads-per-fill.md) | A `curl_formadd` file named `-` is sent as an unknown-size read of the engine's standard input (the harness's case `<stdin>`, else empty), and in a body with a read-callback part each file part is read at most once per buffer fill, as libcurl's `read_part_content` (upstream test650, BL-2625) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0684](ADR-0684-curl-easy-cleanup-closes-the-handles-own-connections.md) | `curl_easy_cleanup` closes the idle connections the handle's performs opened in the engine's cache (not a multi handle's or the curl tool's), so an FTP one sends `QUIT`; an FTP `-I` transfer remembers its directory for the reused connection (upstream test661, BL-2629) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0685](ADR-0685-a-paused-read-callback-part-waits-and-the-progress-callback-ticks.md) | A paused `curl_mime_data_cb` or `curl_formadd` stream part waits for `curl_easy_pause` on the handle's pause gate, and while a transfer is paused its progress callback is called again every second, as `curl_easy_perform` wakes (upstream tests 670 to 673, BL-2631) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0686](ADR-0686-connect-only-active-socket-is-an-engine-number-curl-multi-wait-polls.md) | A `CURLOPT_CONNECT_ONLY` 1 connection gets an engine socket number from `0x60000000` as `CURLINFO_ACTIVESOCKET` and `CURLINFO_LASTSOCKET`, and `curl_multi_wait` answers that number's `POLLOUT` and `POLLIN` from the connection, for a transfer in the handle (upstream test677, BL-2633) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0687](ADR-0687-the-harness-stands-in-for-curl-ftp-pwd-stop-and-easy-cleanup-leaves-the-multi.md) | The conformance harness stands in for the debug build's `CURL_FTP_PWD_STOP` (the engine's first perform stops its FTP connect phase before `PWD`, and the case gets Debug), and `curl_easy_cleanup` removes a handle from the multi handle it is still in (upstream test753, BL-2636) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0688](ADR-0688-a-pending-upload-read-races-the-response-status-line.md) | Over HTTP/1.x a stream body read that stays pending races the response's status line: a status of 300 or above first stops sending and closes, or with `KeepSendingOnError` sends the rest once the response is delivered and keeps the connection (upstream case 1533, BL-2729) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0689](ADR-0689-the-upstream-case-ratchet-makes-no-task-overlap.md) | The upstream case ratchet (`PassingUpstreamCases.txt`) merges line by line (`merge=union`) and makes no overlap in `task-board.ps1`, so the tasks that add cases to it can run in parallel lanes | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0690](ADR-0690-the-upstream-case-runner-lists-the-wakeup-feature.md) | The upstream case runner lists the `wakeup` feature on every platform, since `Libcurl`, which runs the ported libtest programs, has `curl_multi_wakeup`; cases 1564, 1565, 2412 and 2414 pass and join the ratchet, 2405 fails and is filed as BL-2743 (BL-2661) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0691](ADR-0691-rtsp-handler-makes-libcurl-requests-as-curl-8-22-rtsp-c.md) | `RtspProtocolHandler` makes libcurl's RTSP requests from per-perform `RtspRequestSettings` and a handle-lived `RtspSessionState` in curl 8.22.0's `lib/rtsp.c` header order; `SETUP` without `Transport` fails with 43, and there is no session-ID refusal (BL-2751) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0692](ADR-0692-a-libcurl-handle-keeps-its-last-401-digest-challenges.md) | A Libcurl handle (`ManagedEasyTransfer`) keeps the `WWW-Authenticate` values of its last 401 and passes them as `HttpRequestOptions.KeptDigestChallenges`, which `HttpProtocolHandler` answers on the next transfer's first request when Digest is the one scheme allowed, as curl 8.22.0's handle-lived Digest state (BL-2747) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0693](ADR-0693-ci-runs-on-work-dark-factory-finish-instead-of-being-cancelled.md) | A CI run on `work/dark-factory` always finishes (`cancel-in-progress` is false there): a newer push replaces only the waiting run, so parallel lanes no longer leave Linux and macOS untested | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0694](ADR-0694-the-board-offers-narrow-touches-before-whole-projects.md) | `task-board.ps1` orders ready tasks of equal priority and waiting count by how many whole project folders their `touches` name, fewest first, so tasks naming files in a busy project run side by side before one holding it all | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0695](ADR-0695-curl-has-threadsafe-and-threaded-resolver.md) | The upstream case runner reports `threadsafe` (on Curl's `Features:` line) and `threaded-resolver` (which `runtests.pl` derives from `AsynchDNS` without `c-ares`) on every platform, so case 3026 runs and passes and case 506 is skipped as for the reference curl (BL-2700) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0697](ADR-0697-url-bytes-that-are-not-utf-8-are-lone-surrogates.md) | A URL byte that is not part of valid UTF-8 is carried in .NET text as the lone surrogate U+DC00 plus the byte (`UrlByteText`), so the URL API keeps `%c0` and `\xff` through decoding and encoding as libcurl 8.22.0 does (BL-2738) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0698](ADR-0698-ratchet-stands-in-for-curl-ws-force-zero-mask.md) | The ratchet stands in for the debug build's `CURL_WS_FORCE_ZERO_MASK` in the WebSocket libtest cases: `Debug` for a case naming `ws` and setting it, and the libtest engine's frame masks zero (`ZeroMaskWebSocketRandomSource`; upstream tests 2700 to 2723, BL-2698) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0699](ADR-0699-easy-handle-keeps-the-picked-auth-scheme.md) | A library easy handle keeps the authentication scheme its last perform picked, unmasked, as libcurl 8.22.0 keeps `authhost.picked`, so `CURLINFO_HTTPAUTH_USED` reports it on the next perform and a kept Basic goes before a challenge (upstream test694, BL-2709) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0700](ADR-0700-a-refused-kept-digest-answer-is-sent-once-more.md) | A 401 to a first request that answered a handle's kept Digest challenges is answered once more with the same value when the previous perform picked Digest, as libcurl 8.22.0's kept `authhost.avail` ignores that 401's Digest header as a duplicate (upstream test2027, BL-2745) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0701](ADR-0701-ratchet-gives-the-libtest-engine-curl-entropys-mime-boundary.md) | The ratchet gives the libtest engine the MIME boundary upstream's debug build makes under `CURL_ENTROPY` (`curl_mime_init` through `Curl_rand_alnum`), through the engine's internal `createFormBoundary` (upstream test1972, BL-2695) | Accepted | 2026-10-18 |
| [0702](ADR-0702-verbose-without-a-debug-function-writes-to-stderr.md) | A library handle with `CURLOPT_VERBOSE` and no `CURLOPT_DEBUGFUNCTION` writes libcurl's default `* `, `> ` and `< ` lines to `CURLOPT_STDERR`, else the engine's standard error, which the ratchet sets to the case's stderr (upstream tests 1506 and 1510, BL-2726) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0703](ADR-0703-verifyhost-without-verifypeer-still-checks-the-name.md) | `CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYHOST` without `CURLOPT_SSL_VERIFYPEER` still refuses a certificate that does not name the host with exit 60 (`TlsClientOptions.ChecksHostNameWhenInsecure`), as curl 8.22.0 checks the name apart from the peer (upstream test2118, BL-2692) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0704](ADR-0704-cainfo-blob-is-trusted-in-place-of-the-cainfo-file.md) | `CURLOPT_CAINFO_BLOB` and `CURLOPT_PROXY_CAINFO_BLOB` reach `TlsClientOptions.CaCertificateBlob`, trusted as a `--cacert` file is and in its place (upstream test678, BL-2725) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0705](ADR-0705-connect-only-websocket-close-before-the-head-ends-is-ok.md) | A `CURLOPT_CONNECT_ONLY` 2 WebSocket perform whose server closes before the reply head ends answers `CURLE_OK` with no upgraded connection, as curl 8.22.0's `Curl_http_done` skips its empty-reply check under `connect_only` (upstream test2304, BL-2712) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0706](ADR-0706-upstream-case-594-stays-off-the-ratchet.md) | Upstream case 594 stays off `PassingUpstreamCases.txt`: curl 8.22.0 lists it in `tests/data/DISABLED`, and its expected `QUIT` after exit 28 contradicts `ftp_done_status`/`ftp_quit`; real curl ends 594's setup with exit 12 after 120 s and no `QUIT` (BL-2795) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0707](ADR-0707-empty-ca-environment-variables-count-as-unset.md) | An empty `CURL_CA_BUNDLE`, `SSL_CERT_DIR` or `SSL_CERT_FILE` counts as unset, and an empty or malformed `--capath` adds nothing and an empty `--cacert` is exit 77, never an unhandled exception (GF-0097, BL-2822) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0708](ADR-0708-an-abort-after-unread-bytes-at-the-write-stays-an-abort.md) | A Windows read aborted after a write that found unread bytes on the socket (test1802's CONNECT tunnel the proxy closed after leftover bytes) stays `Connection was aborted`; ADR-0419's rewrite to reset applies only when none were unread (GF-0096, BL-2821) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0709](ADR-0709-digest-stays-available-after-an-auth-problem.md) | Digest stays available into the next perform after a final 401 offering Digest that sets curl's `authproblem` - a non-stale Digest challenge with a nonce held, or a bare `NTLM` refusing an NTLM answer - and the kept Digest answer's resend keys on that state (upstream 2027, 2030, BL-2823) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0710](ADR-0710-the-shift-end-merge-is-followed-by-a-five-reviewer-review.md) | After each shift-end merge, one headless run of three PR Review Toolkit reviewers (errors, types, code; the audit office covers tests and comments) reads what the shift merged, beside the next shift and files a task for each Critical (High) or Important (Normal) issue; it never holds the merge. A two-shift trial, then Stewart decides | Trial | 2026-10-19 |
| [0711](ADR-0711-upstream-unittest-cases-run-as-ported-unit-programs.md) | Upstream's 84 `unittest` cases run as ported unit programs (`IUnitProgram`, explicit `UnitPrograms.Ported`) in new `Curl.Conformance.UnitProgram.UnitLibrary`/`.UnitTests`; 64 are ported by BL-2842 to BL-2848, 20 have no counterpart because they test C-only structures (BL-2840) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0712](ADR-0712-curl-ships-a-curl-config-executable-on-every-platform.md) | Curl ships a `curl-config` executable (`Curl.Config`, `Curl.Config.UnitLibrary`, `.UnitTests`) on every platform, though the Windows reference build has none, so upstream cases 1013, 1014, 1022 and 1023 run against it (BL-2853) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0713](ADR-0713-a-listed-upstream-case-whose-precheck-refuses-this-platform-is-not-a-regression.md) | A listed upstream case skipped because its precheck prints `Test requires a Unix system` (test2072 on `MSWin32`) is `Inconclusive` where it cannot run, as ADR-0488's feature skips are, and held to the list on Linux and macOS (BL-2828) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0714](ADR-0714-perl-command-cases-run-a-registered-csharp-interpretation.md) | A `<command type="perl">` case runs a C# interpretation registered by its script's file name in place of curl, and `%CURL` is the token `curl` an interpretation runs in process (BL-2863) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0715](ADR-0715-sftp-remote-time-comes-from-the-download-stat.md) | An SFTP download's `-R` time is the `mtime` of the download's own `STAT` answer when it carries `ACMODTIME`, no second `STAT`; curl never applies the SCP `T` line's time (BL-2871) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0716](ADR-0716-a-faked-form-file-name-of-dev-null-is-the-unix-name-everywhere.md) | In an upstream case, `%DEV_NULL` as a form part's faked file name (`filename=%DEV_NULL`) is `/dev/null` on every platform; anywhere else it stays the platform's null device, `NUL` on Windows (test173, BL-2830) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0717](ADR-0717-upstream-unit-cases-for-libcurl-private-c-structures-have-no-counterpart.md) | Upstream unit cases 1300, 1305, 1306, 1309, 1330, 1602, 1603, 1616, 1661, 2600, 2601, 2606, 3211, 3212, 3213, 3300, 3301 and 3306 have no C# counterpart: each tests a private C structure, allocator or thread hook Curl replaces with .NET collections, `Task`s and `Channel`s (BL-2849) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0718](ADR-0718-upstream-c-source-and-man-page-tooling-cases-have-no-counterpart-except-managen.md) | Upstream cases 1135, 1140, 1165, 1167, 1169, 1173, 1185, 1275, 1279 and 1544 test C-source, nroff, Perl and OS/400 tooling Curl does not have; 1705 and 1706 (managen) are covered by new task BL-2881 (BL-2852) | Accepted | 2026-10-19 |
| [0719](ADR-0719-test1707-pl-reads-real-curl-8-22-0-s-manual-as-curl-txt.md) | test1707.pl's `curl.txt` (cases 1707, 1708, 1710) is real curl 8.22.0's `curl -M` text, vendored as test data until `managen` exists (BL-2866) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0720](ADR-0720-the-harness-reports-http3-to-cases-naming-its-http3-stand-in.md) | The harness reports `http/3` (and `Debug`) to a case naming its `http/3` stand-in server, so 2500 to 2503 run; held on Linux and macOS, Inconclusive on Windows (BL-2838) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0721](ADR-0721-the-harness-stands-in-for-curl-smallreqsend-and-curl-smallsends-by-splitting-writes.md) | The harness stands in for `CURL_SMALLREQSEND` and `CURL_SMALLSENDS` by splitting each connection's writes, so upstream cases 363, 1294, 1295 and 5028 run (BL-2832) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0722](ADR-0722-libtest-environment-cases-96-506-518-537-558-and-1592.md) | Upstream cases 96 and 558 (`TrackMemory`), 506 (`!threaded-resolver`), 518 and 537 (`getrlimit`) stay off; 1592 runs on a stalled-lookup resolver stand-in (BL-2839) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0723](ADR-0723-a-unit-program-port-runs-a-check-where-the-platforms-curl-build-compiles-it.md) | A unit-program port runs a guarded check only where the platform's curl build compiles it (Schannel on Windows, OpenSSL elsewhere); elsewhere it prints the C program's `not tested since ...` line and passes (BL-2843) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0724](ADR-0724-parallel-progress-times-past-999-days-follow-curl-8-22-timebuf.md) | A `-Z` progress time past 999 days is written in 30-day months, then 365-day years, then ` >99999y`, as curl 8.22.0's `timebuf` writes it (BL-2899) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0725](ADR-0725-an-mdtm-date-curl-reads-but-the-calendar-lacks-rolls-over.md) | An `MDTM` date curl reads but the calendar lacks (31 February, day 00, second 60) rolls over as curl's `time2epoch` does; month 00, a year before 1583 or a time past `DateTimeOffset.MaxValue` is unknown (BL-2904) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0726](ADR-0726-capsule-datagram-channel-decodes-by-curls-capsule-rules.md) | `CapsuleDatagramChannel` decodes received capsules by curl 8.22.0's `Curl_capsule_process_udp_raw` rules: another type, context ID or length 0, or a datagram longer than the buffer, fails the receive (BL-2894) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0727](ADR-0727-doh-sends-the-https-rr-query-first-for-an-https-origin.md) | `--doh-url` sends the HTTPS-RR query first, before A and AAAA, for a direct `https://` origin, `--ech` or not, as curl 8.22.0's HTTPSRR build does; `--ech` reuses the kept record (BL-2920); amended with the query's `--trace-config doh` lines (BL-2954) and the `-v` `HTTPS-RR <host>:<port>:` line before `Host ... was resolved.` (BL-2980), written again for an origin the DNS cache answers (BL-2986), and which `--trace-config` components write them, with the `HTTP status N` error (BL-2979) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0728](ADR-0728-https-record-decoder-follows-curls-doh-resp-decode-httpsrr.md) | The HTTPS record decoder follows curl 8.22.0's `doh_resp_decode_httpsrr`: control characters in the target and out-of-order keys refused, trailing bytes and an empty `alpn` accepted, an empty `ech` refused, each failure one curl code (BL-2924) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0729](ADR-0729-unit-program-1323-has-no-counterpart-and-the-limits-batch-is-split.md) | Unit program 1323 (`curlx_timediff_ms` over `struct curltime`) has no counterpart; BL-2848 ports 1605 and 1606, and the ports of 1303, 1399 and 3216 move to BL-2958, BL-2959 and BL-2960 | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0730](ADR-0730-a-reset-met-after-waiting-on-the-upload-source-is-exit-56.md) | A write that fails after `HttpRequestBodyWriter` waited on its upload source reads the connection once; a failed read there ends with exit 56 `Recv failure:`, as curl 8.22.0 meets the reset with `recv` while its send is held (BL-2956) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0731](ADR-0731-protocol-projects-sit-in-a-protocols-solution-folder.md) | Each `Curl.Protocol.<Name>.*` project sits with its tests in the `/Protocols/<Name>/` solution folder of `Curl.slnx`; the directories on disk stay flat (BL-2963) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0732](ADR-0732-the-harness-stands-in-for-curl-time-curl-debug-size-and-curl-version-in-write-out.md) | The conformance harness has `-w` honour curl's debug-build `CURL_TIME` (the `%time{}` clock with its microseconds, the getinfo times, speeds and local port), `CURL_DEBUG_SIZE` and `CURL_VERSION` through `DebugWriteOutStandIn`; the shipped `curl` never reads them (BL-2909) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0733](ADR-0733-redirect-hops-phase-times-add-up-as-curl-pgrstime-adds-them.md) | A redirect chain's `%{time_namelookup}` to `%{time_starttransfer}` add each hop's own phase time, as curl 8.22.0's `Curl_pgrsTime` does (`RedirectHopTimings`, upstream unit1399) (BL-2959) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0734](ADR-0734-a-transfer-refuses-a-host-name-idn-cannot-convert.md) | A transfer refuses a host name `IdnMapping` cannot convert (bytes that are not UTF-8, a label too long) with exit 3, as curl 8.22.0 in a UTF-8 locale; the URL API keeps it as decoded (BL-2962) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0735](ADR-0735-the-harness-stands-in-for-curl-upload-size-curl-dbg-suppress-connect-hds-and-the-tools-curl-entropy.md) | The conformance harness stands in for curl's debug-build `CURL_UPLOAD_SIZE` (an upload-capping file system), `CURL_DBG_SUPPRESS_CONNECT_HDS` (`--suppress-connect-headers`) and the curl tool's `CURL_ENTROPY` WebSocket key through new `CreateRunner` parameters; the shipped `curl` never reads them (BL-2910) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0736](ADR-0736-a-socks-proxy-at-localhost-with-a-path-is-reached-over-that-unix-socket.md) | A SOCKS proxy at `localhost` with a path (`socks5h://localhost/path`, `--socks5 localhost/path`) is reached over that Unix domain socket, as curl 8.22.0's `parse_proxy` reads it, on every platform (BL-2955) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0737](ADR-0737-disabled-upstream-cases-stay-off-the-ratchet-unicode-stays-off-ssl-sessions-off-windows.md) | Upstream's eight disabled cases of BL-2858 stay off the ratchet; the harness keeps `Unicode` off until Curl handles test3203, test1703 and test472 under it; `ssl-sessions` is reported where `SSLS-EXPORT` is (Linux and macOS); an IP host gets only a cookie for its exact address (test8) (BL-2858) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0738](ADR-0738-doh-answers-decode-nxdomain-and-cap-ttls-as-curl-8-22-0-does.md) | DoH answers decode RCODE 3 as `DnsMessageFailure.NxDomain` ("No such name") and cap each answer TTL at 86400 seconds, as curl 8.22.0's `doh_resp_decode` does; exit 6 is unchanged; CNAME data, `NoContent` and 8.22.0's `--trace-config doh` lines follow in BL-2970 (BL-2969) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0739](ADR-0739-cookie-and-netrc-files-follow-curl-get-line-netrc-in-text-mode-on-windows.md) | Cookie and netrc files follow curl 8.22.0's `Curl_get_line` line rules: a cookie line past 4998 characters is reported as `CURLE_TOO_LARGE` (the engine's exit 100 is BL-2971), a netrc line of 16384 bytes or more anywhere is a syntax error, and netrc is read in text mode on Windows (`^Z` ends it, CR LF is one line feed) (BL-2916) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0740](ADR-0740-dns-shuffle-addresses-shuffles-each-look-up-below-the-dns-cache.md) | `CURLOPT_DNS_SHUFFLE_ADDRESSES` shuffles each look-up's addresses (Fisher-Yates over `RandomNumberGenerator`) below the DNS cache, as curl 8.22.0's `dns_shuffle_addr`; `--resolve` entries not yet; the harness reports `shuffle-dns` to case 1608 alone (BL-2922) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0741](ADR-0741-windows-arguments-stay-in-the-ansi-code-page-as-the-mingw-reference-curl-reads-them.md) | Windows arguments stay in the ANSI code page, as the non-`Unicode` mingw reference curl 8.22.0 reads them (measured: `あ` sent as `?`, `ä` as `E4`; System32's `Unicode` curl sends UTF-8); `Unicode` stays off and test472 off the ratchet; test472's SigV4 signature already matches (BL-2975) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0742](ADR-0742-library-perform-fails-too-large-on-a-cookie-file-line-too-long.md) | A `Libcurl` or `Curl.Api` perform whose `CURLOPT_COOKIEFILE` holds a line past 4998 characters answers `CURLE_TOO_LARGE` (100) before anything is sent, as `Curl_pretransfer`; a `RELOAD` keeps discarding its answer, the perform's own load failing the same way (BL-2978) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0743](ADR-0743-the-integrate-lock-tests-affected-projects-and-resolves-outside-it.md) | Under the integrate lock a lane builds the whole solution but runs only the fast tests of the projects its commits can affect, and resolves a rebase conflict with the lock released (BL-2967) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0744](ADR-0744-a-lane-claims-ci-fix-tasks-past-the-usage-stop.md) | A lane claims High CI-fix tasks past the usage stop (`Invoke-Claim -CiFixOnly`), so a used-up window no longer leaves a red CI unfixed (BL-2946, AF-0308) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0745](ADR-0745-limit-rate-holds-the-upload-in-the-tool.md) | `--limit-rate` holds a `-T` or `-F` upload in the runner with `RateLimitedUploadStream`, seekable so a resend can rewind it; `-d` bodies are BL-2957, a reset while the upload waits is BL-2956's exit 56 (BL-2938) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0746](ADR-0746-a-run-time-feature-mask-hides-proxy-ipv6-and-idn.md) | `EngineFeatureMask` in `Curl.Engine.UnitLibrary`, off by default in the curl tool and `Libcurl`, lets the harness run as a build without proxy, IPv6 or IDN; under `Proxy` a set proxy answers exit 4 with curl's "proxy support is disabled" line (BL-2984) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0747](ADR-0747-upstream-case-1478-stays-off-the-ratchet.md) (superseded by BL-3402) | Upstream case 1478 stays off the ratchet: `managen listhelp` and the expected `tool_listhelp.c` are both upstream files, and `CurlHelpTextTests` already pins Curl's `--help` against real curl 8.22.0 (BL-2850) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0748](ADR-0748-no-stand-in-for-curl-dbg-he-aaaa-await-ms.md) | No stand-in for `CURL_DBG_HE_AAAA_AWAIT_MS`: Curl's DoH order is fixed already (ADR-0380) and case 2100's `<strippart>` hides the POST order, so 2100, 2101 and 2117 pass with only the `Debug` report (BL-2837) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0749](ADR-0749-upstream-source-analysis-scripts-read-the-vendored-headers.md) | Upstream's symbol and error-code scripts (cases 1119, 1175, 1477) read the vendored headers, not `Libcurl`'s tables, with no C preprocessor and `libcurl-errors.md` standing for the generated `.3` (BL-2991) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0750](ADR-0750-test1139-stands-in-for-curl-1-with-the-option-files.md) | `UpstreamTest1139Script` stands in for the generated `curl.1` with the `.IP` line `managen` writes for each `docs/cmdline-opts/*.md` option file, since no release tarball has `curl.1` (BL-2992) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0751](ADR-0751-forced-entropy-stands-in-for-the-digest-client-nonce.md) | The debug build's `CURL_ENTROPY` stands in for the Digest and DIGEST-MD5 client nonce through an optional `digestRandomBytes`, and `Debug` is reported to the `!SSPI` Digest and NTLM cases (BL-2834) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0752](ADR-0752-test1222-reads-a-libcurl-manual-converted-from-the-markdown.md) | `UpstreamTest1222Script` reads the libcurl `.3` pages `UpstreamCd2nroff` converts in memory from the vendored `docs/libcurl` Markdown when no built pages exist, so case 1222 runs its full deprecation check (BL-3000) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0753](ADR-0753-lanes-run-on-a-slim-context-with-cheaper-models-for-routine-work.md) | Lanes run on a slim context (no user settings, plugins or MCP servers, `LaneRules.md` for CLAUDE.md), with Sonnet for routine feature work, an 80-turn cap, a pre-claim check and killed runs priced from their usage (BL-3001) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0754](ADR-0754-a-sasl-exchange-says-when-its-empty-answer-is-an-empty-line.md) | `ISaslExchange.SendsLastAnswerAsEmptyLine` tells the IMAP, POP3 and SMTP handlers to send an empty answer as an empty line (curl's "no message", DIGEST-MD5's answer to `rspauth`) rather than `=` (BL-3004) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0755](ADR-0755-upstream-cases-745-and-1276-check-c-only-header-tooling.md) | Upstream cases 745 (`typecheck-gcc.h` against `curl.h`) and 1276 (`lib/optiontable.pl` against `lib/easyoptions.c`) check C-only header tooling and have no counterpart; the other seven libcurl table checks pass (BL-2851) | Accepted | 2026-10-21 |
| [0756](ADR-0756-the-ci-watch-defers-a-ci-task-a-green-run-proves-fixed.md) | The CI watch files a job-step failure with the files its log names (or `Measure-CodeQuality.ps1` / `Curl.slnx`), never empty touches, and defers a Backlog CI task once a green run containing its filing proves it fixed (BL-3028) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0757](ADR-0757-a-shift-counts-only-tasks-claimable-now.md) | One claimable-now predicate (not over its cost cap, not interactive only) decides whether `-Continuous` starts the next shift, how many lanes `-Lanes Auto` runs and what a lane claims; a board of only capped tasks ends the shift and traces when the next cap frees (BL-3090) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0758](ADR-0758-an-idle-continuous-shift-polls-the-board.md) | An idle `-Continuous` shift polls the board every `-IdlePollMinutes` (`Get-IdlePollDecision`: stop at the `-Hours` deadline or a usage limit, start the next shift through the one hand-over when a task is claimable, else wait) and writes an idle/polling heartbeat; the waiting-on-Stewart alarm rings when polling stops (BL-3092) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0759](ADR-0759-a-task-over-its-cost-cap-is-split-by-one-bounded-run.md) | A task over its cost cap is split by one Sonnet run ($1, 30 turns) at most once per task per day, from the claim's over-cap skip and from a run stopped at the cap; the shift commits and pushes the split inside `Enter-Lock`, and a failed split blocks the task with its reason (BL-3091) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0760](ADR-0760-a-run-starts-only-with-2-dollars-of-its-cap-left.md) | A task's runs of the last day must leave `$MinRunBudgetUsd` ($2) of its cost cap, else a fresh claim is refused and the task split; no run gets less than $2, so a requeued claim no longer starts on $1.02 and requeues again (BL-3079, AF-0326) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0761](ADR-0761-a-process-finding-s-task-measures-the-live-logs-after-its-fix.md) | A process finding's task (other than a `:cost-outlier` one) measures the live logs written after its fix, not the frozen log copy its reproduction reads, and is filed interactive only; the finding still closes only by re-audit (BL-3064, BL-3066, BL-3080, BL-3082, BL-3083, BL-3084) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0762](ADR-0762-bare-curl-names-ai-help-in-its-try-help-line.md) | Bare `curl` (empty command line) names `--ai-help` in its try-help line; every other refusal keeps curl's exact line. | Superseded by 0769 | 2026-10-22 |
| [0763](ADR-0763-llms-txt-and-the-agent-pages-are-published-by-a-site-pages-job.md) | A `site-pages` job in gource.yml publishes llms.txt, download/, mcp/ and ai-help/ onto the gource branch; download/ and ai-help/ are made from the preview release (BL-3148) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0764](ADR-0764-a-write-failure-keeps-the-transfer-s-report-for-write-out.md) | A write failure (exit 23) on standard output or an `-o` file keeps the handler's report, so `-w` prints the transfer's real values, as curl 8.22.0 does (BL-3170) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0765](ADR-0765-ftp-takes-x-as-its-listing-command-from-httprequestoptions.md) | The FTP handler sends `-X` (`HttpRequestOptions.CustomMethod`) in place of `LIST`/`NLST` for a listing, never for a file download (BL-3165) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0766](ADR-0766-an-unhandled-exception-ends-the-run-with-exit-2.md) | Port 0 fails the connect with exit 7 as curl 8.22.0 does, and any exception escaping the run ends it with `curl: (2) <message>` and exit 2 (BL-3172) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0767](ADR-0767-an-empty-guessed-port-leaves-the-url-without-a-scheme.md) | A URL typed without a scheme whose authority ends in an empty port is left without one, so the parser rejects it with exit 3 as curl 8.22.0 does (BL-3175) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0768](ADR-0768-parallel-transfers-start-in-curl-s-measured-order-not-url-order.md) | `-Z` transfers to one host start in curl 8.22.0's measured order (the first waiter behind those starting with it), deterministically, not in URL order (BL-3178) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0769](ADR-0769-bare-curl-prints-curls-exact-try-help-line.md) | Bare `curl` prints curl's exact try-help line and exits 2; `--ai-help` is named only in `curl --help`. Supersedes 0762. | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0770](ADR-0770-a-data-connection-ends-its-send-side-before-it-closes.md) | `IConnection.ShutDownSendingAsync` sends TLS `close_notify` and a TCP FIN after discarding unread bytes, and FTP calls it on a data connection before closing it (BL-3179) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0771](ADR-0771-the-transfer-tool-checks-argument-shapes-before-binding.md) | Curl.Mcp's `transfer` tool checks its raw arguments' JSON shapes in a call-tool filter before the SDK binds them, refusing a wrong shape naming the field and the shape it must have (BL-3188) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0772](ADR-0772-blank-option-arguments-follow-curl-8-22-0.md) | Blank option arguments are accepted or refused as curl 8.22.0 does; the c-ares and ECH options refuse a blank one as a build that has them does (BL-3183) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0773](ADR-0773-a-postfieldsize-past-the-data-sends-the-data-alone.md) | `CURLOPT_POSTFIELDSIZE` cuts the `CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS` body to that many bytes; a size past the data sends the data alone (BL-3190) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0774](ADR-0774-a-failed-transfer-keeps-the-response-it-reached.md) | A failed HTTP transfer keeps the response code, version, header size and peer it reached, and `PRIMARY_PORT` is -1 after a failure that reached no peer, as curl 8.22.0 (BL-3195) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0775](ADR-0775-the-posix-password-prompt-reads-dev-tty-and-writes-the-lf-only-when-echo-was-off.md) | Off Windows the `-u` password prompt reads `/dev/tty` (else standard input), turns echo off with `tcsetattr`, and writes the LF after the password only when echo was off, as curl 8.22.0 (BL-3200) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0776](ADR-0776-url-get-with-urldecode-stays-a-string.md) | `curl_url_get` with `CURLU_URLDECODE` keeps its `string` form, with no `byte[]` overload; raw bytes come from the undecoded get (BL-3197) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0777](ADR-0777-curlopt-accept-encoding-is-sent-exactly-as-given.md) | `CURLOPT_ACCEPT_ENCODING` is sent exactly as given; only the empty string sends every supported encoding (BL-3191) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0778](ADR-0778-ipv6-sockets-are-dual-stack-so-mapped-ipv4-literals-connect.md) | `TcpDialer` makes every IPv6 socket dual-stack, so an IPv4-mapped literal such as `[::ffff:127.0.0.1]` reaches an IPv4 server as in curl 8.22.0 (BL-3174) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0779](ADR-0779-curlopt-nobody-keeps-header-lines-out-of-the-body-except-ftps.md) | `CURLOPT_NOBODY` alone sends header lines to the write callback only for FTP, whose `-I` lines curl 8.22.0 always writes there; `CURLOPT_HEADER` sends every scheme's (BL-3199) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0780](ADR-0780-content-and-transfer-decoding-turn-off-separately.md) | `CURLOPT_HTTP_CONTENT_DECODING` and `CURLOPT_HTTP_TRANSFER_DECODING` each turn their own decoding off (BL-3192) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0781](ADR-0781-timecondition-lastmod-sends-last-modified.md) | `CURLOPT_TIMECONDITION` `CURL_TIMECOND_LASTMOD` sends the time as `Last-Modified` and compares as if-modified-since (BL-3198) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0782](ADR-0782-differential-conformance-runner-runs-on-demand-from-an-integration-tests-project.md) | The differential conformance runner's core is `Curl.Conformance.Differential.UnitLibrary`; it runs real binaries on demand from an IntegrationTests project, not in CI, with the reference build's faults as named exclusions (BL-3231) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0783](ADR-0783-multi-wait-skips-a-passed-transfer-timeout.md) | A multi wait or poll leaves out a transfer timeout that has already passed, waiting for the transfer's perform to end with exit 28, so a perform-and-poll loop never spins (BL-3206) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0784](ADR-0784-differential-responder-compresses-with-the-bcl-and-pins-framing.md) | The differential responder compresses with `GZipStream`/`ZLibStream`/`DeflateStream`, sets Python's gzip header bytes, and pins the framing and the decompressed content rather than the deflate bytes (BL-3246) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0785](ADR-0785-differential-case-files-give-bytes-as-base64-or-a-repeated-string.md) | A differential case file gives `stdin` and `files` content as UTF-8 text, `{"base64"}` or `{"repeat", "count"}`, and every `Cases/**/*.json` is embedded and read by path with `DifferentialCaseFileReader.ReadEmbedded` (BL-3259) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0786](ADR-0786-z-order-keep-alive-extension-first-waiter-last-only-after-the-first-connection-closed.md) | The -Z order rule extends to keep-alive: the first waiter on a host goes last only when the host's first connection was closed; otherwise URL order (BL-3255, amends 0768) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0787](ADR-0787-getinfo-lengths-condition-and-effective-url-under-curlopt-port.md) | `CURLINFO_CONTENT_LENGTH_DOWNLOAD_T` -1 for chunked, differing lengths and FAILONERROR, 0 for 204 and 304 with a length; `CONDITION_UNMET` 1 for every 304; `file://` file time and length; the effective URL keeps a `CURLOPT_URL`'s own port under `CURLOPT_PORT` (BL-3194, amends 0672) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0788](ADR-0788-http2-status-line-over-http1-refused-on-every-platform.md) | An `HTTP/2 ddd` status line over HTTP/1.x fails with exit 8 on Windows too, now Windows runs HTTP/2 (measured, upstream test1704, BL-3270, amends 0679) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0789](ADR-0789-each-library-easy-and-multi-handle-runs-over-caches-of-its-own.md) | Each library easy handle and each multi handle runs over caches of its own; a share handle links only the kinds it shares, the curl tool keeps the engine-wide caches (BL-3271, amends 0542, 0594, 0606) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0790](ADR-0790-curl-api-differential-cases-run-in-process-beside-equivalent-command-lines.md) | `Curl.Api` differential cases run in process beside their equivalent curl command lines (`-A '' -w '\n%{response_code}'` first), compared on wire bytes, body, response code and exit code (BL-3242) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0791](ADR-0791-differential-runner-replays-libcurl-scenarios-against-a-native-reference-library.md) | The differential runner replays `scen.json` against Curl's Libcurl and a reference libcurl loaded with `NativeLibrary` from `CURL_DIFFERENTIAL_REFERENCE_LIBCURL`; non-ASCII hosts are excluded as `BrokenIdn` (BL-3236) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0792](ADR-0792-differential-runner-calls-the-reference-libcurl-through-function-pointers.md) | The differential runner calls the reference libcurl through function pointers, in process beside Curl's Libcurl (BL-3237) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0793](ADR-0793-differential-runner-replays-libcurl-multi-and-share-cases-through-one-target-interface.md) | The differential runner replays Libcurl multi and share cases through one target interface (BL-3238) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0794](ADR-0794-a-p12-client-certificate-is-presented-where-this-windows-builds-schannel-curl-fails-to-acquire-it.md) | A P12 client certificate is presented on Windows where this Windows build's Schannel curl fails every P12 with exit 35 `SEC_E_UNKNOWN_CREDENTIALS`; failures curl itself makes still match (measured, BL-3290) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0795](ADR-0795-the-schannel-build-ignores-cert-status.md) | The Schannel build ignores `--cert-status`, as curl 8.22.0 Schannel does: no stapled-status check and no hand-built route on Windows; supersedes the Schannel part of ADR-0191 (measured, BL-3291) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0796](ADR-0796-schannel-illegal-message-and-protocol-version-refusal-text.md) | The Schannel build writes curl's fatal-alert text for every `SEC_E_ILLEGAL_MESSAGE`, and `SEC_E_UNSUPPORTED_FUNCTION` for a `protocol_version` refusal under `--tls-max 1.2` (measured, BL-3292) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0797](ADR-0797-schannel-cacert-leaf-is-a-trust-anchor.md) | The Schannel build takes a server certificate listed in `--cacert` as a trust anchor, so a file holding only the leaf verifies it (measured, BL-3289) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0798](ADR-0798-openssl-cacert-certificates-are-partial-chain-anchors.md) | The OpenSSL build takes any certificate of the chain listed in `--cacert` as a trust anchor (measured, BL-3335) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0799](ADR-0799-curl-mcp-drops-its-two-packages-for-a-hand-written-server.md) | `Curl.Mcp` drops `ModelContextProtocol` and `Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting` for a hand-written MCP server: a `Curl.Mcp.Protocol.UnitLibrary` core, hand-schema tools, a hand-written host, golden-conversation parity (BL-3373, plan BL-3374..BL-3389) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0800](ADR-0800-json-rpc-parser-rules-of-the-hand-written-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written MCP server's JSON-RPC parser: parse error for non-UTF-8 or non-JSON, invalid request for non-objects, null or non-scalar ids and bad `jsonrpc`/`method`/`params`, ids echoed byte for byte, null `params` as absent, last duplicate key wins (BL-3376) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0801](ADR-0801-tool-and-resource-model-of-the-hand-written-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written MCP server's tool and resource model: wire shapes in golden-file order with optional properties omitted when null, raw-JSON input schemas validated as `type: object` at registration, duplicate tool names and resource URIs refused with `ArgumentException` (BL-3378) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0802](ADR-0802-upstream-case-836-stays-off-the-ratchet-its-uid-fetch-is-stale.md) | Upstream case 836 stays off the ratchet: curl 8.22.0 sends `UID FETCH 2 BODY[]` for `;UID=2`, as Curl does, where the case expects `FETCH 2 BODY[]`; amends ADR-0737 (BL-3398) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0803](ADR-0803-upstream-source-analysis-perl-cases-are-interpreted-over-the-vendored-upstream-files.md) | Upstream's source-analysis perl cases 1140, 1165, 1169, 1173, 1182, 1185 and 1275 are interpreted over the vendored upstream files; supersedes the no-counterpart rows of ADR-0718 and ADR-0755 for 745, 1135, 1167, 1276, 1279 and 1544 (BL-3406) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0804](ADR-0804-the-hand-written-transfer-tool-reads-a-missing-or-null-field-as-omitted.md) | The hand-written `transfer` tool reads a missing or `null` field as omitted: a missing `url` is refused as `Invalid url: it is empty.` and `"method": null` sends a GET (BL-3384) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0805](ADR-0805-hand-written-stdio-host-of-the-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written stdio host of the MCP server: configuration read before any input, SIGINT, SIGQUIT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP through `PosixSignalRegistration`, a 30 second shutdown timeout on the injected `TimeProvider`, exit 0 on a clean stop and 1 on a read failure or timeout, timestamped log lines to standard error only (BL-3382) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0806](ADR-0806-a-library-handles-pipewait-waits-in-the-connection-pool.md) | A library handle's `CURLOPT_PIPEWAIT` sets its pool's `WaitsForMultiplexing` (`EngineToolOptions.Parallel`, `ParallelImmediate` false), as the curl tool's `-Z` without `--parallel-immediate` does (BL-3351) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0807](ADR-0807-checksrc-pl-puts-the-d-directory-before-every-path-not-starting-dot-slash.md) | The C# `checksrc.pl` (case 1185) puts the `-D` directory before every path not starting `./` or `../`, absolute ones too, strips CR LF or LF on every platform, and never warns `COPYRIGHTYEAR` (BL-3419) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0808](ADR-0808-windows-features-line-omits-http2-and-httpsrr.md) | The Windows `curl -V` Features line lists neither `HTTP2` nor `HTTPSRR`, as the Schannel reference build does not, though `--http2` and HTTPS records still work there; supersedes ADR-0871's Features-line part (BL-3359) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0809](ADR-0809-http2-keeps-running-on-windows-against-gf-0098.md) | `--http2` keeps running on Windows (ADR-0871) though the nghttp2-less Schannel reference refuses it with exit 2; GF-0098 (test1704) is left to Stewart and the gap measurement (BL-3431) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0810](ADR-0810-conformance-cases-split-into-original-upstream-and-extended-ours.md) | Conformance cases split into `CurlTestCases/Original` (upstream `tests/data`) and `CurlTestCases/Extended` (our own `ext<number>.curltest`); the upstream count and ratchet see only `Original` (BL-3462) | Accepted | 2026-10-24 |
| [0811](ADR-0811-ci-watch-reads-running-runs-and-cancels-a-run-without-the-fix.md) | The CI watch reads a running run's failed jobs before the run ends, and while CI is red cancels a running run whose commit lacks the CI fix the waiting run carries (BL-3454, BL-3456) | Accepted | 2026-10-24 |
| [0812](ADR-0812-a-run-that-only-finds-prerequisites-does-not-spend-the-cost-cap.md) | A run that sends its task back to Backlog waiting on an open `depends-on` ID, with no work to keep, does not come off the task's cost cap (`Register-PrerequisiteRequeueRun`); amends AF-0095 (BL-3364, BL-3451, BL-3452, BL-3453) | Accepted | 2026-10-24 |
| [0813](ADR-0813-a-lane-ends-when-only-interactive-work-is-left.md) | A lane with nothing ready waits only while a lane-eligible task is in Doing, so it ends when only interactive work is left, naming it in an `idle` trace line; `Test-InteractiveOnlyTask` also counts audit-path and guard-file touches, read from `task-board.ps1`'s `Test-AuditPath` (BL-3500, AF-0435) | Accepted | 2026-10-25 |
| [0814](ADR-0814-the-shift-end-merge-pull-request-runs-no-duplicate-ci.md) | The shift-end merge's pull request (head `factory/merge-*`) skips CI's build, test, pack and quality jobs and the Integration tests job, which duplicate its head's passing `work/dark-factory` run and took runners from the next one; `work/dark-factory` still never cancels a running run (BL-3512) | Accepted | 2026-10-25 |
| [0815](ADR-0815-a-failed-standard-error-write-is-ignored.md) | Standard error goes through `StandardErrorFailureIgnoringStream`, which ignores an `IOException` from a write or flush as curl ignores a failed `fputs` to `stderr`, so `2>/dev/full` no longer aborts the process (CGR-008, BL-3537) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0816](ADR-0816-curl-multi-cleanup-inside-a-transfer-callback-is-refused.md) | `curl_multi_cleanup` from inside a transfer callback answers `CURLM_RECURSIVE_API_CALL` and does nothing (`CurlMulti.CleanupAsync`, `IMultiTransfer.IsInTransferCallback` from an `AsyncLocal` the multi perform sets), so it no longer deadlocks (CGR-012, BL-3541) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0817](ADR-0817-ciphers-is-expanded-as-an-openssl-cipher-string.md) | The OpenSSL build expands `--ciphers` as OpenSSL's `SSL_CTX_set_cipher_list` does (`OpenSslCipherString`): `DEFAULT`, aliases such as `HIGH` and `aRSA` joined by `+`, the `!`, `-` and `+` prefixes and `@STRENGTH`/`@SECLEVEL=n`, over the suite table in OpenSSL 3.5.5's `ALL` order; amends ADR-0011 (BL-3552, CGR-023) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0818](ADR-0818-integrate-renumbers-a-lanes-new-adr-another-lane-took-first.md) | Integrating renumbers an ADR a lane added under a number the shared branch already uses, with its file name, heading, README row and citations on the lane's added lines; base ADRs keep theirs (`DarkFactory/AdrIds.ps1`; BL-3834) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0929](ADR-0929-windows-http2-options-keep-running-under-adr-0871-despite-cgr-040.md) | `--http2`, `--http2-prior-knowledge` and `--proxy-http2` keep running on Windows under Stewart's ADR-0871 rather than being refused as CGR-040 asks; Curl matches the nghttp2 build there, and the 101-then-HTTP/1 exit code (16) is BL-3650's (BL-3569) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0820](ADR-0820-iconnection-exposes-the-operating-system-socket-handle.md) | `IConnection.SocketHandle` gives the operating system socket handle a connection runs on (`StreamConnection` its `NetworkStream`'s, every wrapper its inner connection's, the proxy's through a tunnel), amending ADR-0610 for this read-only member (BL-3846, CGR-034) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0821](ADR-0821-last-modified-unreadable-is-the-epoch-one-second-before-is-no-time.md) | An unreadable `Last-Modified` is the epoch (`-R` stamps 1970-01-01, `-z` is met) and `Wed, 31 Dec 1969 23:59:59 GMT` is -1, no file time for `-R`, as curl 8.22.0 measures (CGR-047, BL-3576) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0822](ADR-0822-write-out-writes-escaped-invalid-utf-8-bytes-as-their-bytes.md) | `-w` writes each lone surrogate U+DC80 to U+DCFF (an argument byte that was not valid UTF-8) as that byte, on every platform, so `-w $'\xfe'` writes FE as curl 8.18.0 does on Linux (BL-3852, CGR-016) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0823](ADR-0823-x-method-and-b-strings-go-out-as-their-argv-bytes.md) | A `-X` method and the `-b name=value` strings go out as their bytes in the platform's argument encoding, so on Linux `-X 中` and `-b c=中` send the UTF-8 bytes as curl 8.18.0 does (`HttpRequestHeadFormatter`, `CookieEngine.FromHttpOptions`; BL-3594, CGR-065) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0824](ADR-0824-gzip-and-zlib-trailer-failures-write-the-decoded-bytes-and-name-the-check.md) | A gzip or zlib trailer that fails its check has every decoded byte written first, then exit 61 with zlib's `incorrect data check` or `incorrect length check`, as curl 8.22.0 does: the trailer is fed to the BCL stream alone and the check named from where it failed (`HttpContentCodingDecoder`, `HttpContentChecksumTrailer`; BL-3600, CGR-071) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0825](ADR-0825-the-proxy-urls-user-information-wins-over-proxy-user.md) | The proxy URL's user information wins over `-U`/`--proxy-user` and the library's proxy credential options, which are used only for a proxy URL without user information, as curl 8.22.0's `parse_proxy` does (`TransferProxySelection`; BL-3608, CGR-079) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0826](ADR-0826-cgr-096s-p12-client-certificate-difference-is-kept-as-adr-0794-decided.md) | CGR-096's difference is kept: on Windows Curl presents a P12 client certificate where Schannel curl fails with exit 35, whose failing call and text depend on the Windows build (measured, BL-3625) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0827](ADR-0827-the-openssl-build-raises-a-legacy-tls-minimum-to-tls-1-2.md) | The OpenSSL build raises a `--tlsv1.0` or `--tlsv1.1` minimum under a TLS 1.2 or later ceiling to TLS 1.2, as OpenSSL 3's default security level does, unless `--ciphers` ends at `@SECLEVEL=0` (`HandBuiltTlsProvider.WithSecurityLevelMinimum`; BL-3623, CGR-094) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0828](ADR-0828-the-windows-password-prompt-reads-the-console-input-buffer.md) | On Windows the `-u`/`--proxy-user` password prompt reads the console input buffer (`CONIN$`) with every input mode off, as curl 8.22.0's `_getch`, never a redirected standard input (CGR-044, BL-3573) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0829](ADR-0829-a-redirect-location-is-reported-as-curls-normalised-url.md) | A redirect `Location` is reported, and followed, as curl's URL parser rebuilds it: the host after a single slash (`http:/b` is `http://b/`), `%2E` dot segments removed, a Windows drive-letter `file` path as `file://c:/x`, and `http:` refused for its slashes (BL-3591, CGR-062) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0830](ADR-0830-write-out-writes-command-line-text-in-the-ansi-code-page-on-windows.md) | `-w` writes its literal text and command-line values in the system ANSI code page on Windows and UTF-8 elsewhere (`WriteOutTemplateRenderer`'s text encoding); a `-w @file` and a `-w` in a UTF-8 config file are re-spelled so their bytes come out unchanged (BL-3841, CGR-015) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0831](ADR-0831-the-socket-callbacks-run-before-each-tcp-attempt.md) | The open-socket and socket-option callbacks run before each TCP attempt through `SocketCallbackConnector.AttemptSocketOpener`: `CURL_SOCKET_BAD` makes no connection (exit 7), the returned socket is the one connected (as it is under `CURL_SOCKOPT_ALREADY_CONNECTED`), and a failed attempt's socket goes to the close-socket callback (BL-3565, CGR-036) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0832](ADR-0832-a-dialer-publishes-each-socket-it-creates-before-connecting-it.md) | A dialer publishes each socket it creates before connecting it (`ITcpDialer.PublishingSocketsTo`), so a socket-action transfer waits `CURL_POLL_OUT` on its real socket while it connects (`TransferSocketWait.ConnectingOn`; BL-3890, CGR-034) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0833](ADR-0833-imap-holds-its-greeting-and-login-to-the-connect-timeout.md) | IMAP holds its greeting, `CAPABILITY`, `STARTTLS` and login to `--connect-timeout` (300 s by default) from the start of the request, ending with exit 28 `Operation timed out after N milliseconds with 0 bytes received` as curl 8.22.0 does (`ImapConnectPhaseLimit`; BL-3882, CGR-003) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0834](ADR-0834-the-schannel-build-lets-a-stapled-ocsp-response-answer-a-cacert-chains-revocation.md) | The Schannel build runs a revocation-checked `--cacert` connection on the hand-built client and lets a good stapled OCSP response answer an unknown revocation status and a revoked one fail it, exit 60 (`TlsClientRouting.ReasonInBuild`, `ServerCertificateVerification.Judge`; BL-3543, CGR-014) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0835](ADR-0835-smb-and-smbs-are-disabled-as-in-the-reference-builds.md) | `smb://` and `smbs://` fail with exit 1 and `Protocol "smb" is disabled` before connecting, as curl 8.22.0's reference builds have no SMB; `--proto` and `--proto-default` refusing both names follows in BL-3888 and BL-3889 (CGR-043, BL-3887) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0836](ADR-0836-the-openssl-build-reads-a-capath-directory-by-subject-hash-file-names.md) | The OpenSSL build reads `--capath` and `SSL_CERT_DIR` as OpenSSL's hashed directory lookup does: a list split at the path separator, only `<subject hash>.<n>` files from `.0` up, only certificates of that subject hash (`OpenSslSubjectNameHash`; BL-3640, CGR-111) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0837](ADR-0837-http2-against-an-http1-peer-exits-16.md) | HTTP/2 (prior knowledge or the h2c upgrade) against a peer that answers in HTTP/1.1 exits 16 with nghttp2's "expected SETTINGS frame" text, as curl 8.18.0 does whenever the server keeps the connection open and as upstream test1801 expects; replaces BL-3372's exit 55 (`Http2Session.ReadAndRouteFrameAsync`; BL-3650, CGR-121) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0838](ADR-0838-windows-reads-the-command-line-through-the-ansi-code-page.md) | On Windows each command-line argument is read through the system ANSI code page with best fit (`AnsiCodePageArguments`), as the ANSI-argv reference curl reads it (CGR-015, BL-3892) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0839](ADR-0839-a-file-path-holding-raw-bytes-opens-under-those-bytes-off-windows.md) | Off Windows `PhysicalFileSystem` opens a read path holding bytes that are not UTF-8 (`RawBytePath`: each such byte kept as U+DC00 + byte) under those raw bytes with the runtime's `open` shim, as curl's `open()` does; a missing one is still `NotFound` (CGR-114, BL-3899) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0840](ADR-0840-a-refused-command-line-echoes-argument-text-in-the-ansi-code-page-on-windows.md) | On Windows a refused command line's lines echo argument text in the system ANSI code page; a refused config file line stays UTF-8 (CGR-015, BL-3893) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
