| [0841](ADR-0841-dns-options-refused-on-every-reference-build.md) | `--dns-servers`, `--dns-interface`, `--dns-ipv4-addr` and `--dns-ipv6-addr` are refused with exit 2 on every platform unless the parse reads as a c-ares build (`ActsAsCAresBuild`), as every reference build refuses them; supersedes ADR-0454 decision 2 for them (CGR-041, BL-3570) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0842](ADR-0842-the-socket-callbacks-and-curlinfo-activesocket-give-the-operating-system-socket.md) | `CURLINFO_ACTIVESOCKET`/`LASTSOCKET` and, without an open-socket callback, the close-socket callback's argument are the connection's operating system socket (`IConnection.SocketHandle`), the callback running before the engine closes it; in-memory connections keep engine numbers (CGR-129, BL-3658) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0843](ADR-0843-curlinfo-content-length-upload-is-the-last-http-requests-body-size.md) | `CURLINFO_CONTENT_LENGTH_UPLOAD` after an `http` request is that last request's body size: 0 with no body or after a redirect to `GET`, -1 when sent chunked (CGR-127, BL-3656) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0844](ADR-0844-sftp-and-scp-header-lines-never-go-to-the-body-output.md) | `-i`, `-I` and `CURLOPT_HEADER` never write an `sftp` or `scp` transfer's header lines (the `-Q pwd` and `statvfs` output) to the body output; they go only to `-D` (CGR-132, BL-3661) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0845](ADR-0845-the-socket-option-callback-gets-an-operating-system-socket-opened-before-the-connect.md) | Without an open-socket callback, the socket-option callback gets a TCP socket the engine opens before the connect and hands the dialer, unconnected under `CURL_SOCKOPT_ALREADY_CONNECTED` so the first send fails with exit 55; the engine closes it after the close-socket callback (CGR-145, BL-3674) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0846](ADR-0846-windows-file-names-from-the-command-line-and-config-files-take-the-ansi-code-page.md) | On Windows `-o` and `-T` names take the ANSI code page's best-fit text and a UTF-8 config file's `output` is re-spelled to its bytes read in that code page, as the mingw curl 8.22.0 opens them (`cafÃ©.bin`) (CGR-114, BL-3900) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0847](ADR-0847-http2-responses-whose-length-does-not-add-up-shut-the-connection-down.md) | An HTTP/2 response whose DATA does not add up to its `content-length`, a body on HEAD, 204 or 304, a 1xx head ending the stream, or an invalid or repeated `content-length` sends GOAWAY PROTOCOL_ERROR and exits 16 as nghttp2 does, even with `--ignore-content-length` (CGR-138, BL-3667) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0848](ADR-0848-windows-reads-at-stdin-and-telnet-stdin-in-text-mode.md) | On Windows, standard input for `-d`, `--data-ascii`, `--json`, `--variable`, `-H`, `--proxy-header` and `-w` `@-`, and a `telnet` transfer's standard input without `-T`, is read in text mode (Ctrl-Z ends it, CR LF reads as LF), as curl 8.22.0's Windows build reads it (CGR-112, BL-3641) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0849](ADR-0849-the-schannel-build-ignores-doh-cert-status.md) | The Schannel build ignores `--doh-cert-status` as it ignores `--cert-status`, so a DoH server stapling no OCSP response still resolves the name on Windows (CGR-154, BL-3683) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0850](ADR-0850-multi-and-easy-calls-from-inside-a-transfer-callback-are-refused.md) | A multi call from inside one of the handle's transfer callbacks answers `CURLM_RECURSIVE_API_CALL` and does nothing (`curl_multi_info_read` no message, `curl_multi_wakeup` still `CURLM_OK`), and `curl_easy_perform` of the handle whose callback runs answers `CURLE_RECURSIVE_API_CALL`, as libcurl 8.22.0 (CGR-146, BL-3675) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0851](ADR-0851-each-library-easy-and-multi-handle-resolves-names-in-a-dns-cache-of-its-own.md) | Each library easy handle and multi handle resolves names in a DNS cache of its own, so a new handle's resolver-start callback runs and its abort answers 42; test1512 comes off the ratchet until BL-3917 (CGR-151, BL-3680, amends 0789) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0852](ADR-0852-resolve-entries-answer-for-the-doh-servers-own-name.md) | `--resolve` entries answer for the DoH server's own host and port, loaded silently into the DoH connector's DNS cache, as curl 8.22.0's DoH probes find them in the shared cache (CGR-155, BL-3684) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0853](ADR-0853-doh-url-user-and-password-send-basic-authorization.md) | A user name or password in the `--doh-url` sends `Authorization: Basic` (percent-decoded `user:password`) after `Host` on each DoH POST, none when both are empty, as curl 8.21.0 does (CGR-156, BL-3685) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0854](ADR-0854-a-close-delimited-doh-response-is-read-to-the-close.md) | A DoH response framed by neither `Content-Length` nor chunked coding is read to the close and decoded, as curl 8.22.0 reads it for HTTP/1.0 and HTTP/1.1; a TLS close without `close_notify` still fails (CGR-160, BL-3689) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0855](ADR-0855-a-parallel-transfer-waiting-for-its-host-runs-its-max-time.md) | Under `-Z` a transfer waiting for its host's first connection runs its `-m` from when its batch was added and, once it passes, times out with exit 28 and `with 0 bytes received` without connecting, after the host's first transfer ends (CGR-109, BL-3638) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0856](ADR-0856-windows-doh-sends-no-https-rr-query.md) | On Windows `--doh-url` sends no HTTPS-RR (type 65) query for an `https://` origin, as the Schannel build has no HTTPSRR (`TcpConnector.AsksHttpsRecords`); the harness reports `HTTPSRR` on Linux and macOS only (CGR-157, BL-3686) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0857](ADR-0857-doh-names-that-cannot-be-encoded-exit-27.md) | A host name the DoH resolver cannot put in a query (an empty label, one over 63 bytes, or a query over 272 bytes) exits 27 `Failed to encode DoH packet [1]` or `[13]` and sends nothing, as curl 8.22.0 does (CGR-158, BL-3687) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0858](ADR-0858-curl-easy-perform-of-a-handle-in-a-multi-handle-answers-failed-init.md) | `curl_easy_perform` of an easy handle that is in a multi handle answers `CURLE_FAILED_INIT` with the error text `easy handle already used in multi handle` and sends nothing, as libcurl 8.22.0 (CGR-147, BL-3676) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0859](ADR-0859-release-build-uses-only-an-https-doh-url.md) | The release build uses only an `https` DoH URL: an `http` or scheme-less `--doh-url` resolves nothing (exit 6) and never reaches the server; the harness keeps the debug build's `http` (CGR-163, BL-3692) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0860](ADR-0860-tls-sessions-resume-only-between-matching-verification-settings.md) | An `SslStream` handshake resumes a TLS session only when the first handshake to the host had the same verification settings (`-k`, CA file, blob and directory, `--pinnedpubkey`, `--cert-status`, client certificate); otherwise `AllowTlsResume` is off, as curl makes a new session (CGR-164, BL-3693) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0861](ADR-0861-an-auth-secured-ftp-control-connection-is-kept-in-tls-and-a-ccc-cleared-one-is-not-kept.md) | An FTP control connection `AUTH` secured is kept in TLS for the next URL (`FtpKeptConnection.SecuredControl`); one `CCC` cleared is never kept and ends with `QUIT` (CGR-165, BL-3694) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0862](ADR-0862-cert-status-under-insecure-trusts-no-ocsp-responder.md) | `--cert-status` under `-k` trusts no OCSP responder: in the OpenSSL build every stapled response that reads as successful is exit 91 `OCSP response verification failed`, as curl 8.18.0 on OpenSSL 3.5.5 (CGR-167, BL-3696) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0863](ADR-0863-perform-never-calls-the-socket-callback-and-a-dead-multi-handle-calls-no-callback.md) | `curl_multi_perform` never calls the socket callback, and once a socket or timer callback answers -1 every later socket action answers 0 with the transfers still running, no message and no callback, while a later perform ends them with 42 and calls no callback, as libcurl 8.22.0 (CGR-148, BL-3677, amends 0599) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0864](ADR-0864-file-url-escapes-are-read-in-the-ansi-code-page-on-windows-and-as-raw-bytes-elsewhere.md) | A `file://` URL's `%XX` escapes are read in the system ANSI code page on Windows and as raw bytes elsewhere, as curl 8.22.0 (non-Unicode, Schannel) and curl 8.18.0 (Linux) read them (CGR-114, BL-3643) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0865](ADR-0865-an-ssh-session-is-kept-on-its-connection-for-the-next-url-of-the-same-login.md) | An `scp`/`sftp` session is kept on its connection after a successful transfer and reused, with its SFTP session and home directory, for the next URL of the same user, password and key files; the cache's close sends the channel close and `DISCONNECT` 11 (CGR-130, BL-3659) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0866](ADR-0866-off-windows-curl-never-touches-the-dotnet-terminfo-console.md) | Off Windows Curl never reads `Console.WindowWidth` or writes through a .NET console stream, either of which writes `ESC[?1h ESC=` to the terminal: the width is `ioctl(TIOCGWINSZ)` on standard input through the runtime shim, and a terminal standard output and standard error are written over descriptors 1 and 2 (CGR-115, BL-3644) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0867](ADR-0867-range-appends-a-dash-after-any-leading-number-and-sftp-reads-the-range-text.md) | `-r` appends a dash after any leading number that is not before a dash, and an `sftp://` download reads the `-r` text as `Curl_ssh_range` does, so text after the first range is exit 33, as curl 8.22.0 (CGR-131, BL-3660) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0868](ADR-0868-output-files-are-opened-shared-for-reading-and-writing.md) | Every write open of an output file shares it for reading and writing, as the C runtime's `fopen` does on Windows, so two `-Z` transfers writing one `-o` file both write it (CGR-174, BL-3703) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0869](ADR-0869-standard-error-is-the-null-stream-when-descriptor-2-was-not-inherited.md) | Off Windows standard error is `Stream.Null` when descriptor 2 is closed or close-on-exec (opened by the .NET runtime after the shell closed it, `2>&-`), as curl's `main_checkfds` sends it to `/dev/null`; checked through `SystemNative_FcntlGetFD` (BL-3824) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0870](ADR-0870-standard-output-is-closed-when-descriptor-1-was-not-inherited.md) | Off Windows standard output is closed (`ClosedStandardOutputStream`, so a write fails with curl's exit 23) when descriptor 1 is closed or close-on-exec (`1>&-`, opened by the .NET runtime), as curl's write to its read-only `/dev/null` fails (BL-3936) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0930](ADR-0930-schannel-build-checks-revocation-against-the-system-store.md) | The Schannel build checks revocation online against the system store; a revoked chain is exit 35 `CRYPT_E_REVOKED` (BL-3215) | Accepted | 2026-10-22 |
| [0931](ADR-0931-library-handle-starts-the-low-speed-watchdog.md) | A library handle starts the low-speed watchdog, so `CURLOPT_LOW_SPEED_LIMIT`/`TIME` end a slow transfer with 28 (BL-3196) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0932](ADR-0932-differential-host-runs-the-built-curl-console-on-port-18080.md) | The differential host runs the built `Curl.Console` (or `CURL_DIFFERENTIAL_OURS`) against a loopback server on 18080 with control on 18081, following `runner.py`'s per-run rules (BL-3260) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0871](ADR-0871-windows-build-runs-http2.md) | The Windows build runs `--http2`, `--http2-prior-knowledge` and `--proxy-http2` and lists `HTTP2` in `curl -V`; supersedes ADR-0397's HTTP/2 part (Stewart's decision, BL-3207) | Accepted |
| [0872](ADR-0872-curl-api-request-removes-a-default-header-through-removed-headers.md) | A `Curl.Api` request removes a default header through `CurlRequest.RemovedHeaders`, sent as `Name:` (BL-3294) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0873](ADR-0873-sha-digest-fails-with-exit-94-where-the-sspi-build-is-matched.md) | A SHA-256 or SHA-512-256 Digest challenge fails with exit 94 where the Schannel build's SSPI is matched (Windows), as SSPI answers only MD5 and MD5-sess (measured, BL-3281) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0874](ADR-0874-the-schannel-build-ignores-curves-and-sigalgs.md) | The Schannel build ignores `--curves` and `--sigalgs`, whatever their value, as curl 8.22.0 Schannel does: no exit 59 or 35, the Schannel profile's hello and Schannel's handshake-failure text; supersedes the Schannel part of ADR-0151 and ADR-0284 (measured, BL-3305) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0875](ADR-0875-digest-problem-line-follows-any-answer-under-several-allowed-schemes.md) | The `Digest authentication problem, ignoring.` line follows any answer sent under several allowed schemes, such as `--anyauth` (BL-3307) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0876](ADR-0876-differential-runner-replays-printf-scenarios-and-skips-long-double-floats-against-the-reference.md) | The differential runner replays printf scenarios and does not run long double float conversions against the reference, which crashes on them (BL-3311) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0877](ADR-0877-curl-url-get-bytes-gives-the-c-abis-raw-bytes.md) | `CurlUrlHandle.GetBytes` gives `curl_url_get`'s part as the C ABI's raw bytes, a decoded `%FF` the byte 0xFF, over ADR-0697's mapping (BL-3299) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0878](ADR-0878-an-abort-after-the-peers-fin-at-the-write-stays-an-abort.md) | A Windows read aborted after a write that found the peer's FIN unread (a kept connection the server closed, then reused) stays `Connection was aborted`, as curl 8.22.0 Schannel prints it; amends ADR-0419 and ADR-0708 (measured, BL-3304) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0879](ADR-0879-stdio-transport-rules-of-the-hand-written-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written MCP server's stdio transport: blank lines skipped, `\r\n` and an unterminated last line accepted, a 4 MiB line limit read as an invalid request, writes flushed whole under a lock with no BOM (BL-3377) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0880](ADR-0880-upstream-case-1211-stays-off-the-ratchet-because-curl-8-22-0-sends-quit-and-exits-10.md) | Upstream case 1211 stays off the ratchet: the reference curl 8.22.0 sends the same commands as Curl, `QUIT` included, and exits 10 where the disabled case expects no `QUIT` and exit 28; amends ADR-0737 (BL-3400) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0881](ADR-0881-upstream-case-938-stays-off-the-ratchet-its-expected-authzid-is-stale.md) | Upstream case 938 stays off the ratchet: it expects `user.one\0user.one\0secret` as the `AUTH PLAIN` message, while curl 8.22.0 and Curl both send `\0user.one\0secret` and `\0user.two\0secret` on its two connections; amends ADR-0737's row for 938 (BL-3399) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0882](ADR-0882-upstream-case-594-stays-off-the-ratchet-because-libcurl-8-22-0-takes-68-seconds-and-exits-12.md) | Upstream case 594 stays off the ratchet: libcurl 8.22.0 with lib591's options waits the 8 second accept timeout and a minute more, then exits 12 with no `QUIT` after 68 seconds, as Curl does, where the disabled case expects `QUIT` and exit 28 within 20 seconds; amends ADR-0737 (BL-3401) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0883](ADR-0883-dispatcher-rules-of-the-hand-written-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written MCP server's dispatcher: revisions 2024-11-05 to 2025-11-25 echoed and the latest answered otherwise, no gating before `initialize`, capabilities from the handlers present, invalid messages without an id dropped, each request cancellable on its own task, other exceptions -32603 without detail (BL-3379) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0884](ADR-0884-tools-list-and-tools-call-handlers-of-the-hand-written-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written MCP server's `tools/list` and `tools/call` handlers: registration order on one page with the cursor ignored, `tools` capability from `tools/list` alone, -32602 for a missing name, unknown tool or non-object arguments, a throwing tool answered as the tool error `An error occurred invoking '<name>'.` (BL-3380) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0885](ADR-0885-resource-handlers-of-the-hand-written-mcp-server.md) | The hand-written MCP server's resource handlers: only `resources/list` contributes the `resources: {}` capability, the cursor is ignored, and params naming no string `uri` read as the empty URI and answer -32002 `Resource not found: ''.` (BL-3381) | Accepted | 2026-10-23 |
| [0886](ADR-0886-the-default-tls-range-is-tls-1-2-to-1-3.md) | With no TLS version option, or only a TLS 1.3 ceiling, `TlsVersionRange.ToSslProtocols` offers TLS 1.2 and TLS 1.3, never the operating system's default, which offered TLS 1.1 and 1.0 on Windows; amends ADR-0138 (BL-3533, CGR-004) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0887](ADR-0887-ftps-data-handshake-waits-for-the-transfer-command.md) | The FTPS data connection's TLS handshake runs after the transfer command is answered in passive mode as in active mode, so servers that start data TLS after `RETR` (vsftpd, proftpd) are not deadlocked (BL-3534, CGR-005) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0888](ADR-0888-telnet-sends-its-upload-from-the-receive-loop.md) | A telnet session sends its `-T` upload from the receive loop, one buffer after each read's replies (or after 100 ms without one), so upload bytes never precede the opening negotiation replies nor land inside `IAC SB NAWS ... IAC SE` (BL-3536, CGR-007) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0889](ADR-0889-connect-timeout-holds-the-ssh-handshake.md) | `--connect-timeout` (300 s by default) holds the SSH identification exchange, key exchange and authentication, which end in exit 28 `Operation timed out after N milliseconds with 0 bytes received`, with a `DISCONNECT` once the server's identification has arrived, as curl 8.22.0 does (BL-3539, CGR-010) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0890](ADR-0890-an-http2-or-http3-header-field-with-cr-lf-or-nul-is-ignored.md) | An HTTP/2 or HTTP/3 response header field whose value holds a CR, LF or NUL is left out of the head without a message, as curl's nghttp2 build does, so no header line can be injected (BL-3540, CGR-011) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0891](ADR-0891-an-ftp-upload-data-connection-is-drained-before-it-closes.md) | An FTP upload's data connection is ended with `close_notify` and FIN and read until the server closes it (at most two seconds) before it is disposed, so Windows does not reset it and lose the upload (BL-3535, CGR-006) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0892](ADR-0892-ldap-starts-tls-or-refuses-under-ssl.md) | LDAP honours `--ssl`/`--ssl-reqd`: the WinLDAP dialect refuses `--ssl-reqd` on `ldap://` with exit 4 `LDAP local: explicit TLS not supported` before sending a byte; the OpenLDAP dialect sends StartTLS and binds inside TLS, failing a refused StartTLS with exit 64 under `--ssl-reqd` (CGR-013, BL-3542) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0893](ADR-0893-write-out-header-values-keep-their-raw-bytes.md) | `-w` writes `%{content_type}`, `%header{…}` and `%{header_json}` as the raw header bytes, carried through the renderer as private-use characters by `WriteOutTemplateRenderer.MarkHeaderBytes`, not re-encoded from Latin-1 to UTF-8 (BL-3550, CGR-021) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0894](ADR-0894-a-redirect-target-names-its-scheme-without-slashes.md) | `RedirectFollower` reads scheme-like text and a colon at the start of a redirect target as its scheme, with or without `//`, and refuses a scheme curl does not parse with exit 1 before parsing, so `Location: localhost:20200/x` is not followed as http (BL-3547, CGR-018) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0895](ADR-0895-host-names-are-mapped-as-uts-46-before-punycode-by-hand.md) | A host name that is not ASCII is mapped as UTS #46 (non-transitional) does by `CurlUrlHostNameMapping` before punycode - ignored characters dropped, lower-cased, full-width ASCII and ligatures to ASCII, combining marks composed - because invariant globalization maps nothing; the rest of the table is BL-3829 (BL-3548) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0896](ADR-0896-schannel-ciphers-are-alg-ids.md) | The Schannel build reads `--ciphers` as ALG_IDs (`CALG_*` names, decimal values, `USE_STRONG_CRYPTO`) through `SchannelAlgorithmList`: an entry naming none is exit 59, a list Schannel accepts none of is exit 35 `SEC_E_ALGORITHM_MISMATCH`, and a list stops the range at TLS 1.2; amends ADR-0011 (BL-3551, CGR-022) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0897](ADR-0897-header-file-bytes-reach-the-wire-unchanged.md) | Header file bytes (`-H @file`, `--proxy-header @file`, `-F ...;headers=@file`) and a `-K` file's `cookie` and `request` values reach the wire unchanged: bytes UTF-8 cannot read are held as lone surrogates U+DC80-U+DCFF and written back as themselves (BL-3549, CGR-020) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0898](ADR-0898-an-unfinished-header-line-at-close-shows-the-header-before-it-whole.md) | A peer that closes in the middle of a header line has shown the header before it whole, so curl acts on it (a Content-Length then fails with exit 18), and a lone unfinished `\r` is written with the head (CGR-027, BL-3556) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0899](ADR-0899-a-zero-size-file-source-is-read-until-its-end.md) | A `file://` source whose open reports size zero (procfs, FIFO, `/dev/stdin` pipe, `/dev/zero`) is read until its end, or a positive `-r` byte count, as curl does (`FileProtocolHandler.CountOfUnknownSize`, CGR-028, BL-3557) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0900](ADR-0900-a-refused-http2-stream-is-retried-on-a-new-connection.md) | An HTTP/2 stream the server resets with REFUSED_STREAM, or leaves unprocessed by a GOAWAY whose last stream ID is below it, is exit 56 marked `IsStreamRefused` and retried on a new connection up to five times, as for HTTP/3 (BL-3560, CGR-031) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0901](ADR-0901-multi-driven-callbacks-run-inside-perform.md) | The callbacks of a transfer a multi handle drives are queued to `MultiCallbackQueue` and run by the next `curl_multi_perform` or `curl_multi_socket_action` on its caller's thread, as libcurl's single-thread callback contract says (BL-3562, CGR-033) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0902](ADR-0902-a-failed-write-to-a-side-file-is-handled-as-curl-handles-it.md) | A failed write or close of a `--stderr`, `--trace` or `%output{}` file is ignored, a `-D` file's close is ignored after its `Failed writing headers` exit 23, and a `-c` jar whose close fails is not written, as curl 8.18.0 does (CGR-026, BL-3838) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0902](ADR-0902-a-failed-write-to-a-side-file-is-handled-as-curl-handles-it.md) | A failed write or close of a `--stderr`, `--trace` or `%output{}` file is ignored, a `-D` file's close is ignored after its `Failed writing headers` exit 23, and a `-c` jar whose close fails is not written, as curl 8.18.0 does (CGR-026, BL-3830) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0903](ADR-0903-a-connect-asks-its-socket-opener-before-each-tcp-attempt.md) | A connect asks `ConnectTarget.SocketOpener` (`ITcpSocketOpener`) before each TCP attempt to proceed, refuse (exit 7, no connection) or use an application-supplied socket handle, and tells it when a failed attempt's socket closed, through `SocketOpenerTcpDialer` (BL-3833, CGR-036) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0904](ADR-0904-doh-requests-go-through-the-proxy-environment-variables.md) | DoH requests take the proxy the environment variables name for the DoH URL (never `-x`): an `http://` DoH URL is POSTed to an HTTP proxy in absolute form with `Proxy-Connection: Keep-Alive`, an `https://` one tunnelled with a CONNECT carrying no `User-Agent`; measured on curl 8.22.0 Schannel (CGR-037) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0905](ADR-0905-ascii-ftp-downloads-write-lf-off-windows.md) | An ASCII FTP download (listing, `-B`, `;type=a`) writes each CRLF and lone CR as LF off Windows, as curl's `ftp_cw_lc_write` does under `CURL_PREFER_LF_LINEENDS` (`FtpDownloadLineEndings`, CGR-024, BL-3553) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0906](ADR-0906-an-http2-stream-reset-with-http-1-1-required-is-resent-over-http-1-1.md) | An HTTP/2 stream the server resets with HTTP_1_1_REQUIRED is sent again once over HTTP/1.1 on a new connection offering only `http/1.1` by ALPN, as curl 8.18.0 with nghttp2 does (BL-3561, CGR-032) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0907](ADR-0907-gopher-dict-pop3-mqtt-send-command-line-text-in-the-ansi-code-page-on-windows.md) | Gopher selectors, dict paths, POP3 `-X` commands and MQTT topics are sent in the system ANSI code page on Windows and UTF-8 elsewhere, through a per-library `<Protocol>CommandLineText` like SMTP's (BL-3842, CGR-015) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0908](ADR-0908-windows-resolver-refuses-ipv4-shorthand.md) | On Windows the system resolver resolves no IPv4 address in shorthand (`127.1`, `0`, `2130706433`), so `--connect-to` to one is exit 6 and `--interface` one exit 45 as in the Schannel build; proxy URL hosts are normalised to a dotted quad (BL-3575, CGR-046) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0909](ADR-0909-linux-and-macos-read-argv-raw-bytes-with-surrogate-escapes.md) | On Linux and macOS Curl reads argv's raw bytes (`/proc/self/cmdline`, `_NSGetArgv`) and keeps each invalid UTF-8 byte as the lone surrogate U+DC00 + byte (`InvalidUtf8ByteEscape`), so `-d`, `--data-urlencode` and `--url-query` send it unchanged as curl 8.18.0 does (BL-3868, CGR-016) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0910](ADR-0910-url-and-netrc-credentials-are-sent-as-their-bytes.md) | A URL's percent-decoded user name and password and a netrc file's credentials are sent as their bytes, not re-encoded to the Windows ANSI code page (CGR-052, BL-3581) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0911](ADR-0911-origin-form-path-escapes-are-sent-in-upper-case.md) | The origin-form path's `%XX` escapes go out with upper-case hex digits (request line, HTTP/2 and HTTP/3 `:path`, WebSocket upgrade, authentication target, SigV4 path); the query, a forward proxy's absolute form and `%{url_effective}` keep them as typed (CGR-054) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0912](ADR-0912-j-reads-the-content-disposition-name-in-the-platform-file-name-encoding.md) | `-J` reads the `Content-Disposition` file name in the system ANSI code page on Windows and UTF-8 elsewhere (`CredentialEncoding.ForPlatform`), as the Schannel build opens it; raw non-UTF-8 bytes off Windows are BL-3868's (BL-3580, CGR-051) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0913](ADR-0913-very-large-timeouts-wait-the-longest-timer-wait.md) | A `-m`, `--connect-timeout`, `--keepalive-time` or `--speed-time` past what a .NET timer or `TimeSpan` holds, which curl takes on Linux and macOS, waits the longest .NET can instead of failing with exit 2 (BL-3568, CGR-039) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0914](ADR-0914-socket-action-transfers-wait-for-readiness-beneath-tls.md) | A transfer a multi handle drives by socket actions reads and writes its dialed TCP connection only after the multi handle signals the socket ready (`TransferSocketWait`, `SocketActionGatedConnection`, beneath TLS so buffered records never wait), and publishes `OUT`/`IN` and its earliest deadline (BL-3847, CGR-034) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0915](ADR-0915-url-glob-takes-an-ipv6-zone-id-as-curls-url-parser-does.md) | The URL globber takes `[addr%zone]` as an IPv6 literal when curl 8.22.0's URL parser would: an optional `25`, else not two hex digits, then 1 to 15 characters with no space, control, `/`, `?`, `#` or `@` (BL-3586, CGR-057) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0916](ADR-0916-off-windows-credentials-keep-bytes-that-are-not-utf-8.md) | Off Windows the credential encoding is UTF-8 that keeps each byte outside a valid sequence as a surrogate escape, so `http://user:pa%FFss@host/` sends the byte FF (CGR-053, BL-3582) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0917](ADR-0917-socket-action-transfers-dial-through-a-gated-dialer.md) | A transfer a multi handle drives by socket actions dials through `SocketActionGatedTcpDialer` (`OUT` while it dials, each connection wrapped in `SocketActionGatedConnection` beneath TLS), carried on `ManagedEasyTransfer.SocketWait` and `ManagedEngineSharedState.SocketWait`; with none nothing changes (BL-3859) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0918](ADR-0918-ech-refused-on-every-reference-build.md) | `--ech` is refused with exit 2 on every platform unless the parse reads as an ECH build (`ActsAsEchBuild`), as every reference build refuses it; supersedes ADR-0520 decision 2 (CGR-042, BL-3571) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0919](ADR-0919-file-url-backslashes-stay-as-typed.md) | A `file` URL's backslashes stay as typed: no conversion to `/`, no separator for dot segments, and `file://\host` is rejected, as curl 8.21.0 and 8.18.0 were measured to do (BL-3589, CGR-060) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0920](ADR-0920-output-file-names-with-invalid-utf-8-bytes-open-under-their-raw-bytes.md) | Off Windows an `-o`, `-O` or `-J` file name holding argument bytes that are not valid UTF-8 opens under those raw bytes through the runtime's `SystemNative_Open` (CGR-016, BL-3853) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0921](ADR-0921-a-url-argument-byte-that-is-not-utf-8-is-sent-as-itself.md) | A URL argument byte that is not valid UTF-8 (off Windows the lone surrogate U+DC00 + byte) is sent as itself: `%FF` in the path, the raw byte in the query, as curl 8.18.0 does on Linux (CGR-016, BL-3851) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0922](ADR-0922-an-unsolicited-101-is-the-final-head.md) | A `101 Switching Protocols` no h2c upgrade asked for is the final head: its body runs to the close whatever its framing headers say, and an empty one fails with exit 52, as curl 8.22.0 does (`HttpResponseHeadReader.AsksH2cUpgrade`, `HttpResponseBodyReader.FramingHeadersOf`; BL-3599, CGR-070) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0923](ADR-0923-redirect-url-carries-an-absolute-locations-header-bytes-marked.md) | An absolute `Location`'s bytes from 0x80 travel in the redirect and effective URL marked as U+F700 + byte (`HeaderByteText`), so `%{redirect_url}` and `%{url_effective}` write them as those bytes as curl 8.22.0 does, while command-line text stays UTF-8 (BL-3867, CGR-062) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0924](ADR-0924-sspi-digest-reads-and-answers-challenges-as-windows-curl-does.md) | Where the SSPI build is matched, Digest reads a challenge as Windows curl's SSPI does (`DigestSspiChallenge`): exit 94 for the forms it refuses, exit 67 for an empty user, the realm from a `DOMAIN\user` name, no `userhash`, the algorithm as `MD5`, `charset=utf-8`, and only the user name and realm escaped (BL-3606, CGR-077) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0925](ADR-0925-sni-is-the-url-host-lowercased-without-a-trailing-dot.md) | The SNI `SslStreamTlsProvider` sends is the URL host lowercased with one trailing dot removed (`ServerNameIndication`), as curl does; the Host header keeps its case (BL-3622, CGR-093) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0926](ADR-0926-certs-is-printed-in-the-layout-of-the-platforms-curl-build.md) | `-w %{certs}` is printed in the layout of the platform's curl build: x509asn1.c's under Schannel, `ossl_certchain`'s under OpenSSL (`OpenSslPeerCertificateText`), as curl 8.18.0 with OpenSSL 3.5.5 does on Linux (CGR-091, BL-3620) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0927](ADR-0927-a-connect-reply-fails-invalid-response-header-only-for-a-later-line-without-a-colon.md) | A CONNECT reply fails with exit 43 `Invalid response header` only when a line after the first ends holding no colon and not starting with a space or tab; a first line that is not `HTTP/1.x` is status 0, exit 7, as curl 8.22.0 does (`HttpProxyTunnel`; BL-3610, CGR-081) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0928](ADR-0928-sasl-login-sends-an-empty-password-as-an-empty-line.md) | SASL LOGIN sends an empty password as an empty line, not `=`, as curl 8.22.0 does over SMTP, POP3 and IMAP (`ScriptedSaslExchange.SendsLastAnswerAsEmptyLine`; BL-3632, CGR-103) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0933](ADR-0933-z-stops-waiting-for-multiplexing-once-a-key-showed-http-1.md) | Under `-Z` a transfer no longer waits for a connection being opened to a pool key once a connection to that key held an HTTP/1 session, so a host's waiting transfers open side by side as curl 8.22.0 opens them (CGR-171, BL-3700) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0934](ADR-0934-variable-content-and-expanded-values-carry-the-argument-encoding.md) | `--variable` `=content` and imports are held in the argument encoding (the ANSI code page on Windows), and an `--expand-` value's bytes are read back as typed text, so typed and file bytes go out unchanged as curl's do; supersedes ADR-0064's U+FFFD clause (CGR-015, BL-3848) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0935](ADR-0935-max-total-connections-closes-the-oldest-idle-connection-to-make-room.md) | Before a multi transfer starts under `CURLMOPT_MAX_TOTAL_CONNECTIONS`, the multi pool closes its oldest idle connections until the new one fits, unless one to the same host and port waits to be reused, as libcurl 8.22.0's `Curl_cpool_check_limits` (CGR-150, BL-3679) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0936](ADR-0936-record-curlexchange-sh-serves-tftp-with-a-perl-responder.md) | `Record-CurlExchange.sh -Tftp` is served by a perl responder (core modules), because bash cannot answer a datagram from a new UDP port (BL-3938). |
| [0937](ADR-0937-z-frees-a-hosts-waiters-when-any-transfer-ends.md) | Under `-Z` any transfer that ends, at any host or with a refused connect, frees the transfers waiting for another host's first to end, and an HTTP/1.x group's transfer finding a busy plain TCP connection opens its own (`ParallelHostQueue.Leave`, `PoolingConnector.PlainTcpMayMultiplex`; CGR-172, BL-3701) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0938](ADR-0938-socket-action-transfers-hold-the-expect-100-deadline.md) | A socket-action transfer's expect-100 wait is fed to its `TransferSocketWait` through `HttpProtocolHandler.ContinueWaitBegins` and `ContinueWaitEnds`, which `EngineComposition.CreateProtocolHandlers` sets from `ManagedEngineSharedState.SocketWait`; with none nothing changes (BL-3864) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0939](ADR-0939-a-final-unsolicited-101-leaves-the-connection-intact.md) | A final unsolicited 101 whose body runs to the close marks the connection reusable and reports it left intact, as curl 8.22.0 does (BL-3870) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0940](ADR-0940-w-at-file-writes-the-files-bytes-unchanged.md) | `-w @file` writes the file's bytes unchanged: invalid UTF-8 kept as `InvalidUtf8ByteEscape` surrogates off Windows, the raw bytes spelled in the ANSI code page on Windows (`CommandLineOptionTable.WriteOutFileTemplate`, CGR-016, BL-3862) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0941](ADR-0941-seclevel-zero-lifts-the-openssl-builds-legacy-ceiling.md) | In the OpenSSL build `--ciphers` ending at `@SECLEVEL=0` lifts the refusal of a `--tls-max 1.0` or `1.1` ceiling (`RefusesItsVersionRange`), as curl 8.18.0 on OpenSSL 3.5.5 connects (BL-3879) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0942](ADR-0942-idn-host-names-are-converted-label-by-label-as-libidn2-does.md) | A transfer host that is not ASCII is converted label by label as libidn2 does (empty labels kept, hyphen rules, no bidi check), a byte that is not UTF-8 read as Windows-1252 on Windows, and a refused name fails with exit 3 `URL using bad/illegal format or missing URL` (BL-3588, CGR-059) | Accepted | 2026-10-26 |
| [0943](ADR-0943-header-files-and-created-directories-with-invalid-utf-8-bytes-use-their-raw-bytes.md) | Off Windows a `-D` file and a `--create-dirs` directory whose name holds argument bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are made under those raw bytes (`SystemNative_Open`, `SystemNative_MkDir`; CGR-016, BL-3868) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0944](ADR-0944-a-staple-whose-issuer-the-server-did-not-send-is-error-computing-ocsp-id.md) | With `--cert-status` and a trust store, a staple whose issuer the server did not send is exit 91 `Error computing OCSP ID`, as curl 8.18.0 on OpenSSL 3.5.5 prints (BL-3917) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0945](ADR-0945-standard-handles-write-at-their-shared-file-offset.md) | A redirected standard output, and off Windows standard error, writes at the handle's shared file offset (`SharedOffsetHandleStream` over `WriteFile` or `SystemNative_Write`), not a `FileStream`'s own position, so `> f 2>&1` keeps curl's order (BL-3937) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0946](ADR-0946-a-failed-o-open-echoes-client-returned-error-as-the-v-line-after-its-warning.md) | A failed `-o` open echoes `client returned ERROR on write of N bytes` as the `-v` line, after its warning (BL-3951) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0947](ADR-0947-sspi-ntlm-without-a-credential-is-login-denied.md) | On the SSPI build, an NTLM Type 1 that SSPI cannot acquire the credential for (an overlong user name) fails exit 67 `Login denied` before sending anything, as curl's `AcquireCredentialsHandle` failure does (BL-3649) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0948](ADR-0948-openssl-build-asks-for-an-encrypted-pem-key-pass-phrase.md) | The OpenSSL build asks `Enter PEM pass phrase:` on stderr for an encrypted PEM key given without `--pass`, once for a line and twice at the end of input, as OpenSSL's default callback does (BL-3624) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0949](ADR-0949-a-closed-standard-input-fails-at-file-reads-and-reads-empty-elsewhere.md) | A standard input closed at start is a `ClosedStandardInputStream`: `@-` data reads fail with exit 26 as curl 8.22.0's `fread` does, while `-K -` and the other readers take it as empty (BL-3972, CGR-110) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0950](ADR-0950-a-request-resent-on-a-fresh-connection-counts-its-digest-nonce-on.md) | A request resent on a fresh connection after its reused one died counts its Digest nonce on (`nc=00000002`, same `cnonce`), as curl 8.22.0 makes the answer again (BL-3874) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0951](ADR-0951-traditional-encrypted-pem-keys-are-decrypted-by-hand.md) | The OpenSSL build decrypts a traditional encrypted PEM key (`Proc-Type: 4,ENCRYPTED`, `DEK-Info` AES-128/192/256-CBC or DES-EDE3-CBC) by hand with `EVP_BytesToKey` (BL-3980) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0952](ADR-0952-a-config-file-url-host-is-sent-as-the-file-bytes.md) | On Windows, a `-K` file URL's host written raw is re-spelled with its user information so it goes out as the file's UTF-8 bytes read as Windows-1252 (`ü` gives `xn--14-ria7423a`), as curl 8.22.0 sends it (BL-3956, CGR-059) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0953](ADR-0953-a-location-host-is-read-as-windows-1252-on-windows-so-test1448-stays-off-the-ratchet.md) | A `Location` host takes the platform's reading (Windows-1252 on Windows), so upstream test1448's UTF-8 redirect fails exit 6 on Windows as curl 8.22.0 does and stays off the ratchet (BL-3964, BL-3965: also the whole-host reading, Windows-1252 on Windows and the UTF-8 locale on Linux and macOS) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0954](ADR-0954-a-parallel-runs-first-batch-is-set-up-before-any-transfer-starts.md) | A `-Z` run sets up its first `--parallel-max` plus one transfers before any starts, and a setup failure among them abandons the batch, each reported with the failure's code; past it a bad glob is taken as written and the run carries on, and a setup failure's code is the run's, as curl 8.22.0 (CGR-170, BL-3699, amends 0127) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0955](ADR-0955-a-socket-action-transfer-with-no-os-socket-gets-an-engine-socket-number.md) | A socket-action transfer whose connection has no operating system socket gets an engine socket number from `0x50000001` up, and an action naming it signals its wait (BL-3981) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0956](ADR-0956-openssl-build-always-gives-sslstream-a-certificate-selection-callback.md) | The OpenSSL build always gives `SslStream` a certificate selection callback, choosing none without `--cert`, so its TLS 1.3 hello offers `post_handshake_auth` and a post-handshake CertificateRequest gets an empty Certificate, as curl's does (BL-3698) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0957](ADR-0957-no-platform-sends-an-https-rr-doh-query-or-lists-httpsrr.md) | No platform sends an HTTPS-RR DoH query or lists `HTTPSRR`: the OpenSSL reference build has none either (CGR-157 Linux half, BL-3914, amends 0856) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0958](ADR-0958-a-form-reading-standard-input-is-sent-once-per-option-group.md) | A `-F` form reading standard input is sent once per option group: a later serial transfer fails with exit 26 `client mime read EOF fail`, and under `-Z` only the group's last URL sends the form, the others an empty body, as curl 8.22.0 (CGR-175, BL-3704) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0959](ADR-0959-closing-an-sslstream-connection-sends-close-notify.md) | Closing an `SslStream` connection sends `close_notify` first, unless TLS has already ended or the server has closed the transport, as curl 8.18.0's OpenSSL build and curl 8.22.0's Schannel build do (BL-3984) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0960](ADR-0960-off-windows-a-host-of-one-byte-characters-that-are-utf-8-is-read-as-that-utf-8.md) | Off Windows, a host whose characters are all one byte and spell valid UTF-8 (a `Location` header's bytes) is read as that UTF-8, so upstream test1448's redirect reaches `xn--4cab6c.se` as curl does (BL-3983) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0961](ADR-0961-a-doh-url-with-no-scheme-is-https.md) | A `--doh-url` with no scheme is `https://`, as curl 8.22.0 sends it a TLS ClientHello (CGR-038, BL-3567, amends 0859) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0962](ADR-0962-a-t-directory-off-windows-uploads-an-empty-stream-of-unknown-size.md) | A `-T` directory off Windows uploads an empty stream of unknown size, sent chunked with only the last chunk over HTTP, and exits 0, as Linux curl does; the Windows build still refuses it with `cannot open` (CGR-069, BL-3895) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0963](ADR-0963-socks4-name-with-no-ipv4-address-is-unresolved.md) | A SOCKS4 host name that resolves to no IPv4 address is exit 6 `Could not resolve host`; only an IPv6 literal is exit 97 "not supported" (CGR-210, BL-3739) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0964](ADR-0964-windows-create-dirs-refuses-a-directory-path-of-248-characters.md) | `--create-dirs` on Windows refuses a directory whose full path is 248 characters or more as `ENOENT`, as curl's `mkdir` does (CGR-188, BL-3717) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0965](ADR-0965-a-tls-alert-after-the-handshake-fails-the-read-with-the-alert-text.md) | A fatal TLS alert after the handshake fails the read (exit 56) with the build's alert text: Schannel `SEC_E_ILLEGAL_MESSAGE`, OpenSSL `certificate required` / `unknown ca` (CGR-223, BL-3752) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0966](ADR-0966-each-library-easy-and-multi-handle-numbers-its-transfers-and-connections-from-0.md) | Each library easy and multi handle numbers its transfers and connections from 0, as a new multi does (CGR-247, BL-3776) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0967](ADR-0967-form-part-from-a-closed-standard-input-aborts-with-exit-42.md) | A `-F` part read from a closed standard input sends the part head chunked and aborts with exit 42 `operation aborted by callback`; `ClosedStandardInputStream` moves to `Curl.Core` (CGR-110, BL-3985) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0968](ADR-0968-tftp-under-a-family-choice-filters-an-ip-literal-as-tcp-does.md) | TFTP under `-4` or `-6` filters an IP address literal off Windows and fails with exit 7, as the TCP connect does; `UdpDatagramConnector.FiltersAddressLiterals` (CGR-186, BL-3994) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0969](ADR-0969-http2-callback-failures-and-goaway-use-nghttp2-wording.md) | HTTP/2 flow-control and SETTINGS errors read `nghttp2 recv error -902`, and a GOAWAY mid-response reads `Recv failure: Connection reset by peer`, as curl with nghttp2 does (CGR-272, BL-3801) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0970](ADR-0970-resolve-cache-line-and-ipv6-host-follow-curl-8-22-wording.md) | `-v` writes `[DNS] added H:P:A to cache` and brackets an IPv6 literal host in `Established connection` (CGR-221, BL-3750) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0971](ADR-0971-the-tls-handshake-message-observer-sits-on-the-connections.md) | The hand-built TLS client reports each handshake message and `change_cipher_spec` to a `TlsHandshakeMessageObserver` from its connections, received messages framed by the handshake classes (BL-4014) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0972](ADR-0972-schannel-build-holds-the-final-handshake-flight-until-the-certificate-is-judged.md) | The Schannel build holds the client's final handshake flight until the server certificate is judged (CGR-228, BL-3757) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0973](ADR-0973-socket-action-transfers-report-their-real-socket-and-deadline.md) | A socket-action transfer gives the socket callback its real socket with `CURL_POLL_OUT` then `CURL_POLL_IN`, and the timer its earliest deadline, -1 when none; each action lets it run to its next wait, and its wait stops gating the connection once the transfer finishes or leaves the handle (`ManagedMultiTransfer`, `TransferSocketWait.StopGating`; BL-4016, CGR-034) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0974](ADR-0974-a-warning-reaches-the-debug-callback-as-an-info-line.md) | Every Curl.Engine wrapper forwards `ITransferEvents.ReportWarning`, and `DebugCallbackTransferEvents` hands a warning to the debug callback as one `CURLINFO_TEXT` line without the `Warning: ` prefix (BL-3996) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0975](ADR-0975-the-tool-carries-on-past-not-built-in-for-the-ca-path-and-tls13-cipher-setopts.md) | The curl tool carries on past `CURLE_NOT_BUILT_IN` for `CURLOPT_CAPATH`, `CURLOPT_PROXY_CAPATH`, `CURLOPT_TLS13_CIPHERS` and `CURLOPT_PROXY_TLS13_CIPHERS`; their warnings stay the Schannel build's lines before each transfer (BL-4029) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0976](ADR-0976-smtp-response-read-failure-always-reports-errno-0.md) | An SMTP response-read failure always reports `errno: 0`; the Linux `errno: 115` of curl 8.18 is a stale errno left by the connect (BL-3764, CGR-235) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0977](ADR-0977-j-names-keep-invalid-utf-8-bytes-raw-off-windows.md) | Off Windows a `-J` name's bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are kept as their raw bytes, so the file opens under them as curl 8.18.0 on Linux does (BL-3858) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0978](ADR-0978-winldap-logon-bind-opens-an-empty-connection-before-its-own.md) | Without `-u`, the WinLDAP dialect opens curl's own connection, which carries no bytes, before the connection WinLDAP binds and searches on; with `-u` and in the OpenLDAP dialect there is one connection (`LdapProtocolHandler`; BL-3999, CGR-234) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0979](ADR-0979-a-connect-target-names-its-tls-server-name-and-system-store-trust.md) | A `ConnectTarget` names its TLS server name (SNI and certificate name) and can ask for a handshake trusting only the system store (BL-4034, for CGR-289) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0980](ADR-0980-host-names-are-mapped-with-the-whole-uts-46-table-and-nfc.md) | A host name that is not ASCII is mapped with the whole UTS #46 table (Unicode 15.1) and put in NFC by hand from generated compact tables in `CurlUrlHostNameMappingTable`, as libidn2 2.3.8 does for curl 8.22.0 (BL-3829) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0979](ADR-0979-a-connect-target-names-its-tls-server-name-and-system-store-trust.md) | A `ConnectTarget` names its TLS server name (SNI and certificate name) and can ask for a handshake trusting only the system store (BL-4026, for CGR-289) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0981](ADR-0981-off-windows-output-text-writes-escaped-bytes-raw.md) | Off Windows, `-w` text and the runner's standard error lines write each escaped byte raw, so `%{filename_effective}` and the `File exists` warning print it as curl 8.18.0 on Linux does (BL-4032) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0982](ADR-0982-waitfds-names-each-transfers-real-socket.md) | `curl_multi_waitfds` names each running transfer's real socket, with or without a socket callback: a handle with none gives the transfer a `TransferSocketWait` whose gating is stopped from the start, only to record its socket (`ManagedMultiTransfer`; BL-4037, CGR-034) | Accepted; its no-dialled-socket point replaced in part by ADR-0997 | 2026-10-28 |
| [0983](ADR-0983-the-hand-built-openssl-build-reports-its-handshake-messages.md) | The hand-built client's OpenSSL build reports every handshake message, the negotiated group and the peer signature type (BL-4009) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0984](ADR-0984-a-socket-action-lookup-waits-on-a-loopback-wakeup-socket.md) | A socket-action transfer's look-up waits readable on a loopback wakeup socket (`LoopbackLookupWakeup`) told with `CURL_POLL_IN` and signalled when the lookup ends (`SocketActionGatedResolver`, `TransferSocketWait.BeginResolve`; BL-4036, CGR-034) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0985](ADR-0985-the-openssl-builds-traced-handshakes-run-on-the-hand-built-client.md) | In the OpenSSL build a connection whose handshake messages are traced (`-v`, `--trace`) runs on the hand-built client, which reports them; `SslStream` shows none (`TlsClientRouting`, BL-4010, CGR-226) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0986](ADR-0986-wrapped-transfers-run-on-a-socket-wait-and-computing-ones-ask-for-timer-0.md) | A wrapped handle runs on its `IEasyTransfer.PerformingTransfer`'s socket wait, and a socket-wait transfer still computing after the settle limit asks the timer callback for 0 (`ManagedMultiTransfer`; BL-4038, CGR-034) | Accepted; its fake-transfer point replaced by ADR-0997 and ADR-0998 | 2026-10-28 |
| [0987](ADR-0987-schannel-hand-built-sub-tls12-alert-text.md) | The Schannel build prints SEC_E_ILLEGAL_MESSAGE for an alert received under `--tlsv1.0 --tls-max 1.1` and SEC_E_UNSUPPORTED_FUNCTION under any other TLS 1.0 or 1.1 ceiling; a closed transport stays `failed to receive handshake` (BL-4022, CGR-224) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0988](ADR-0988-winldap-ntlm-bind-over-ldaps-asks-for-no-protection.md) | WinLDAP's bind as the logged-on user asks for no signing or sealing over `ldaps`, starts no security layer there, and a close during that bind is `Unavailable` (BL-4046) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0989](ADR-0989-openssl-build-group-and-signature-names-from-handshake-records.md) | The OpenSSL build names the TLS group and peer signature type of `-v`'s `SSL connection using` line from the plaintext handshake records `ConnectionStream` shows a `ServerHandshakeRecordReader`; the Schannel build names neither (BL-4041, CGR-226) | Accepted | 2026-10-29 |
| [0990](ADR-0990-a-refused-connect-tunnel-is-past-connect-for-the-progress-meter.md) | A CONNECT the proxy refused is past connect for the progress meter, so a failed transfer draws the header and zero row (BL-3771) | Accepted |
| [0991](ADR-0991-schannel-build-refuses-tls13-ciphers-and-proxy-capath.md) | The Schannel build answers `CURLE_NOT_BUILT_IN` for `CURLOPT_TLS13_CIPHERS`, `PROXY_TLS13_CIPHERS` and `PROXY_CAPATH`; the OpenSSL build takes them (BL-4011, CGR-265) | Accepted | 2026-10-29 |
| [0992](ADR-0992-winldap-ldaps-connection-honours-insecure.md) | WinLDAP's own `ldaps://` connection trusts only the system store but honours `-k`, as curl 8.22.0 does (measured; `ISystemStoreTrustTlsProvider.SystemStoreOnly`; BL-4044, CGR-289; amends ADR-0979) | Accepted | 2026-10-28 |
| [0993](ADR-0993-libcurl-easy-handle-draws-the-built-in-progress-meter.md) | A Libcurl easy handle draws the built-in progress meter under CURLOPT_NOPROGRESS 0 (CGR-282, BL-4012) | Accepted |
| [0996](ADR-0996-schannel-close-notify-lines-ride-the-tls-message-event.md) | Schannel's close_notify, shutdown and store-cache `-v` lines ride the existing TLS message event (BL-4067, CGR-225) | Accepted | 2026-10-29 |
